We formulate background estimation as a per-beam temporal modeling problem and investigate complementary statistical strategies that capture dominant as well as multi-modal background structures, combined with spatial filtering in the angular and 3D domain. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce HighwayScene, a new multi-LiDAR dataset recorded in a static roadside setup, and extend the public CoopScenes dataset with static/dynamic point-wise annotations. Across multiple scenes and heterogeneous sensing technologies, we demonstrate that beam-wise statistical modeling provides a robust and transferable solution. Combining lightweight per-beam models with spatial consistency filtering substantially improves precision while maintaining high recall and real-time capability. All datasets, annotations, and implementations are publicly released.

</details>


### [34] [SpIn-ViT: Designing a Sparsity-Induced Vision Transformer That Is Mechanistically Interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14922)
*Philip H. Lee,Parth Padalkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpIn-ViT是一个联合训练预训练ViT和修改版稀疏自编码器（SAE）的框架，将稀疏patch级表示直接与图像分类目标对齐，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时学习语义连贯的神经元激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验SAE方法在ViT优化完成后才在冻结表示上训练，导致其潜在特征与下游分类目标不一致，解释性特征与分类任务脱节。

Method: SpIn-ViT端到端联合训练预训练ViT和修改版SAE，将稀疏patch级表示直接与图像分类目标对齐，并可从SAE神经元中提取可解释的规则集构建神经符号模型。

Result: 在9个图像分类基准上，相比SOTA后验SAE方法：平均分类准确率提高8.84%，AI可解释性得分提升近4倍，人类评估得分提升超2倍；神经符号模型平均准确率提高5.97%，规则集缩小58.8%。

Conclusion: SpIn-ViT证明将SAE与分类目标联合训练能同时提升可解释性和预测性能，且能生成更简洁有效的神经符号规则集，为ViT的机制可解释性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has recently expanded to Vision Transformers (ViTs), with Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) increasingly used as post-hoc tools to decompose internal representations into sparse and more interpretable features. However, because post-hoc SAEs are trained on frozen representations after the ViT has already been optimized, their latent features are not directly aligned with the downstream classification objective. We introduce SpIn-ViT, a framework that jointly trains a pretrained ViT and a modified SAE end-to-end, directly aligning sparse patch-level representations with image classification. SpIn-ViT learns semantically coherent neuron activations that localize meaningful image regions while maintaining competitive predictive performance. We evaluate SpIn-ViT across nine image-classification benchmarks using classification accuracy, quantitative interpretability metrics, AI-based and Human evaluations. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art post-hoc SAE method, SpIn-ViT achieves 8.84% higher average classification accuracy, an AI-based interpretability score nearly four times as high, and a human-evaluation score more than twice as high. We further extract interpretable rule-sets using the SAE neurons to create neurosymbolic models which achieve 5.97% higher average classification accuracy while requiring a 58.8\% smaller rule-set than the neurosymbolic models created from the SOTA post-hoc SAE method.

</details>


### [35] [PaSTel: Anchoring Histology in Spatial Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Hierarchical Bio-Prior Contrastive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14924)
*Azim Dehghani Amirabad,Junchao Zhu,Pushpak Pati,Walid Abdelmoula,Tommaso Mansi,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaSTel是一个层次化多模态预训练框架，通过在不同尺度整合生物先验知识（TF-IDF基因重加权、KEGG通路锚点、空间聚类区域建模），解决了空间转录组学中基因选择受管家基因主导和点级对齐忽略空间依赖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组学多模态预训练方法存在两个关键局限：1）空间信息基因选择常被广泛表达的家用基因主导，导致表征判别力弱；2）独立的点-斑块对齐无法捕获对组织结构至关重要的空间依赖性。

Method: PaSTel在三个层面整合生物先验：1）点水平采用TF-IDF重加权筛选空间信息基因；2）功能水平利用KEGG通路作为锚点编码全局生物学语义；3）区域水平通过空间聚类聚合邻近点以建模中尺度组织结构。

Result: 在多个下游任务中，PaSTel一致优于现有的视觉编码器和视觉-组学编码器，证明了多尺度生物先验的引入能产生更具信息性和可迁移性的表征。

Conclusion: PaSTel表明在空间转录组学预训练中融入多尺度生物学先验，能够获得比传统方法更丰富、更具判别力和可迁移性的表征，为ST多模态学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) links tissue morphology with molecular programs, motivating multimodal pretraining methods that align histology images with gene expression. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: spatially informative gene selection is often dominated by ubiquitous housekeeping genes, leading to weakly discriminative representations, and independent spot-patch alignment fails to capture spatial dependencies that are critical for tissue organization. To address these challenges, we introduce PaSTel, a hierarchical multimodal pretraining framework that integrates biological priors at three levels. At the spot level, TF-IDF reweighting is used to identify spatially informative genes; at the functional level, curated KEGG pathways serve as anchors for encoding global biological semantics; and at the regional level, spatial clustering aggregates neighboring spots to model meso-scale tissue structure. Across multiple downstream tasks, PaSTel consistently outperforms existing vision and vision-omics encoders, demonstrating that incorporating multiscale biological priors yields more informative and transferable representations for spatial transcriptomics.

</details>

*Thomas Manzini,Priyankari Perali,Raisa Karnik,Stephen Johnson,Robin R. Murphy*

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了多源遥感影像（无人机、载人飞机、卫星）中的标注者和审核者表现，发现低分辨率影像（卫星）的标注分歧率显著高于高分辨率影像（无人机），并提出基于来源自适应的质量控制和任务分配建议。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发现两个关键发现：（1）初始标注被委员会修订的比例随分辨率下降而急剧上升（载人飞机25.27%，卫星36.95%）；（2）单一审核未解决分歧，委员会仍需修订无人机6.85%、载人飞机14.05%、卫星20.86%的标签。

Conclusion: 统一审核分配方式会在低分辨率影像中留下最多残余分歧，建议采用自适应任务分配和预算感知的质量控制策略来更高效地管理多源数据集标注流程。

Abstract: This paper presents the first known empirical investigation of annotator and reviewer performance across multi-source remotely sensed imagery, evaluating human labeling across drone, crewed aviation, and satellite views. Because existing aerial imagery datasets rely predominantly on single-source imagery, there is no currently established state of practice for efficiently allocating human labor to curate large-scale, multi-source aerial datasets. This work addresses this limitation by analyzing annotator and reviewer performance within a post-disaster building damage assessment dataset of 9 disasters, where 20041 buildings in drone, 20695 buildings in crewed aviation, and 33392 buildings in satellite imagery were labeled. These labels, provided by 187 annotators, were then refined through two successive quality-control stages: a single-reviewer pass followed by a consensus-committee review. Our analysis reveals two findings that raise questions for standard crowd-sourcing practices. First, initial annotations were revised by the final committee at rates that rise steeply from higher- to lower-resolution sources (25.27% for crewed aviation and 36.95% for satellite), with the same ordering at every observed workflow stage. Second, a single individual review reduced but did not resolve this disagreement: after review, the committee still revised 6.85% of drone, 14.05% of crewed, and 20.86% of satellite labels. These observations suggest that, in workflows like this one, uniform review allocation leaves the most residual disagreement in lower-resolution imagery. Based on this evidence, and consistent with prior work on adaptive task assignment and budget-aware quality control, this paper offers three recommendations for multi-source dataset curation.

</details>

*Sajjad Abdoli,Ghassan Al-Sumaidaee,Ahmed Rashad*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型评测多基于平均难度提示词，无法充分展现系统在高组合性要求（精确对象计数、多对象属性绑定、嵌入文本可读性、明确空间约束）上的性能差距。作者旨在通过最难的提示词子集对那些具有挑战性的组合性任务进行严格评测，揭示生产系统之间的真实差距。

Method: 从DataSeeds.AI样本数据集（DSD）全语料库中通过自动化复杂度评分筛选出48个最难提示词。采用三阶段评测流水线：首先使用GPT-5.4-Pro生成原子化、加权、MECE（互斥且完全穷尽）的评分标准；然后由Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview独立判定每个标准是否满足；最后对四个生产系统（Hunyuan 3.0、Gemini 3 Pro Image、FLUX.2、Ideogram 3.0）生成图像进行独立评委评分，并进行失败模式分析。

Result: Gemini 3 Pro Image以84.8/100排名第一，略领先于FLUX.2的82.3/100；Ideogram 3.0和Hunyuan 3.0分别为65.7/100和63.3/100。失败分析显示领先系统（Gemini 3 Pro Image、FLUX.2）主要因对象计数错误和几何伪影失分，而落后系统（Ideogram 3.0、Hunyuan 3.0）更多出现文字乱码，其中Ideogram 3.0还频繁遗漏所要求的元素。

Conclusion: 在最难的组合性文生图任务中，Gemini 3 Pro Image表现最佳但优势并不悬殊，FLUX.2紧随其后；两个领先系统与两个落后系统之间存在明显的性能断层。对象计数和几何处理仍是所有系统的共性短板，而文本渲染是区分系统层次的关键能力。完整逐样本评分标准、得分与失败标注可从作者处获取。

Abstract: Text-to-image models are typically reported on average-case prompts, which understates the gap between systems on compositionally demanding requests involving precise object counts, multi-object attribute binding, legible embedded text, and explicit spatial constraints. We evaluate four production text-to-image systems: Hunyuan 3.0, Gemini 3 Pro Image ("Nano Banana Pro"), Black Forest Labs FLUX.2, and Ideogram 3.0. The evaluation uses the 48 hardest prompts drawn from the DataSeeds.AI Sample Dataset (DSD), selected through an automated complexity-scoring pass over the full corpus. Every generated image is graded using an independent-judge rubric. GPT-5.4-Pro authors an atomic, weighted, mutually exclusive and collectively exhaustive (MECE) evaluation rubric, while Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview independently determines whether each criterion is satisfied. Gemini 3 Pro Image ranks first with a score of 84.8/100, narrowly ahead of FLUX.2 at 82.3/100. Ideogram 3.0 and Hunyuan 3.0 score 65.7/100 and 63.3/100, respectively. Failure analysis shows that the leading systems primarily lose points through object miscounting and geometric artifacts, whereas the trailing systems more frequently produce garbled text. Ideogram 3.0 also frequently omits requested elements. Full per-sample rubrics, scores, and failure annotations are available from the authors upon request.

</details>

*Meng Ma,Shuyang Li,Naigang Wang,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种风险自适应的边缘-云架构，通过车载交通评估决定何时请求云端VLM推理，在保持任务成功率的同时将云请求减少54.1%，并减少AEB触发次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端视觉语言模型（VLM）比车载小模型具有更强的上下文推理能力，但频繁的视觉上传会增加通信开销，并给战术决策引入网络和推理延迟，影响自动驾驶的实时性。

Method: 采用风险自适应的边缘-云架构：车载VLM与轻量级检测器捕捉时序交通状况和路径相关危险，用于保守的本地响应和有选择性的云端访问；云端模型提供战术建议，而验证、车辆控制和自动紧急制动保持本地执行。

Result: 在CARLA实验中，该方法匹配了周期性云端访问的任务成功率，同时将云请求减少54.1%并记录更少的AEB触发。在延迟道路施工消融实验中，语义事件在预定的下一次审计之前触发了请求。在三种模拟网络配置下仍能减少云流量，但变道耗时比周期性访问更长。

Conclusion: 车载交通评估可作为选择性VLM推理的实用触发机制，在保持安全性和任务成功率的同时显著减少云端通信开销。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted vision-language models (VLMs) offer greater contextual reasoning capabilities than smaller onboard models, but frequent visual uploads increase communication overhead and add network and inference latency to tactical decisions. We present a risk-adaptive edge-cloud architecture in which onboard traffic assessment determines when cloud reasoning is requested. An onboard VLM and a lightweight detector capture temporal traffic conditions and path-relative hazards for conservative local response and selective cloud access. The cloud model provides tactical advice, while validation, vehicle control, and automatic emergency braking remain local. In CARLA experiments, our method matched the task success rate of periodic cloud access while reducing cloud requests by 54.1% and recording fewer automatic emergency braking (AEB) activations. In a delayed-roadwork ablation, semantic events triggered requests before the next scheduled audit. Across three emulated network profiles, the method continued to reduce cloud traffic, although lane changes took longer than with periodic access. Onboard traffic assessment therefore served as a practical trigger for selective VLM inference in these experiments.

</details>


### [39] [Registration-Free Hyperspectral Reconstruction from RGB via a Permutation-Invariant Gram-Matrix Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14994)
*Jiangsan Zhao,Masayuki Hirafuji,Seishi Ninomiya,Jakob Geipel,Wei Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于排列不变性监督原则的高光谱图像与RGB图像融合方法，无需精确配准和已知相机响应函数（CRF），通过匹配解混丰度图的Gram矩阵实现RGB到HSI的映射学习，在无需配准、CRF或成对监督的条件下达到与传统方法相当的精度，并在假设被违反时保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高光谱图像融合方法通常假设LR-HSI与HR-RGB之间精确配准且CRF已知，但这些假设在跨传感器场景中难以满足。作者希望通过去除这两个假设，使RGB-to-HSI映射能够在更现实的条件下学习。

Method: 提出排列不变性监督原则：解混丰度图的Gram矩阵仅依赖共享的材料组成而不依赖像素排序。通过匹配丰度Gram矩阵，无需空间对应和预定义CRF即可学习RGB到HSI的映射。在此基础上，使用残差光谱超分辨率函数直接将HR-RGB映射到HR-HSI。

Result: 在室内、自然场景和遥感基准测试中，该方法达到与传统依赖配准和CRF假设的方法相当的精度，且在这些假设被违反时保持鲁棒性。在HR-RGB像素完全随机排列下，该方法重构结果不变，而SOTA融合方法崩溃。损失消融实验表明重构精度对具体Gram矩阵匹配差异不太敏感。

Conclusion: 性能主要来源于排列不变性原则本身而非损失调参，证明该方法的核心思想有效且通用。该工作为无配准、无CRF条件下的高光谱图像重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing a spatially and spectrally high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI) from a low-resolution HSI (LR-HSI) and a high-resolution RGB image (HR-RGB) usually assumes precise registration and a known camera response function (CRF). Both assumptions are difficult to satisfy with different sensors. We remove both through a permutation-invariant supervision principle: the Gram matrix of an unmixed abundance map depends on shared material composition but not on pixel ordering. Matching abundance Gram matrices therefore allows RGB-to-HSI mapping to be learned without spatial correspondence and without a predefined CRF. Under a full random permutation of HR-RGB pixels, a state-of-the-art fusion method collapses, whereas our reconstruction is unchanged after inverse reindexing for evaluation. Building on this principle, a residual spectral super-resolution function maps HR-RGB directly to HR-HSI without registration, known CRF, or paired supervision. Across indoor, natural-scene, and remote-sensing benchmarks, the method achieves accuracy comparable to approaches that require these assumptions while remaining robust when they are violated. Loss ablations further show that reconstruction accuracy is largely insensitive to the specific discrepancy used to match the Gram matrices, indicating that performance arises primarily from the permutation-invariant principle rather than loss tuning.

</details>


### [40] [FZ-VLM: A Two Stage Florence-Zephyr Vision Language Model Framework for Pulmonary Nodule Characterization and Clinical Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15004)
*Pramit Dutta,Jenita Manokaran,Richa Mittal,Ryan Appleby,Eranga Ukwatta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FZ-VLM，一个两阶段Florence-Zephyr视觉语言模型框架，用于肺部CT中肺结节的结构化特征描述和临床决策支持。第一阶段使用微调的Florence-2模型从2D轴向CT切片提取放射学属性，第二阶段使用Zephyr-7B模型生成结节描述、随访建议和纵向分析。实验结果显示在解剖位置（77.18%）、边缘特征（67.96%）、衰减类型（79.13%）及直径估计（MAE 2.58mm）上均优于GPT-4基线和人类基线，专家评估显示93.9%准确率和98.6%完整性得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是导致癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一，CT是主要筛查工具。结节检测后，放射科医生需手动评估解剖位置、直径、边缘特征和衰减类型以支持风险评估，但这一工作流程耗时且存在观察者间差异。现有AI方法往往专注于孤立任务，缺乏统一且具有临床意义的解释框架。

Method: 提出FZ-VLM两阶段框架：第一阶段使用微调的Florence-2模型从专家标注的2D轴向CT切片中提取放射学属性；第二阶段使用Zephyr-7B模型利用这些属性生成结节描述、随访建议和纵向分析，实现从图像到临床决策的端到端管线。

Result: 第一阶段模型在解剖位置（77.18%）、边缘特征（67.96%）、衰减类型（79.13%）上达到较高准确率，直径估计MAE为2.58mm，均优于GPT-4基线和人类基线。第二阶段专家评估显示93.9%准确率、98.6%完整性得分、76.1%临床相关性和89.5%综合得分。安全性分析显示大部分输出临床安全，但部分随访建议仍需专家复核。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提出两阶段视觉语言模型框架用于结构化结节特征描述和临床决策支持，展示了VLM在肺结节临床解释中的潜力，同时指出随访建议等输出仍需要专家审查以确保临床安全。

Abstract: Lung cancer remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related mortality worldwide, and Computed Tomography (CT) is a primary imaging tool for screening and followup assessment. After pulmonary nodule detection, radiologists manually assess anatomical location, diameter, margin characteristics, and attenuation type to support risk assessment and clinical decision-making. However, this post-detection workflow is time-consuming and can be affected by inter-observer variability. Existing Artificial Intelligence methods often focus on isolated tasks, limiting their use as a unified, clinically grounded interpretation framework. This study presents FZ-VLM, a two-stage Florence-Zephyr Vision Language Model framework for unified structured pulmonary nodule characterization in lung CT. The framework uses a fine-tuned Florence-2 model to extract radiological attributes from expert-annotated 2D axial CT slices, while a Zephyr-7B model uses these attributes to generate nodule descriptions, follow-up recommendations, and longitudinal analyses. Results showed that the Stage 1 model achieved 77.18\% accuracy for anatomical location, 67.96\% accuracy for margin characteristics, and 79.13\% accuracy for attenuation type, with a Mean Absolute Error of 2.58 mm for diameter estimation, outperforming evaluated GPT-4-based baselines as well as the human baseline. Expert radiologist evaluation of Stage 2 showed 93.9\% accuracy, 98.6\% completeness score, 76.1\% clinical relevance, and an overall score of 89.5\%. Safety analysis showed that most outputs were clinically safe, although some follow-up recommendations still required expert review. To the best of our knowledge, this study presents the first two-stage Vision-Language Model framework for structured nodule characterization and clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [41] [MetaReason: Precise Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning via Editing Meta Information for Solving Geometry Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15006)
*Penghao Yin,Haomin Wang,Qihong Tang,Xiaoye Qu,Hongjie Zhang,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaReason框架，通过结构化元信息解析几何图像、可控编辑合成高保真视觉状态来辅助辅助线构造，结合TutorGeo数据集和SFT+RL训练，在ExamGeo基准上超越开源模型并与闭源模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理平面几何任务时过度依赖纯文本推理，虽然已有方法引入中间视觉状态辅助推理，但受限于几何表示不准确和渲染保真度低，导致输出不可靠。

Method: 1) 将几何图像解析为结构化元信息；2) 利用预定义工具进行可控编辑以合成高保真视觉状态；3) 基于增强视图进行推理。构建TutorGeo数据集（17k图像转元信息样本、60k文本推理轨迹、60k交错多模态推理轨迹），结合监督微调和强化学习训练模型。

Result: MetaReason显著优于现有开源模型，并在性能上与专有闭源模型具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过结构化元信息驱动的多模态推理框架，可以有效提升平面几何任务中辅助线构造的准确性，从而实现更可靠的几何推理。

Abstract: Although visual reasoning is crucial for solving complex geometry tasks, existing vision-language models rely heavily on text-only reasoning. Some recent methods introduce intermediate visual states to facilitate reasoning, but they are often hindered by inaccurate geometric representations and low rendering fidelity, ultimately leading to unreliable outputs. To address these limitations, we propose MetaReason, a framework for multimodal reasoning in plane geometry that leverages structured meta-information to enable accurate auxiliary-line construction. The framework first parses geometric images into meta-information, performs controllable edits with predefined tools to synthesize high-fidelity visual states, and then conducts reasoning based on these augmented views. To support this framework, we construct TutorGeo, a comprehensive dataset containing 17k image-to-meta conversion samples, 60k text-only reasoning traces, and 60k interleaved multimodal reasoning traces. Using this dataset, we combine supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning to develop robust multimodal reasoning capabilities. We also introduce ExamGeo, a benchmark derived from real-world examination problems that enables systematic evaluation across varying difficulty levels. Experimental results demonstrate that MetaReason significantly outperforms existing open-source models and achieves competitive performance against proprietary models.

</details>


### [42] [DualMiT-Net: Local-Global Transformer-Convolutional Fusion for Breast Mass Segmentation in Mammographic Regions of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15019)
*Alibek Kamiluly,Milana Muratova,Yash Patel,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DualMiT-Net，一种双分支网络用于乳腺肿块分割。局部分支使用MiT-B5编码器学习肿块形状、纹理和边界信息，全局分支使用EfficientNet-B5编码器学习周围乳腺组织上下文。在CBIS-DDSM数据集上，该方法取得Dice系数0.9375和IoU 0.8834，优于六个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺肿块分割是计算机辅助乳腺X线摄影的重要步骤，但由于肿块对比度低、形状不规则、边界与周围组织融合等特点，分割难度较大。现有的方法难以同时兼顾肿块局部细节和周围组织上下文信息。

Method: 提出DualMiT-Net双分支网络架构：1) 局部分支采用Mix Transformer (MiT-B5)编码器学习肿块的形状、纹理和边界信息；2) 全局分支采用EfficientNet-B5编码器学习周围乳腺背景上下文；3) 两条分支在深层编码器层级共享特征，并在单一解码器中逐步融合；4) 使用空间门控机制控制解码过程中全局信息的注入量；5) 评估了四种输入表示，最终选择百分位窗口化乳腺X线图像结合Gabor纹理响应作为输入。

Result: 在CBIS-DDSM数据集的肿块子集上，使用患者级划分进行评估。经过三次训练运行，采用指数移动平均权重的DualMiT-Net取得了平均Dice系数0.9375和平均IoU 0.8834，且在相同的训练数据和设置下优于六个标准编码器-解码器基线模型。

Conclusion: 结合局部肿块信息与更广泛的乳腺背景上下文能够提供准确且一致的乳腺肿块分割结果，验证了双分支网络架构在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Breast mass segmentation is an important step in computer-aided mammography, but it remains difficult because masses can have low contrast, irregular shapes, and boundaries that blend with surrounding breast tissue. To address this problem, we present DualMiT-Net, a dual-branch network that uses both a focused view of the mass and a wider view of the surrounding tissue. The local branch uses a Mix Transformer (MiT-B5) encoder to learn mass shape, texture, and boundary information, while the global branch uses an EfficientNet-B5 encoder to learn surrounding breast context. Features from the two branches are shared at the deeper encoder levels and are then progressively fused in a single decoder. A spatial gate controls how much global information is added during decoding. We also evaluated four input representations and selected a percentile-windowed mammogram combined with a Gabor texture response. The model was trained and evaluated on the mass subset of the Curated Breast Imaging Subset of the Digital Database for Screening Mammography (CBIS-DDSM) using a patient-level split. Across three training runs, DualMiT-Net with exponential moving average weights achieved a mean Dice coefficient of 0.9375 and a mean Intersection over Union of 0.8834. It also achieved better Dice and IoU scores than six standard encoder-decoder baselines trained using the same data and training settings. These results show that combining local mass information with wider breast context can provide accurate and consistent breast mass segmentation.

</details>


### [43] [Geometry-Calibrated Closed-Form Shrinkage for SAR Despeckling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15028)
*Xuran Hu,Mingzhe Zhu,Djordje Stanković,Yujie Zhu,Zhenpeng Feng,Yifang Ban,Ljubiša Stanković*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无训练、确定性的SAR图像去斑方法。通过对数-Yeo-Johnson变换、相似块分组和左奇异基上的系数收缩，作者证明了三个此前被视为可调的超参数实际上由构造本身唯一确定：字典正交性使加权Lasso有闭式软阈值解；组宽高比γ=p²/K通过随机矩阵论将正则化常数转化为几何校准修正；奇异投影使系数噪声近似高斯，定位了精确斑点似然失效的临界点。该方法在24项PSNR/SSIM对比中18项排名第一，并在5个传感器的真实SAR数据上达到最优比率图像偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR去斑是一个逆恢复问题，需要在抑制乘性非高斯噪声的同时保留散射结构。现有的非局部稀疏估计器通常依赖多个手工调节的超参数（如字典、正则化权重、收缩尺度），这些参数在不同图像和传感器间需要重新调整，缺乏理论依据且使用不便。作者希望揭示这些"可调"参数实际上由算法构造本身唯一决定，从而得到一个无需训练、无需调参的确定性估计器。

Method: 该方法基于非局部稀疏估计框架：首先对SAR图像应用对数-Yeo-Johnson变换，然后将相似图像块堆叠成组，每组在自身的左奇异基上编码，并对系数进行收缩。核心贡献在于三个理论分析：(1) 字典正交性使得加权Lasso具有精确的逐系数软阈值闭式解，无需迭代内层求解器，两个权重矩阵实为单一阈值场的分子分母；(2) 利用随机矩阵理论，根据组宽高比γ=p²/K将正则化常数转化为几何校准修正，将块大小、组大小和收缩尺度统一为一个解析确定的自由度；(3) 奇异投影使系数噪声在不同look数下均近似高斯，从而确定了精确斑点似然失效的临界点。

Result: 在三个合成基准上与12种已发表方法进行24项PSNR/SSIM对比，该估计器在18项中排名第一；在来自5个传感器的6种真实SAR配置上，达到了比率图像与理论斑点模型的最低平均偏差。该方法完全确定性、无需训练，且仅需一套解析确定的设置即可适用于所有图像和传感器。

Conclusion: 作者通过严格的理论分析表明，非局部稀疏SAR去斑估计器的多个"可调"参数实际上是由算法内在几何和统计结构唯一决定的，从而将启发式调参转化为解析推导。这不仅简化了方法部署，还揭示了精确斑点似然何时停止提供有用信息的理论边界，为SAR去斑提供了坚实的理论框架和实用的确定性算法。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) despeckling is an inverse-recovery problem in which multiplicative non-Gaussian noise must be suppressed without erasing scattering structures. We revisit a nonlocal sparse estimator that applies a log--Yeo--Johnson transformation, stacks similar patches into groups, codes each group on its own left singular basis, and shrinks the resulting coefficients. Three quantities usually treated as tunable are shown to be fixed by this construction. First, the group dictionary is orthonormal, so the weighted Lasso admits an exact coefficient-wise soft-threshold solution: the iterative inner solver is unnecessary, and the two apparent weighting matrices are the numerator and denominator of a single threshold field rather than independent modules. Second, because the dictionary is estimated from the noisy group itself, its retained subspace absorbs speckle in proportion to the group aspect ratio $γ=p^2/K$; a random-matrix argument converts the corresponding regularization constant into a geometry-calibrated correction and collapses patch size, group size, and shrinkage scale into one analytically determined degree of freedom. Third, singular projection makes the coefficient noise nearly Gaussian at every tested look number, which locates the point at which an exact speckle likelihood ceases to be informative. The resulting estimator is deterministic, training-free, and applies one set of analytically determined settings to every image and sensor. It ranks first in 18 of 24 PSNR/SSIM comparisons against twelve published methods on three synthetic benchmarks, and attains the lowest mean deviation of the ratio image from the theoretical speckle model over six real-SAR configurations from five sensors. Code is available \href{https://github.com/Teriri1999/Geometry-Calibrated-Closed-Form-Shrinkage-for-SAR-Despeckling}{here}.

</details>


### [44] [Generation of Synthetic Fingerphotos with GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15029)
*Conor Miller-Lynch,Sandip Purnapatra,Syed Konain Abbas,Lambert Igene,Faraz Hussain,Soumyabrata Dey,Stephanie Schuckers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用StyleGAN2-ADA和StyleGAN3生成合成指印照片（fingerphotos），并通过比较生物特征统计、真实与合成指印之间的匹配分数以及合成指印之间的匹配分数，评估了合成指印的真实性、隐私保护性和多样性，为未来合成指印评估提供了量化对比基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非接触式指纹识别数据量有限，且分享真实指纹存在安全风险，因此需要探索生成合成指印数据的方法，以替代或补充真实数据用于系统开发和评估。

Method: 使用StyleGAN2-ADA和StyleGAN3两种现有图像生成架构生成合成指印照片；通过比较真实与合成指印的生物特征统计、计算真实与合成指印之间的匹配分数，以及不同合成指印之间的匹配分数，来评估真实性、隐私保护和多样性。

Result: 提供了对合成指印照片在真实性、隐私保护性和多样性三个维度的定量评估结果，并公开了评估代码。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来合成指印照片的评估提供了一个定量比较的参考点，表明基于GAN的合成指印生成方法具有一定可行性。

Abstract: Contactless fingerprinting is an emerging approach to biometric authentication that allows users to scan their fingerprints without touching a scanner. Due to the limited amount of contactless fingerprint data available and the security risks associated with sharing real individuals' fingerprints, it is valuable to explore methods of generating synthetic data that can be used in place of - or in conjunction with - real data to develop and evaluate contactless fingerprinting systems. In this paper, we present and evaluate synthetic fingerphotos generated using StyleGAN2-ADA and StyleGAN3, existing image generation architectures. We evaluate the realism, privacy preservation, and variety of the synthetic fingerphotos by comparing their biometric feature statistics to those of real fingerphotos, computing match scores between real and synthetic fingerphotos, and computing match scores between different synthetic fingerphotos. This paper provides a quantitative comparison point for future evaluations of synthetic fingerphotos. The evaluation code is made available at https://github.com/cmillerlynch/fingerphoto-gan.

</details>


### [45] [MOSS-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15045)
*Pengyu Wang,Chenkun Tan,Shaojun Zhou,Qirui Zhou,Yanxin Chen,Xingyang He,Huazheng Zeng,Jijun Cheng,Chenghao Wang,Xiaomeng Qian,Pengfei Wang,Zhan Huang,Shanqing Gao,Wei Huang,Longjun Cao,Wu Ran,Jie Liu,Changtai Zhu,Hongkai Wang,Yixian Tian,Chenghao Liu,Zhen Ye,Xinghao Wang,Botian Jiang,Guoguo Feng,Zhaoye Fei,Ruixiao Li,Mingshu Chen,Yang Gao,Qinyuan Cheng,Shimin Li,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSS-VL是一个将实时交互（边生成边感知）作为一等能力的开源视觉语言模型家族，通过门控交叉注意力实现视觉与语言解码器的协同设计，配合合成交互语料监督和分阶段课程训练（所有实时特定训练集中在最后一个轻量阶段）。在离线基准上与同等规模模型竞争并领先时序推理视频集；在四个流式基准中，三个取得最佳平均成绩（第四个排名第二），尤其在三个测试主动行为的子集上大幅领先（OmnIMMI主动提醒66.0 vs 37.5）。仅有11.3B参数且视觉token在解码序列之外，相比Qwen3-VL-8B将首token延迟优势从2.8倍扩大到5.1倍。所有五个检查点、训练课程和实时推理代码均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型大多专注于离线理解任务，缺乏对实时交互（如流式视频中边看边说）的能力支持。作者希望构建一个将实时交互视为一等公民的模型家族，弥补开源模型在流式交互场景（如主动提醒、实时对话）中的不足，并解决实时感知与生成之间的架构矛盾。

Method: 采用全栈协同设计：(1) 架构上，语言解码器仅通过门控交叉注意力访问视觉信息，使模型在生成文本时能自然感知新到来的视觉帧；(2) 数据上，使用合成的交互语料监督模型决定何时说话、何时保持沉默、何时修正；(3) 训练上，采用分阶段课程，将全部实时特定训练集中在一个轻量的最终阶段，建立在一个强大的离线基础之上。同时将视觉token置于解码序列之外以提升推理效率。

Result: MOSS-VL-Instruct在离线基准上与同等规模模型竞争，并在时序推理视频集上领先。MOSS-VL-Realtime在四个流式基准中三个取得最佳平均成绩；在三个测试主动行为的子集上表现卓越（66.0 vs 37.5）。相比Qwen3-VL-8B，时间到首token优势从2.8倍扩大到5.1倍（随视觉上下文增长）。

Conclusion: MOSS-VL证明了通过精心设计的架构、数据与训练策略，可以将实时交互能力高效集成到开源视觉语言模型中，同时保持离线性能并显著提升流式推理效率。模型全部资源开源，为该方向提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: We present MOSS-VL, an open vision-language model family that treats real-time interaction -- perceiving while it speaks -- as a first-class capability. It is co-designed across the stack: the language decoder attends to vision only through gated cross-attention, so the model can naturally see incoming frames while generating; a synthesized interaction corpus supervises when to speak, when to stay silent, and when to revise; and a staged curriculum concentrates all real-time-specific training in one light final stage over a strong offline foundation. Offline, MOSS-VL-Instruct is competitive at comparable scale and leads temporal-reasoning video sets. Across four streaming benchmarks, MOSS-VL-Realtime posts the best average on three (second on the fourth) among open-source streaming models, sweeping the three subsets that squarely test proactive behavior -- 66.0 vs. 37.5 for the best baseline on OmniMMI Proactive Alerting. With 11.3B parameters but visual tokens outside the decoded sequence, MOSS-VL widens its time-to-first-token advantage over same-backbone Qwen3-VL-8B from 2.8x to 5.1x as visual context grows. We release all five checkpoints, the training curriculum, and the real-time inference code at https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-VL.

</details>


### [46] [Frequency and Edge-Guided Segment Anything Model for Remote Sensing Image Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15054)
*Feng Gao,Zizhe Pan,Haoting Wang,Ruzhuang Hua,Jingchao Cao,Junyu Dong,Qian Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FE-SAM，一种频率和边缘引导的SAM框架用于遥感图像语义分割。通过频率调制适配器（FMA）自适应分解和调制频域特征，并设计EGRefiner整合多尺度边缘增强信息，解决了SAM特征对地物类型适应性不足和边界语义模糊两个问题。在三个基准数据集上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像语义分割需要精细的地物覆盖信息，SAM作为基础视觉模型虽有强大分割能力，但存在两个局限：(1) SAM特征对不同地物类型多样性适应性不足；(2) 物体边界存在语义模糊，阻碍了精确划分。现有基于SAM的方法未能解决这些问题。

Method: 提出FE-SAM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Frequency-Modulated Adapter (FMA)：基于输入数据自适应分解和调制频域特征，选择性增强与不同地物类型对应的有效高、低频分量；(2) EGRefiner：整合从输入图像提取的多尺度边缘增强信息，提升SAM捕获细粒度细节的能力。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FE-SAM的性能优于现有的最先进方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过频域调制和边缘引导两大策略，有效增强了SAM在遥感图像语义分割任务中的适应性和边界精度，验证了该框架的可扩展性和高效性。

Abstract: Remote sensing image semantic segmentation (RSISS) has attracted significant attention due to the growing demand for fine-grained land cover information. The Segment Anything Model (SAM), proposed as a foundation vision model, offers strong segmentation performance and generalization capabilities for RSISS tasks. However, existing SAM-based approaches face two limitations: (1) Insufficient adaptation of SAM's features to the diverse characteristics of land cover types. (2) Semantic ambiguity at object boundaries, which hinders accurate delineation. To address these limitations, we propose Frequency and Edge-guided SAM (FE-SAM), a scalable and efficient framework for RSISS. Specifically, we introduce a Frequency-Modulated Adapter (FMA) that adaptively decomposes and modulates frequency-domain features based on the input data. It selectively enhances informative high- and low-frequency components corresponding to different land cover types. Furthermore, to improve SAM's ability to capture fine-grained details, we design EGRefiner, which integrates multi-scale edge-enhanced information extracted from the input image. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that FE-SAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The source codes are available at: https://github.com/oucailab/FE-SAM.

</details>


### [47] [MEDR: Query-Independent Frame Selection via Multi-Signal Event Modeling and Dynamic Rescoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15058)
*Xinlei Pu,Weijie Shi,Wen Yang,Yi Cao,Hao Chen,Yuanjun Liu,Wenwei Ding,Jia Zhu,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MEDR，一种无需训练、查询无关的视频帧选择方法，通过多信号事件建模和动态重打分，在不牺牲可复用性的前提下，相比均匀采样提升了对关键事件帧的覆盖，并在Video-MME和LongVideoBench上取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀采样的帧选择方法虽然能保证时间覆盖，但可能遗漏短暂出现的关键信息；而查询相关的帧选择方法虽然能检索到与问题相关的帧，但帧的选择依赖于当前问题，导致在多轮视频对话中无法复用同一视觉输入，帧选择需要反复进行。因此需要一种查询无关且能超越均匀采样覆盖能力的帧选择方法。

Method: MEDR包含两大模块：(1) Multi-Signal Event Modeling，将互补的视觉、运动、文本信号组织为信号特定的事件；(2) Dynamic Rescoring，迭代地重新评估每个候选帧相对于当前已选帧集合的表现，根据帧级信号强度、额外事件覆盖度和时间邻近度动态更新分数。最终生成的固定帧集合在构造时无需看到查询内容，因而可在不同问题间复用。

Result: 在Video-MME标准基准上，MEDR将模型准确率提升了0.63%-0.89%；在LongVideoBench长视频子集上，使用Qwen3-VL-8B时准确率提升最高达1.23%；在整体上准确率提升0.53%，且对同一视频的所有问题复用了完全相同的帧集合。

Conclusion: MEDR证明了查询无关的帧选择方法能够同时兼顾可复用性和信息覆盖率，在无需训练、不依赖查询的条件下即可显著超越均匀采样，为多模态大模型处理长视频提供了一种高效且实用的帧选择方案。

Abstract: Frame selection is a fundamental component of multimodal large language models, enabling long videos to be processed under limited visual-token and computational budgets. Uniform sampling preserves temporal coverage but may miss informative content that appears only briefly. To alleviate this limitation, query-dependent methods can retrieve question-relevant frames. However, because the selected frames depend on the current question, the same visual input cannot be directly shared across different questions, and frame selection must be repeated in multi-turn video dialogue. This motivates us to seek a query-independent frame selection method that preserves the reusability of a fixed visual input while improving the coverage of informative events beyond uniform sampling. We propose Multi-Signal Event Modeling and Dynamic Rescoring (MEDR), a training-free and query-independent frame selection method. Multi-Signal Event Modeling organizes complementary visual, motion, and text signals into signal-specific temporal events. Dynamic Rescoring then iteratively reevaluates each candidate relative to the current selected set, updating its score according to frame-level signal strength, additional event coverage, and temporal proximity. The resulting fixed frame set is constructed without observing the query and can be reused across different questions. On the standard benchmark evaluations, MEDR improves model accuracy by 0.63%-0.89% on Video-MME. On the long-video subset of LongVideoBench, it improves accuracy by up to 1.23% with Qwen3-VL-8B. MEDR further improves overall accuracy by 0.53%, while reusing exactly the same frame set for every question about a video.

</details>


### [48] [EgoTac: In-the-wild Tactile Prediction from Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15060)
*Wenkang Zhang,Chengbo Yuan,Zicheng Zhang,Zhengxue Cheng,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTac是一个从第一人称人类视频中直接预测丰富触觉信息（包括连续力测量和二值接触）的泛化模型，在超过570万图像-触觉对上训练，域内力误差低于0.06N，并在跨域接触预测基准上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对灵巧操作至关重要，但大多用于机器人学习的自我中心人类数据缺乏触觉信息。由于传感器限制，大规模采集触觉数据困难，而人类视频数据丰富、接触密集且易于扩展。因此提出疑问：能否仅从视觉推断触觉信号？

Method: EgoTac是一个可泛化的预测模型，从自我中心人类视频直接预测触觉信息。模型在包含超过570万图像-触觉对的统一语料上训练，涵盖连续力测量和二值接触两类触觉数据，通过从多样化数据中学习以捕捉不同交互中的细微触觉动态。

Result: 实验表明：域内预测平均力误差低于0.06N；在跨域接触预测基准上持续优于最先进的接触估计器；能够捕捉真实触觉数据的上升和下降模式；支持无约束真实世界视频的零样本预测。缩放分析还表明数据多样性和数量都能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: EgoTac提供了一条可扩展的路径，从第一人称人类视频中提取触觉先验，从而支持广泛适用的触觉感知机器人学习。

Abstract: Touch is fundamental to dexterous manipulation, yet most egocentric human data increasingly used for robot learning lacks tactile information. Directly collecting large-scale tactile data is challenging due to sensor limitations, while human video data is abundant, contact-rich, and easily scalable. This motivates a natural question: can tactile signals be inferred purely from vision? To address this, we introduce EgoTac, a generalizable model that predicts rich tactile information directly from egocentric human videos. EgoTac is trained on a unified corpus of over 5.7M image-tactile pairs, covering both continuous force measurements and binary contacts. By learning from this diverse dataset, EgoTac captures nuanced touch dynamics across varied interactions. Experiments demonstrate strong performance: in-domain prediction achieves an average force error below 0.06N. On out-of-domain contact prediction benchmarks, EgoTac consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art contact estimator. It also captures the rise and fall patterns of real tactile data and enables zero-shot predictions on unconstrained real-world videos. Scaling analyses further reveal that both data diversity and volume improve performance steadily. Overall, EgoTac provides a scalable pathway to extract tactile priors from egocentric human videos, enabling broadly applicable tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [49] [Do Visual Grounding Decoders Need Feed-Forward Networks? A Controlled Study over Frozen Vision-Language Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15061)
*Tarun Tomar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉接地解码器中前馈网络（FFN）的必要性，发现基于预训练视觉-语言模型特征时，仅含注意力的四块解码器（A4）在多数基准上与带FFN的四块解码器（S4）性能相当甚至略优，同时减少44.4%的可训练参数和10.1%的缓存延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在预训练视觉-语言模型已编码图像和语言上下文的情况下，视觉接地解码器中的FFN层是否仍然必要，旨在简化解码器架构并提升效率。

Method: 对比三种解码器架构：四块仅注意力解码器（A4）、匹配的四块注意力加FFN解码器（S4）、以及八块仅注意力的参数对照解码器（A8），在冻结的VLM特征上进行实验，涵盖RefCOCOg、Ref-Adv-s和FineCops-Ref数据集。

Result: A4在RefCOCOg和Ref-Adv-s上匹配或略超S4；在FineCops-Ref上A4仅有0.52个百分点的微小差距（95% CI [0.12, 0.95]偏向S4），但A8反超S4 0.26个百分点。A4减少44.4%可训练参数和10.1%缓存延迟，端到端延迟仍受骨干网络主导。

Conclusion: 在冻结VLM特征下，可训练视觉接地解码器中的FFN并非必要，仅注意力架构即可达到相当性能且更高效。结论仅适用于可训练接地解码器，不推广到完全仅注意力的VLM。

Abstract: Do feed-forward networks (FFNs) in visual grounding decoders add essential computation once a pretrained vision-language model has already encoded image and language context? We compare a four-block attention-only decoder (A4), a matched four-block attention-plus-FFN decoder (S4), and an eight-block attention-only parameter control (A8) over frozen VLM features. A4 matches or slightly exceeds S4 on RefCOCOg and Ref-Adv-s. FineCops-Ref reveals a small A4 deficit of 0.52 percentage points at IoU@0.5 (95% CI [0.12, 0.95] in favor of S4), but A8 recovers it and finishes 0.26 points above S4. Official FineCops levels do not show a monotonic increase in the gap. A4 reduces trainable decoder parameters by 44.4% and cached-decoder latency by 10.1%, although end-to-end latency remains backbone-dominated. These results concern the trainable grounding decoder, not a complete attention-only VLM.

</details>


### [50] [SA-GEM: Scale-Adaptive and Geospatial Evidence-Modulated Token Pruning for Efficient Remote Sensing Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15075)
*Kexin Ma,Jing Xiao,Bowen Xing,Liang Liao,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SA-GEM是一个即插即用的框架，通过任务自适应的token粒度分配和地理空间证据调制的token剪枝，解决遥感大型视觉语言模型在高分辨率处理中token数量激增而关键证据被稀释的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感LVLMs的性能受限于高分辨率处理——视觉token数量随输入分辨率二次增长，而重要的视觉证据本质上是稀疏的，在扩展序列中被稀释。现有token剪枝方法依赖与尺度无关的分辨率策略和孤立的显著性线索，无法实现任务对齐的粒度自适应和整体证据保留。

Method: 提出SA-GEM框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 轻量级路由器根据查询相关的token粒度需求选择分辨率；2) token重要性调制器联合建模任务相关性、空间结构和局部冗余，以保留整体地理空间证据。

Result: 实验表明更高分辨率并非普遍有益，一旦达到足够的粒度，token质量比token数量更重要。SA-GEM在多个基准上相比现有剪枝方法在准确率和效率上均取得一致提升。在XLRS-Bench上，相比GeoLLaVA-8K准确率提升2.3%，总推理速度提升2.4倍。

Conclusion: SA-GEM证明了在有足够粒度的情况下，token质量比数量更关键，通过任务自适应粒度分配和整体化地理空间证据建模，实现了遥感多模态理解中精度与效率的双赢。

Abstract: RS-LVLMs have advanced multimodal understanding of Earth observation imagery, yet their performance is fundamentally constrained by high-resolution processing, as visual token counts grow quadratically with linear input resolution while important visual evidence is inherently sparse and increasingly diluted across the expanded sequence. Existing token pruning methods largely rely on scale-agnostic resolution policies and isolated importance cues, limiting task-aligned granularity adaptation and holistic evidence preservation. To address this, we present Scale-Adaptive and Geospatial Evidence-Modulated Token Pruning (SA-GEM), a plug-and-play framework that unifies task-adaptive token granularity allocation with holistic geospatial token importance modulation. Specifically, a lightweight router selects the resolution based on query-dependent token granularity, while a token importance modulator jointly models task relevance, spatial structure, and local redundancy to preserve holistic geospatial evidence. We show that higher resolution is not universally beneficial and, once sufficient granularity is reached, token quality matters more than token quantity. Experiments across various benchmarks demonstrate that SA-GEM achieves consistent gains in both accuracy and efficiency over existing pruning methods. On XLRS-Bench, it surpasses GeoLLaVA-8K by 2.3% in accuracy with a 2.4 times total inference speedup.

</details>


### [51] [Distribution-free false-alarm calibration and chance-corrected spatial evaluation for industrial anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15090)
*Jie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合无分布上容忍阈值与配对-交叉空间检验的方法，用于更严格地评估工业视觉检测中的误报率和空间证据，弥补AUROC和掩膜重叠指标的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUROC和异常图与缺陷掩膜的重叠度无法指定选定阈值下的误报率，且重复缺陷位置和掩膜几何形状会夸大重叠度，导致评估结果失真。

Method: 将无分布上容忍阈值与配对-交叉空间检验相结合。配对检验比较每个检测器得分贡献位置与匹配缺陷掩膜，交叉检验则与其它图像的掩膜比较；两者比率之差定义为空间证据提升（相对经验偶然重叠率）。

Result: 在120张来自三个ISP-AD模态的点缺陷图像上评估三个检测器：378个告警中230个与匹配掩膜重叠，但配对率与交叉率在九个检测器-模态组合中有八个相似；仅DINOv2-ASM具有正的95%自举下界（提升0.259，区间0.159-0.347）。在独立Magnetic Tile Defect数据集上，WRN50补丁记忆提升0.203（0.169-0.236），ViT-B/16补丁记忆提升0.231（0.202-0.262），两者单侧置换p=10^-5。精确样本规划显示，150个校准正常样本仅支持1.98%或更高的目标假阳性率下95%置信无分布声明；1%目标需至少299个正常样本。

Conclusion: 研究支持在AUROC和原始掩膜重叠之外，报告工作点性能和机会校正后的空间证据。多数检测器在ISP-AD上的空间证据接近偶然水平，而DINOv2在ASM模态和两种补丁记忆方法在Magnetic Tile数据集上显示出显著空间能力提升。

Abstract: Studies of industrial visual inspection commonly report the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) and the overlap between anomaly maps and defect masks. Neither measure specifies the false-alarm rate at a selected threshold, while recurrent defect locations and mask geometry can inflate overlap. We combine a distribution-free upper tolerance threshold with a paired-minus-crossed spatial test. This test compares each detector's score-contributing locations with the matched defect mask and with masks from other images; the difference in rates defines spatial-evidence lift relative to the empirical chance-overlap rate. We evaluate three detectors on 120 point-defect images from three ISP-AD modalities and three fixed data splits. Of 378 alarms, 230 overlap the matched mask. Paired and crossed rates are nevertheless similar in eight of nine detector--modality cells; only DINOv2--ASM has a positive 95\% bootstrap lower bound (lift 0.259, 95\% interval 0.159--0.347). On the independent Magnetic Tile Defect dataset, the same analysis gives lifts of 0.203 (0.169--0.236) for Wide ResNet-50 (WRN50) patch memory and 0.231 (0.202--0.262) for Vision Transformer B/16 (ViT-B/16) patch memory, with one-sided permutation $p=10^{-5}$ for both. When crossed masks are restricted to the same defect class, the lifts remain 0.185 and 0.210. Exact sample planning shows that, with 150 calibration normals, a 95\%-confidence distribution-free claim is supported only for target false-positive rates of 1.98\% or higher; a 1\% target requires at least 299 normals. The results support reporting operating-point performance and chance-corrected spatial evidence alongside AUROC and raw mask overlap.

</details>


### [52] [MODAL: Multi-Modal Object Re-ID via Model-Driven Sparse Decoupling and Text-Image Differential Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15096)
*Chengbo Huang,Jun-Jie Huang,Long Lan,Tianrui Liu,Xueqiong Li,Yuanxi Peng,Xinwang Liu,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MODAL框架，基于耦合稀疏编码与差分抑制理论，通过多模态特征稀疏解耦模块显式分解单模态特有与多模态共享表示，并结合文本-图像差分滤波模块自适应抑制任务无关响应，在四数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态目标重识别方法缺乏原则性的特征解耦与连贯的多模态整合，导致表示纠缠、跨模态冲突、判别线索模糊，且在模态缺失条件下存在分布偏移问题。

Method: 1) 基于多模态耦合稀疏编码的模型驱动深度展开，构建多模态特征稀疏解耦模块，将特征显式分解为单模态特有、双模态与三模态共享表示；2) 模态感知子空间激活机制，在模态缺失场景下仅激活一致共享子空间；3) 文本-图像差分滤波模块，利用粗粒度文本语义自适应抑制视觉表示中任务无关响应。

Result: 在四个数据集上的大量实验表明，MODAL达到了最先进的性能，并具有更优的可解释性/透明性。

Conclusion: 通过原则性的特征解耦与差分抑制，MODAL有效缓解了跨模态冲突及模态缺失下的性能退化，实现了更透明、更鲁棒的多模态目标重识别。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification (Re-ID) aims to facilitate cross-camera object retrieval in complex environments by leveraging complementary information from visual (e.g., RGB, NIR, TIR) and textual modalities. However, existing approaches often lack principled feature disentanglement and coherent multi-modal integration, leading to entangled representations that introduce cross-modal conflicts, obscure discriminative cues, and suffer distribution shift under modality-missing conditions. To tackle these challenges, we propose MODAL, a novel multi-modal object re-identification framework, grounded in coupled sparse coding theory and differential suppression principles. A core component of MODAL is a Multi-modal Feature Sparse Decoupling module, developed in a model-driven deep unrolling manner based on multi-modal coupled sparse coding. It explicitly decomposes multi-modal features into uni-modal specific, bi-modal and tri-modal shared representations, thereby achieving more transparent and effective feature disentanglement. Benefiting from the principled feature disentanglement, MODAL naturally mitigates performance degradation in incomplete-modality scenarios via a Modality-Aware Subspace Activation that selectively activates only the consistently shared subspaces. Moreover, we propose a Text-Image Differential Filtering module that leverages coarse-grained textual semantics to adaptively suppress task-irrelevant responses in the decoupled visual representations, thereby enhancing discriminative information. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate that MODAL achieves state-of-the-art performance with superior transparency.

</details>


### [53] [ProjFormer: Point Cloud Completion via Geometric-Projective Transformer and Cross-Modal Semantic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15104)
*Sheng Liu,Meng Wang,Ruihui Li,Huilong Pi,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProjFormer，一种跨模态点云补全框架，通过显式投影和自适应特征路由实现几何一致的2D-3D交互，在轻量设计下提升结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云补全因其严重稀疏性和观测模糊性本质上是不适定问题。现有多视角方法引入2D语义缓解该问题，但依赖学习式注意力与固定融合，缺乏几何一致性和自适应性。

Method: 提出ProjFormer框架：(1) 投影引导视角注意力模块（Projective Guided View Attention），通过确定性投影将3D点与多视角特征对齐，实现高效且几何一致的聚合；(2) 几何感知路由网络（geometry-aware routing network），对结构与观测驱动特征进行点级自适应融合，实现渐进式精化。

Result: 在轻量化设计下，ProjFormer达到有竞争力的性能，并在结构完整性上取得改进。

Conclusion: 通过显式投影替代学习式注意力、自适应路由替代固定融合，ProjFormer验证了几何一致性与自适应交互可有效提升点云补全的结构完整性。

Abstract: Point cloud completion is inherently ill-posed due to severe sparsity and ambiguity in partial observations. Existing multi-view methods alleviate this by incorporating 2D semantics, but often rely on learned attention and fixed fusion, which lack geometric consistency and adaptability. We propose ProjFormer, a cross-modal framework that enforces geometry-consistent 2D-3D interaction through explicit projection and adaptive feature routing. A Projective Guided View Attention module aligns 3D points with multi-view features via deterministic projection, enabling efficient and geometrically consistent aggregation. Building on this, a geometry-aware routing network performs point-wise adaptive fusion of structural and observation-driven features for progressive refinement. Experiments show that, under a lightweight design, ProjFormer delivers competitive performance with improved structural completeness.

</details>


### [54] [CETalk: Continuous Valence-Arousal Control for Audio-Driven 3D Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15110)
*Peng Jia,Li Dai,Zhen Xiao,Xueliang Liu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CETalk，一个基于连续Valence-Arousal（VA）表征的音频驱动3D面部动画框架，通过动态情感调制模块、多尺度时间建模机制和动态融合机制，实现了细粒度情感控制和高精度的唇形同步，并在自建的大规模3D-VA-MEAD数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感3D说话头生成方法依赖离散情感类别，无法捕捉情感的连续演化过程，同时忽视了音频发音与情感表达之间的时间频率不匹配问题。这两大局限限制了生成动画的情感真实性和可控性。

Method: CETalk通过三个关键组件实现：1）动态情感调制模块，利用音频衍生线索自适应调节情感强度；2）多尺度时间建模机制，采用并行分支解耦高频发音运动与低频情感动态；3）动态融合机制，通过自适应门控网络整合多尺度特征。此外构建了具有自动VA标注和3D面部运动重建的大规模数据集3D-VA-MEAD用于训练和评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，CETalk在唇形同步准确性和情感表现力方面均优于现有最先进方法，同时支持平滑且可控的情感转换。

Conclusion: 通过连续VA表征替代离散情感类别，并解耦音频发音与情感表达的时间频率特性，CETalk为情感3D说话头生成提供了更精细、更可控的解决方案，验证了连续情感建模在该任务中的有效性和潜力。

Abstract: Emotional 3D talking head generation aims to synthesize expressive facial animations with accurate lip synchronization. However, existing methods often rely on discrete emotion categories, which fail to capture the continuous evolution of affect. They also overlook the temporal frequency mismatch between audio articulation and emotional expression. In this paper, we propose CETalk, an audio-driven 3D facial animation framework conditioned on continuous Valence--Arousal (VA) representations for fine-grained emotion control. CETalk predicts a sequence of FLAME parameters through three key components: a Dynamic Emotion Modulation Module that adaptively scales emotional intensity using audio-derived cues; a Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling mechanism that employs parallel branches to decouple high-frequency articulatory movements from low-frequency emotional dynamics; and a Dynamic Fusion Mechanism that integrates these multi-scale features via an adaptive gating network. To support training and evaluation, we construct 3D-VA-MEAD, a large-scale dataset with automatically estimated VA annotations and reconstructed 3D facial motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CETalk outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both lip-sync accuracy and emotional expressiveness, while enabling smooth and controllable emotion transitions.

</details>


### [55] [Fast Test-Time Refinement for Robust Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15113)
*Jiaming Liang,Chi-Man Pun,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了学习图像压缩（LIC）系统的对抗鲁棒性问题，发现了一个称为"非对称对抗轨迹（AAT）"的特性：从对抗区域恢复到良性区域比反向过程容易得多，通常仅需1-2步。基于此发现，作者提出了快速测试时细化（FTTR）框架，利用输入作为标签（Input-as-Label）的特性实现高效且稳健的LIC系统，并在多种强自适应攻击下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIC展示了出色的率失真性能，但深度神经网络赋予的高表征能力也带来了严重的对抗脆弱性，阻碍其成为可信的标准编解码器。现有的测试时细化（TTR）防御方法存在三大问题：迭代开销过大、鲁棒性机制缺乏理论理解、未在白盒场景或超出ℓ2界攻击下评估。作者旨在弥合这些研究空白。

Method: 作者首先揭示了LIC系统中的非对称对抗轨迹（AAT）特性，即从对抗区域到良性区域的转换明显比反向过程更容易。他们提出了二维管模型（Tube Model）来解释这一现象。基于AAT，设计了快速测试时细化（FTTR）框架，并通过Input-as-Label属性论证了鲁棒性来源于对抗区域的收缩而非梯度混淆。

Result: 通过在多种LIC系统上使用多样化的强自适应攻击进行广泛评估，验证了提出的FTTR框架的有效性——能够在仅1-2步细化内粗略恢复对抗样本，大幅降低计算开销，同时保持了强大的防御能力。

Conclusion: 本文提供了对LIC对抗鲁棒性的系统性理解，提出的FTTR框架实现了实用且稳健的LIC系统，并明确了鲁棒性来源的理论基础（Input-as-Label导致的对抗区域收缩），为可信赖的图像压缩编解码器的开发奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has demonstrated remarkable rate-distortion (RD) performance in benign settings. However, the high representational capacity endowed by deep neural networks (DNNs) comes at the expense of increased adversarial vulnerability. This hinders their adoption as trusted standardized codecs. Recent work has sketched test-time refinement (TTR) as a defense in gray-box scenarios, despite its original purpose of improving benign RD performance. Unfortunately, extensive iterations of TTR incur prohibitive overhead, while the robustness mechanism lacks theoretical understanding. Moreover, TTR has not been evaluated in white-box settings or against attacks beyond $\ell_2$-bounded rate and untargeted distortion objectives. To bridge these gaps, we present a systematic study. Our study reveals an Asymmetric Adversarial Trajectory (AAT) property in LIC systems: transitioning from adversarial to benign regions is significantly easier than the reverse process, where adversarial examples can often be roughly recovered within only 1-2 steps. We provide a two-dimensional Tube Model to explain this phenomenon. Based on AAT, we propose a Fast Test-Time Refinement (FTTR) framework for practical and robust LIC systems. We establish that the robustness arises from the contraction of adversarial regions induced by the Input-as-Label property of LIC systems, rather than from obfuscated gradients. Extensive evaluations with diverse strong adaptive attacks across multiple LIC systems demonstrate the promise of the proposed FTTR framework. The code is available at https://github.com/chinaliangjiaming/FTTR.git.

</details>


### [56] [Perspective-Invariant Attack with Enhanced Transferability of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15115)
*Kaisheng Liang,Yiming Cao,Bin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种透视不变攻击（PIA），通过多自由度顶点采样策略覆盖从2-DOF平移到8-DOF投影映射的完整透视变换层次，生成几何多样的输入变化以增强对抗样本的跨模型可迁移性。此外还提出了PIA-Mix扩展方法，结合辅助变换进一步提升攻击性能，实验证明在多种DNN架构、防御机制及多模态大语言模型上均优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输入变换技术主要依赖局部操作（如块级打乱和缩放），自由度有限，忽视了由视角变化自然产生的全局透视变换，导致对抗样本的跨模型可迁移性受限。

Method: 提出PIA方法，采用多自由度顶点采样策略，系统覆盖从2-DOF平移到8-DOF投影映射的完整透视变换层次；同时提出PIA-Mix扩展，维护互补变换池并高效整合透视变换与辅助方法。

Result: 在多种DNN架构、先进防御机制和多模态大语言模型上的大量实验表明，PIA和PIA-Mix的性能均优于现有最先进的基于迁移的攻击方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入全局透视变换来增强输入多样性，可以有效减少对抗扰动对代理模型的过拟合，从而显著提升对抗样本的跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Adversarial examples generated on a surrogate deep neural network (DNN) can often successfully fool other black-box DNN models. This cross-model transferability poses serious security threats to DNNs in practical applications. Input transformation techniques are widely used to enhance adversarial transferability by increasing the diversity of input images. However, existing methods primarily rely on local operations with limited degrees of freedom (DOF), such as block-wise shuffling and resizing, overlooking global perspective transformations that naturally arise from viewpoint changes. In this work, we propose a Perspective-Invariant Attack (PIA), which introduces a multi-DOF vertex sampling strategy that systematically covers the perspective transformation hierarchy from 2-DOF translation to 8-DOF projective mapping. By generating geometrically diverse input variations, PIA effectively reduces overfitting of adversarial perturbations to the surrogate model, thereby improving adversarial transferability. We further propose PIA-Mix, a generic extension that maintains a complementary transformation pool and efficiently combines our perspective transformation with auxiliary methods for improved transferability. Extensive experiments involving various DNN architectures, advanced defense mechanisms, and multimodal large language models (LLMs) demonstrate that PIA and PIA-Mix outperform state-of-the-art transfer-based attacks.

</details>


### [57] [HOIMask: Towards Generative Masked Modeling for Human Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15141)
*Yihong Ji,Jinsong Zhang,He Hu,Hongbo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HOIMask，这是首个用于离散空间中人体-物体交互（HOI）运动生成的生成式掩码框架。它通过HOI向量量化将运动序列和接触感知信号编码为离散的2D token图，并采用基于Transformer的生成式掩码建模框架，同时引入离散空间的接触感知重建引导，以生成更真实、语义对齐的HOI运动，性能优于现有最先进的扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的HOI生成方法虽然能利用接触融合信号引导生成过程，但迭代去噪过程中存在误差累积，导致人工伪影多、交互质量不稳定。因此需要一种新的生成范式来规避这些问题。

Method: 1) 提出HOI向量量化（VQ），将运动序列和接触感知信号编码为离散的2D人体和物体token图，保留细粒度时空结构（优于传统1D表示）；2) 采用生成式掩码建模框架，基于Transformer联合建模时空与交互依赖；3) 在推理阶段引入离散空间的接触感知重建引导，通过融合接触信号优化HOI token，提升时空一致性。

Result: HOIMask在HOI运动生成任务上优于最先进的基于扩散的方法，生成的HOI运动更加真实且语义对齐，伪影更少、交互质量更稳定。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的运动交互token、专用架构和引导策略，HOIMask成功证明了生成式掩码建模在离散空间中建模HOI运动的可行性与优越性，为HOI生成领域提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have dominated the HOI generation, as they enable critical contact fusions or signals to guide the diffusion process. However, they often result in high artifacts and unstable interaction quality due to error accumulation during iterative denoising. In this work, we propose HOIMask, the first generative masked framework for modeling HOI motion in discrete space. HOIMask first encodes both motion sequences and contact-aware signals into discrete 2D human and object token maps via HOI Vector Quantization (VQ), preserving fine-grained spatial-temporal structure beyond conventional 1D representations. On this basis, a generative masked modeling framework is employed to jointly capture human-object interaction dynamics, leveraging a transformer architecture designed to model complex spatial-temporal and interaction dependencies. To generate more coherent and physically plausible motions, we further introduce a novel contact-aware reconstruction guidance in discrete space during inference, which fuses contact signals to optimize HOI tokens that forces the generated motion with higher spatio-temporal consistency. With craftily designed motion interaction tokens, dedicated architecture and guidance strategy, HOIMask outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion-based methods, generating more realistic and semantically aligned HOI motions. Please refer to https://jyhflash.github.io/HOIMask/ for more results.

</details>


### [58] [A Unified Backbone--Expert Framework with Relation-Token and Residual--Classifier Interfaces for Automatic Modulation Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15160)
*Zhixiang Deng,Houbiao Li,Zongyong Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种统一的"主干-专家"框架用于自动调制识别（AMR），通过共享卷积状态空间主干和两个专门接口来应对不同观测长度下的表征瓶颈，在RML2016.10b和HisarMod2019数据集上分别达到67.28%和87.19%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动调制识别在不同观测长度下存在明显的表征瓶颈，单一模型架构难以同时在不同长度序列上表现优异，需要针对短序列和长序列分别设计专门的解决方案。

Method: 提出统一的"主干-专家"框架：包含一个公共卷积状态空间主干和两个专门接口。短序列场景中，在编码前注入显式的滞后感知复平面描述符作为关系token以补偿信息损失；长序列场景中，设计门控多尺度残差细化模块来修正特征图，并结合固定平均分类器协作以利用互补证据。

Result: 在RML2016.10b上达到67.28±0.14%的平均准确率，在HisarMod2019上达到87.19±0.77%（三次运行的平均±样本标准差）。通过三种子消融实验、原生长度交叉配置测试和控制窗口研究进一步验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 实验证实了"专家接口解耦"相比"一刀切"（one-size-fits-all）架构的优势，即针对不同序列长度设计专门接口能够显著提升AMR性能。

Abstract: Automatic modulation recognition (AMR) faces distinct representation bottlenecks under varying observation lengths, where a single model architecture often fails to excel. To address this, we propose a unified backbone-expert framework with a common convolutional state-space backbone and two specialized interfaces. For short sequences, we inject explicit lag-aware complex-plane descriptors as relation tokens before encoding to compensate for information loss. For long sequences, we design a gated multi-scale residual refinement module to correct the feature map, combined with a fixed-averaging classifier collaboration to harness complementary evidence. Our framework achieves overall average accuracies of 67.28 \pm 0.14% on RML2016.10b and 87.19 \pm 0.77% on HisarMod2019 (mean \pm sample standard deviation over three runs), respectively. The framework's efficacy is further validated through three-seed ablations, native-length cross-configuration tests, and controlled window studies, confirming the benefit of expert-interface decoupling over one-size-fits-all architectures.

</details>


### [59] [From "What-If" to "What-Is": Counterfactual Thinking-Inspired Semantic Alignment for Visual Brain Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15163)
*Kaitao Yan,Chi Liu,Congcong Zhu,Huajie Chen,Gengshen Wu,Minghao Wang,Xiaotong Han,Tianqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConceptAlign，一种用于视觉脑解码的反事实语义对齐框架，通过将解码的视觉token与文本嵌入空间对齐，并引入LLM生成的场景保留反事实替代文本来学习细粒度语义边界，提升fMRI重建的语义准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉脑解码方法虽然能生成视觉上逼真的重建，但由于生成先验过强，重建中常包含错误的物体、属性或关系。传统重建指标只评估最终图像，难以捕捉这些语义错误。因此需要一种能在语义层面约束解码表征的方法。

Method: 1) 将解码的视觉token池化并投影到冻结的文本嵌入空间；2) 与真实描述对齐，同时与场景保持但修改单个关键元素（物体、属性或关系）的反事实替代文分离；3) 反事实替代文由LLM离线生成；4) 采用基于margin的损失学习刺激与合理解释之间的细粒度语义边界，推理时无需LLM调用。

Result: 在Natural Scenes Dataset上，ConceptAlign相比MindEye2主干提升了重建指标、反事实语义判别能力和表征对齐。消融实验、独立LLM与人工编写的替代文以及人类评估均验证了该监督方法的有效性和鲁棒性，在细粒度冲突、小样本解码和跨被试结构上表现良好。

Conclusion: ConceptAlign通过反事实语义对齐有效解决了视觉脑解码中语义错误问题，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升解码的语义保真度，为脑解码语义评估提供了系统性的三层框架。

Abstract: Visual brain decoding reconstructs visual content perceived by a person from neural measurements such as fMRI, providing a computational approach to studying how visual information is represented in the brain. Recent multimodal representations and diffusion priors have improved reconstruction realism. However, visually plausible reconstructions may contain incorrect objects, attributes, or relations because a strong generative prior can complete content not sufficiently specified by the decoded representation. Conventional reconstruction metrics mainly assess the final image and may therefore obscure such semantic errors. We propose ConceptAlign, a counterfactual semantic alignment framework for visual brain decoding. ConceptAlign pools decoded visual tokens and projects them into a frozen text-embedding space, aligning the representation with the ground-truth caption while separating it from scene-preserving near-miss alternatives. Generated offline by an LLM, these alternatives modify one critical object, attribute, or relation while retaining the scene. A margin-based objective learns fine-grained semantic boundaries between the observed stimulus and plausible but incorrect interpretations without requiring LLM calls during inference. We introduce a systematic three-level semantic evaluation framework covering foundational discriminability, counterfactual description discrimination, and representational geometry. Experiments on the Natural Scenes Dataset show that ConceptAlign improves reconstruction measures, counterfactual semantic discrimination, and representational alignment over the MindEye2 backbone. Matched negative-source ablations, independent LLM and human-written alternatives, and human evaluation support the effectiveness and robustness of the supervision, with favorable patterns in fine-grained conflicts, limited-data decoding, and cross-subject structure.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Natural-Image Foundation Models: Benchmarking Satellite Pretraining for Ophthalmic Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15195)
*Lovre Antonio Budimir,Mingya Alexa Gong,Alyssa Foong Quinney,Ivana Matovinović,Yukun Zhou,Pearse A. Keane,Sven Lončarić,Marinko V. Šarunić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将卫星图像作为医学视觉基础模型（MedVFM）的预训练域，以解决医学数据稀缺和隐私限制问题。通过对比卫星图像预训练（DINOv3-SAT493m）与自然图像预训练（DINOv3-LVD1689m）及医学专家模型在眼科多模态成像任务上的表现，发现卫星图像是比自然图像更强的预训练源，在血管丰富的en face模态上甚至能匹配或超越使用医学数据的专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉基础模型（MedVFM）的发展受限于数据可获得性、隐私问题和高昂的开发成本。现行方案通常使用自然图像预训练的通用模型权重进行微调，这引入了显著的分布偏移。作者希望寻找一个更接近医学图像视觉特征、且不受隐私限制的预训练数据源。

Method: 提出将卫星图像作为MedVFM的预训练域，并系统性对比了四个模型：DINOv3-SAT493m（493百万卫星图像预训练）、DINOv3-LVD1689m（17亿自然图像预训练）、DINOv3-RETFound和MAE-RETFound（两个医学专家基线模型），在多个眼科成像模态上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，卫星图像预训练在眼科任务上优于自然图像预训练，尤其在en face血管丰富模态上表现突出。在多个任务中，卫星预训练在无需任何医学数据的情况下，匹配甚至超越了使用医学数据训练的专家模型。

Conclusion: 卫星图像是一个被忽视但极具潜力的MedVFM预训练域，其视觉特征与医学图像更为接近，且不存在医学数据的隐私限制，为解决MedVFM开发中的数据瓶颈提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) have emerged as a promising approach in medical imaging, producing broadly applicable systems that can be efficiently adapted across diverse imaging modalities, anatomical regions, and clinical tasks. However, VFMs require extensive training data, and their progress in medical image analysis is constrained by limited data availability, privacy concerns, and high development costs. To alleviate these constraints, medical VFMs (MedVFMs) are often built upon weights from generalist models pretrained on vast amounts of publicly available natural images, introducing a substantial distribution shift for medical task adaptation. To address this, we propose satellite imagery as a novel pretraining domain for MedVFM development and benchmarking, motivated by its closer visual alignment with medical data and its freedom from the privacy constraints that limit medical datasets. Across multiple ophthalmic imaging modalities, we compare DINOv3-SAT493m pretrained on 493 million satellite images against DINOv3-LVD1689m pretrained on 1.7 billion natural images, together with two medical specialist baselines: DINOv3-RETFound and MAE-RETFound. Our experiments show that satellite imagery is a stronger pretraining source than natural images for ophthalmic tasks, particularly on en face vascular-rich modalities. On several tasks, satellite pretraining matches or exceeds the medical specialists on high-resolution en face inputs, despite using no medical data.

</details>


### [61] [Anchor-Regularized Adaptation for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection with DINOv3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15196)
*Hyeongjun Choi,Juhun Lee,Davide Cozzolino,Luisa Verdoliva,Simon S. Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor-Regularized Adaptation (ARA)方法，通过将LoRA适配与冻结的锚分类器相结合，在利用像素级伪影线索的同时保持预训练表示结构的完整性，从而在AI生成图像检测中同时利用对齐和未对齐数据的互补监督，在九个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明训练数据对齐可以去除伪相关以提升AI生成图像检测的泛化能力，但冻结的DINOv3表示即便在未对齐数据上训练也表现优异。作者希望理解这种泛化能力的来源及其局限性，并解决混合对齐与未对齐数据时表示被扭曲导致泛化下降的问题。

Method: 分析发现DINOv3的泛化源于其决策依赖忠实表征真实图像空间的特征，但其最终层对像素级伪影线索不够敏感。为此提出ARA：使用LoRA捕获像素级伪影，同时利用冻结的锚分类器避免偏离原始表示结构，使模型能在不失泛化性的前提下利用像素伪影线索。

Result: ARA在九个多样化且具有挑战性的基准上达到最先进性能，表明该方法能够有效融合来自未对齐和对齐数据的互补监督，实现更有效的AI生成图像检测。

Conclusion: 通过锚定正则化保持预训练表示结构，同时用LoRA增强对像素伪影的敏感性，可以实现对齐和未对齐数据优势的互补，既提升对伪影线索的敏感度又不牺牲跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent works in AI-generated image detection have shown that careful training data alignment can improve generalization by removing spurious correlations. However, linear probes on frozen DINOv3 representations achieve remarkably strong performance even when trained on misaligned datasets. Motivated by this result, we analyze the underlying rationale and the limits of this generalization. We find that frozen DINOv3 performs well because its decisions rely on features that faithfully represent the space of authentic images. At the same time, its final layer is less effective at capturing the subtle pixel-artifact cues that can be emphasized by aligned training data. We further observe that naively mixing aligned and misaligned data during adaptation improves sensitivity to such cues but at the cost of distorting the pre-trained representation, limiting generalization. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-Regularized Adaptation (ARA). We apply Low-Rank Adaptation to capture pixel-level artifacts while leveraging a frozen anchor classifier to avoid deviations from the original representation structure. This allows the model to exploit pixel-artifact cues without sacrificing generalization. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on nine diverse and challenging benchmarks, indicating that ARA enables complementary supervision from misaligned and aligned data for more effective detection.

</details>


### [62] [TERRA: A Hierarchical Parallel Training and Memory Orchestration Framework for High-Resolution AI-based Earth Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15211)
*Ruohan Wu,Ziqi Zhu,Yang Zhao,Jiarui Tang,Yingzhe Cui,Junshi Chen,Zhao Jing,Jun Shi,Hong An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TERRA是一个针对高分辨率AI地球预测模型的层次化并行训练框架，通过采样感知的窗口/序列/张量并行（SAWSTP）和内存编排（MO）技术，支持高达114亿参数的模型在96块H200 GPU上训练，实现39.76 PFLOPS的吞吐量，并显著降低长时程微调的内存峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率AI地球预测模型训练内存密集，现有的分布式系统（如AERIS）主要针对像素级模型，无法联合支持卷积采样模块和移窗Transformer执行；长时程 rollout 微调进一步增加激活内存，这些挑战亟需解决。

Method: TERRA提出两个核心创新：(1) SAWSTP（采样感知的窗口、序列与张量并行），保持采样模块的空间连续布局，并将token路由到拓扑感知的不规则窗口布局供Transformer执行；(2) 内存编排（MO），提供rollout感知的检查点规划和结合输入缓冲与预算约束的激活卸载策略。

Result: 在1/12° GLORYS的Wenhai负载上，TERRA支持多达114亿参数模型在96块H200 GPU上运行，吞吐量达39.76 PFLOPS，强扩展效率65.0%，弱扩展效率94.1%。相比仅检查点策略，MO将峰值GPU内存分配降低32.2%-51.8%，步时间开销最多20.0%，使更小patch和更长rollout的微调成为可能。

Conclusion: TERRA有效解决了高分辨率地球预测模型在大规模分布式训练中的内存与并行效率问题，在保持高扩展性的同时大幅降低长时程微调的内存占用，为更精准的地球预测提供了可行的训练基础设施。

Abstract: Training high-resolution AI-based Earth forecasting models is memory-intensive. Window-based Swin Transformers reduce the quadratic cost of global attention, but existing distributed systems such as AERIS primarily target pixel-level models and do not jointly support convolutional sampling modules and shifted-window execution. Long-lead rollout finetuning further increases activation memory. To address these challenges, we present TERRA, a hierarchical parallel training framework for high-resolution Earth forecasting. TERRA introduces Sampling-Aware Window, Sequence, and Tensor Parallelism (SAWSTP), which preserves spatially contiguous layouts for sampling modules and routes tokens into topology-aware ragged window layouts for Transformer execution. For long-lead finetuning, Memory Orchestration (MO) provides rollout-aware checkpoint planning and combines input buffering with budget-constrained activation offloading. Experiments on the $1/12^\circ$ GLORYS-based Wenhai workload show that TERRA supports models with up to 11.4B parameters on 96 H200 GPUs and sustains up to $39.76$ PFLOPS, achieving $65.0\%$ strong-scaling and $94.1\%$ weak-scaling efficiency. Compared with checkpoint-only policies, MO further reduces peak allocated GPU memory by $32.2\%$--$51.8\%$ with at most $20.0\%$ step-time overhead, which makes finetuning with smaller patch sizes and longer rollouts feasible for improved forecasting accuracy.

</details>


### [63] [DCA-MoE: Spatially Adaptive Cross-Layer Fusion and Density-Routed Experts for Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15213)
*Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DCA-MoE框架，通过空间自适应层融合（SALF）和密度路由多感受野专家（DR-MoE），在冻结的DINOv3编码器上实现内容相关的特征融合与感受野选择，用于人群计数任务。在NWPU-Crowd验证集上取得31.7 MAE/72.2 RMSE（ViT-L/16）和32.2/75.9（ViT-B/16）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人群计数解码器在特征融合和感受野选择上采用空间不变的方式，即对所有位置使用相同的融合策略和感受野模式。然而人群场景中透视、头部尺度、遮挡和背景杂乱变化剧烈，不同空间位置需要不同的特征融合权重和感受野大小。作者希望让这些决策成为内容相关的（content dependent）。

Method: 1) 冻结DINOv3编码器（不更新骨干网络）；2) 空间自适应层融合（SALF）：为四个对齐的骨干特征预测逐位置权重，实现空间自适应的特征融合；3) 密度路由多感受野专家（DR-MoE）：为每个位置分配局部、中程和大范围残差专家的软混合；4) EBC风格头重建块密度；5) 使用DMCount监督和辅助路由平衡项训练解码器。

Result: 在NWPU-Crowd验证集上，最强配置（DINOv3 ViT-L/16）获得31.7 MAE和72.2 RMSE；匹配的ViT-B/16完整模型获得32.2/75.9。跨数据集结果混合，部分组件基线报告了单一种子的独立选择最小值。

Conclusion: 证据支持空间自适应融合和路由的可行性，但需要进行更广泛的配对评估和多种子实验才能进行因果归因。跨数据集泛化表现不稳定，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Crowd counting must recover reliable local density under severe variations in perspective, head scale, occlusion, and background clutter. Although modern counting objectives provide strong spatial supervision, many multi-level decoders still use spatially invariant feature fusion and apply one receptive-field pattern to every location. We propose DCA-MoE, a framework that makes both decisions content dependent while retaining a frozen DINOv3 encoder. Spatially Adaptive Layer Fusion (SALF) predicts position-wise weights over four aligned backbone features, and Density-Routed Multi-Receptive-Field Experts (DR-MoE) assigns each location a soft mixture of local, mid-range, and large-context residual experts. An EBC-style head reconstructs block density, while DMCount supervision and an auxiliary routing-balance term train the decoder without updating the backbone. On the NWPU-Crowd validation split, the strongest paired configuration, based on DINOv3 ViT-L/16, obtains 31.7 MAE and 72.2 RMSE; the matched ViT-B/16 full model obtains a paired 32.2/75.9. Cross-dataset results remain mixed, and several component baselines currently report independently selected minima from a single seed. The evidence therefore supports the feasibility of spatially adaptive fusion and routing, while broader paired and multi-seed evaluation remains necessary for causal attribution.

</details>


### [64] [Self-Supervised Topologically Invariant Manifold Learning for Railway Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15217)
*Tingqiong Cui,Yibu Yang,Yang Li,Jiahao Fu,Xiaoliu Luo,Xu Wang,Mengzhu Wang,Siyuan Liu,Guanghui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种完全自监督的盲图像质量评估（BIQA）框架，基于边界约束下的拓扑不变流形学习，无需人工标注即可构建稳定质量参考，并在合成和真实工业数据集上展现出优异的零样本迁移能力和拓扑鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BIQA方法依赖合成失真和主观标注，在真实世界域中泛化能力受限，亟需一种无需人工标签的自监督方案。

Method: 通过重复随机裁剪生成渐进式背景稀释尺度，利用目标信息密度的单调降级建立自约束质量流形；使用线性化空间矩投影消除几何畸变；通过单调性散度滤波器筛除背景敏感评估器，选出精英池；用稳健M估计器和主成分稳定器融合为渐近有效的伪真值。

Result: 从11个基线指标中蒸馏出的精英评估器池在CSIQ、LIVEC、LIVE-2等标准基准上取得优越的零样本迁移性能；在CQU铁路车辆监控数据集（2,797张图像）上流形余弦相似度>0.999，工业极端应力下存活率100%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了跨范式的解耦和拓扑韧性，验证了完全自监督BIQA在真实工业场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Existing blind image quality assessment (BIQA) methods typically rely on synthetic distortions and subjective annotations, limiting generalization in real-world domains. To address this, we propose a fully self-supervised BIQA framework based on topologically invariant manifold learning under boundary constraints, which constructs a stable quality reference without manual labels. The framework generates progressive background dilution scales via repeated random cropping around each target; exploiting the monotonic degradation of target information density across these scales, it establishes a self-constrained quality manifold. A linearized spatial moment projection eliminates geometric distortions from random cropping; then a monotonicity divergence filter prunes background-sensitive evaluators, isolating an elite pool \(\mathcal{M}_{\text{elite}}\). A robust M-estimator with a principal component stabilizer fuses the metrics into an asymptotically efficient pseudo-ground truth \(q_{\text{PGT}}\), contracting variance toward the Cramér-Rao lower bound. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that the elite evaluator pool, distilled from 11 baseline metrics, secures superior zero-shot transferability across standard synthetic and wild benchmarks (CSIQ, LIVEC, LIVE-2). Concurrently, deployments on the CQU Railway Rolling Stock Surveillance Dataset (2,797 images) yield a manifold cosine similarity \(>0.999\) and a 100.0\% survival rate under industrial extreme stresses, robustly validating its cross-paradigm decoupling and topological resilience.

</details>


### [65] [PersonaDrive: Controllable Trajectory Prediction with Multi-Dimensional Driving Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15230)
*Chan Lee,Kimin Yun,Yuseok Bae,Seong Tae Kim,Jung Uk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCT数据集与PersonaDrive框架，将驾驶人格沿"时间紧迫性"和"乘坐舒适性"两个轴解耦，形成9种人格网格，并训练可依据自然语言生成人格特定轨迹的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测与端到端自动驾驶方法缺乏有意义的可控性；已有基准要么无人格标注，要么仅支持单一紧迫性谱系，无法区分相同紧迫程度但需不同驾驶动态的人格。

Method: 提出PCT数据集（以时间紧迫性与乘坐舒适性两轴、各三级构成9种人格网格，配备自然语言描述与轨迹）；提出PersonaDrive框架，包含Persona-Conditioned Anchor Transform（PCAT）沿两轴层级重塑锚点，以及Persona-Conditioned Multi-Modal Fusion（PCMF）进行BEV级人格融合；训练由Hierarchical Guide Loss（保持轴向物理排序）和Axis-Decomposed Diversity Loss（防止对角模态坍缩）监督。

Result: 实验表明PersonaDrive在多维场景中一致优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 通过双轴人格解耦与层级锚点变换，可实现语言驱动的可控人格特定轨迹生成，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Although recent trajectory prediction and end-to-end autonomous driving methods improve robustness in urban environments, they still lack meaningful controllability. Existing benchmarks either provide no persona-conditioned annotations or support only a single urgency spectrum (i.e., emergency, normal, relaxed), which cannot distinguish personas that share the same urgency level but require different driving dynamics. To address this, we propose (i) the Persona-Conditioned Trajectory (PCT) dataset, which decomposes driving personas along two axes, Temporal Urgency and Ride Comfort, and combines three levels of each to form a grid of nine personas, each paired with natural-language descriptions and trajectories, and (ii) PersonaDrive, a framework that can learn driving personas from language and can generate persona-specific trajectories. PersonaDrive incorporates Persona-Conditioned Anchor Transform (PCAT), which hierarchically reshapes anchors along both axes, and Persona-Conditioned Multi-Modal Fusion (PCMF) for BEV-level persona fusion. Training is supervised by a Hierarchical Guide Loss enforcing axis-aligned physical orderings and an Axis-Decomposed Diversity Loss preventing diagonal mode collapse. Experimental results show that PersonaDrive consistently improves over the compared baselines across multi-dimensional scenarios. The code and PCT dataset are available at https://github.com/VisualAIKHU/PersonaDrive

</details>


### [66] [UC-VLM: Consistency-Driven Learning for AI-Generated Image Detection with Vision-Language Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15238)
*Lei Tan,Shuwei Li,Mohan Kankanhalli,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UC-VLM是一个统一的多阶段框架，仅依赖二元监督进行AI生成图像(AIGI)检测，通过自动优化指令、视觉判别目标和标签条件生成目标，在GenImage和Chameleon数据集上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLLM-based AIGI检测器大多只微调语言侧，忽视了低级视觉取证线索；同时依赖人工设计提示词或人工标注的推理过程，限制了可扩展性。

Method: UC-VLM采用多阶段训练框架：(1)自动识别有效指令变体；(2)视觉判别目标，增强对非语义取证线索的敏感性；(3)标签条件生成目标，用二元标签监督文本输出。通过将二元监督信号统一复用于视觉适应和标签条件文本生成，避免了人工推理过程或手工提示词的需求。

Result: 在GenImage上达到96.1%的平均准确率（超过最强先前结果4.6%）；在Chameleon数据集上，ProGAN训练下达到69.6%、SDV1.4训练下达到77.9%的准确率，分别超过最佳基线11.2%和15.3%。

Conclusion: 二元弱监督信号可以在统一框架中被有效复用，同时驱动视觉路径的语言输出的优化，且自动优化的指令能降低提示敏感性，为无需人工标注的AIGI检测提供了可行的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) are promising for AI-generated image (AIGI) detection because they can produce both a prediction and a natural-language output. However, most existing VLLM-based detectors primarily fine-tune the language side while giving limited attention to low-level visual forensic cues. They also often depend on manually crafted prompts or human-annotated rationales, which limits scalability.We present UC-VLM, a unified multi-stage framework for AIGI detection that relies solely on binary supervision. UC-VLM first identifies effective instruction variants automatically. It then reuses the same binary label within a multi-stage training framework: (i) a visual discrimination objective that strengthens sensitivity to non-semantic forensic cues, and (ii) a label-conditioned generation objective that uses the binary label to supervise textual outputs. This design turns weak binary supervision into a shared supervision signal for both the visual pathway and the language output. Our key novelty is a unified multi-stage binary-supervised framework that consistently reuses the same authenticity labels for visual adaptation and label-conditioned text generation, while leveraging automatically optimized instructions to reduce prompt sensitivity without requiring human-written rationales or hand-crafted prompts.Experiments show that UC-VLM achieves 96.1% average accuracy on GenImage, exceeding the strongest prior result by 4.6%, and obtains 69.6% / 77.9% accuracy on Chameleon under ProGAN / SDV1.4 training, surpassing the best baseline by 11.2% / 15.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [67] [CG-GLORE: A Conjugate Gradient-Based Global-Local Regularization Network for Sparse-View CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15246)
*Tran Xuan Hieu Le,Doanh C. Bui,Vu Trung Duong Le,Hoai Luan Pham,Khang Nguyen,Mai K. Nguyen,Tu Bao Ho,Yasuhiko Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CG-GLORE，一种基于二阶优化的紧凑深度展开框架，用于稀疏视图CT重建。每层用CG求解基于结构化Hessian代理的线性系统，并设计GLORE网络结合卷积局部特征与Nyström注意力的全局-局部正则化，在AAPM和DeepLesion数据集上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图CT重建是高度病态逆问题，现有深度学习方法多依赖一阶更新或大型正则化网络，在病态条件下效果有限，需要更有效的二阶启发式重建框架。

Method: 1) 深度展开框架模拟二阶优化，每层用CG求解基于结构化Hessian代理的线性系统——保留数据保真项的物理曲率，对学习正则化项用恒等近似；2) 设计GLORE网络：卷积局部特征提取 + 基于稀疏分块和Nyström注意力的长程依赖表示模块，兼顾细节与全局非局部依赖。

Result: 在AAPM和DeepLesion数据集、多种稀疏视图和噪声设置下，CG-GLORE相比代表性方法在定量指标、收敛稳定性、噪声功率和视觉保真度上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 二阶启发式的紧凑展开框架结合高效的全局-局部正则化网络，为病态稀疏视图CT重建提供了一种性能更强、收敛更稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse-view computed tomography (CT) reduces radiation dose by acquiring fewer projection views, but the resulting inverse problem is highly ill-posed and often produces severe streak artifacts. Existing deep reconstruction methods have achieved promising performance, yet many rely on first-order updates or large regularization networks, which can be less effective in ill-conditioned settings. We propose \textbf{CG-GLORE}, a compact deep unrolling framework inspired by second-order optimization for sparse-view CT reconstruction. Each unrolled stage uses a CG-solved linear system based on a structured Hessian surrogate: it retains the physics-induced curvature of the data-fidelity term while using an identity approximation for the learned regularization term. Thus, the method is second-order-inspired rather than an exact Newton method for the full learned objective. To model image priors, we design a Global-Local Regularization Network (GLORE), which combines convolutional local feature extraction with a Long-Range Dependency Representation module based on sparse patchification and Nyström attention. This design captures anatomical details and non-local dependencies while maintaining practical complexity.
