Experiments on AAPM and DeepLesion under multiple sparse-view and noise settings show that CG-GLORE achieves strong quantitative performance, stable convergence, lower noise power, and improved visual fidelity compared with representative reconstruction methods.

</details>


### [68] [Robust structure from motion for aerial-ground images via detector-free feature matching and multi-view track refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15251)
*San Jiang,Hui Wang,Xing Zhang,Zhongwen Hu,Zhijun Wang,Ruisheng Wang,Wanshou Jiang,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种旋转鲁棒的检测器无关匹配网络，结合多视图轨迹细化用于航地影像集成三维重建，解决了视角、尺度和旋转剧烈变化下的特征匹配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航地集成影像的三维重建对高精度城市建模至关重要，但不同平台（航空与地面）采集的图像在视角、尺度和旋转上存在严重差异，导致特征匹配鲁棒性不足，限制了增量式运动恢复结构（ISfM）的重建精度。

Method: 提出四模块工作流：(1) 旋转感知特征提取——采用全向状态空间块（OSS Block）在八个对称方向上进行选择性扫描，建模长距离空间依赖并合成旋转不变特征图；(2) 多尺度注意力变换——利用四叉树注意力构建层级token金字塔，隔离高关联token区域并丢弃无关区域，以线性复杂度捕捉长程上下文；(3) 双向特征匹配——执行对称的从粗到精匹配方案，粗对齐在互近邻约束下计算双向Softmax置信矩阵，精对齐用多层感知机回归亚像素坐标偏移；(4) 多视图轨迹细化——利用集成索引结构评估局部空间邻近性，将断裂的子轨迹链接到最高置信度锚点，确保ISfM流程中的特征可重复性。

Result: 在真实航地数据集上，该方法相比LoFTR在5°位姿误差下的AUC提升了93.9%，ISfM重建精度达到最高，改进幅度为27.6%至32.7%。

Conclusion: 所提方法为航地影像集成三维重建提供了可靠解决方案，有效应对了跨平台影像匹配中的旋转和视角变化挑战。

Abstract: Integrated 3D reconstruction from aerial-ground images is essential for generating high-precision urban 3D models, yet severe variations in viewpoint, scale, and rotation make robust feature matching highly challenging. To address these limitations, this study introduces a rotation-robust detector-free matching network coupled with multi-view track refinement for incremental Structure from Motion (ISfM). The proposed workflow features four key modules. First, rotation-aware feature extraction replaces traditional convolutions with an Omnidirectional State Space Block (OSS Block) that selectively scans across eight symmetrical directions to model long-range spatial dependencies and synthesize rotation-invariant feature maps. Second, multi-scale attention transformation utilizes quadtree attention to build a hierarchical token pyramid that isolates high-association token regions and discards irrelevant areas, capturing long-range context with linear computational complexity. Third, bi-directional feature matching executes a symmetric coarse-to-fine matching scheme where coarse alignment computes dual-direction Softmax confidence matrices under mutual nearest neighbor constraints, and fine alignment uses a multi-layer perceptron to regress sub-pixel coordinate offsets. Finally, multi-view track refinement employs an integrated indexing structure to evaluate localized spatial proximity and link disjoint sub-tracks to the highest-confidence anchor point, ensuring stable feature repeatability across the ISfM pipeline. By using real aerial-ground datasets, experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves AUC at 5° pose error by 93.9% compared with LoFTR and achieves the highest precision in ISfM reconstruction, with the improved accuracy ranging from 27.6% to 32.7%. The proposed method provides a reliable solution for integrated 3D reconstruction of aerial-ground images.

</details>


### [69] [UAV Video Deblurring via Motion-Aware Diffusion: A Path to Robust Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15259)
*Zhiqiang Hu,Shouren Huang,Masatoshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算高效且有效的无人机视频去模糊方法，通过自适应潜在尺度选择器动态调整分辨率以平衡细节保留与推理效率，并引入多帧对齐与可学习门控模块来确保时间一致性，从而提升目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视频常因快速飞行、振动和相机移动产生严重运动模糊，这会显著降低目标检测等下游任务的性能。现有方法在计算效率和效果之间难以兼顾，因此需要一种计算高效且有效的视频去模糊方法。

Method: 1) 提出自适应潜在尺度选择器(Adaptive Latent Scale Selector)，根据无人机运动强度动态调整潜在空间分辨率，在细节保留与推理效率之间取得平衡。2) 引入多帧对齐与可学习门控模块(Multi-Frame Alignment and Learnable Gating)，对前序帧进行扭曲和门控，仅融合相关的时序信息，抑制未对齐或无信息量的特征。

Result: 在真实无人机基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法不仅取得了优越的去模糊性能，还显著提升了目标检测精度，适用于鲁棒的空中视觉任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效从无人机视频流中恢复清晰细节，同时兼顾计算效率，具有较强的实际应用价值，适用于需要鲁棒性的空中视觉场景。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) play a crucial role in various scenarios ranging from disaster response to traffic surveillance. However, aerial video footage often suffers from severe motion blur due to rapid flight maneuvers, vibrations, and camera panning, which can significantly degrade downstream tasks such as target detection. Our goal is to explore a computationally-efficient and effective video deblurring approach to enhance UAV target detection performance. To reduce computational cost, we first propose an Adaptive Latent Scale Selector that dynamically adjusts the latent space resolution according to the intensity of UAV motion, thus balancing detail preservation with inference efficiency. To ensure temporal consistency, we introduce a Multi-Frame Alignment and Learnable Gating module to warp and gate the preceding frames, allowing the model to fuse only relevant temporal information and suppress misaligned or uninformative features. Our method can effectively recover sharp details from the UAV video stream. Extensive experiments on real UAV benchmarks demonstrate that our method not only yields superior deblurring performance but also significantly boosts target detection accuracy, making it highly applicable to robust aerial vision tasks.

</details>


### [70] [VGGT-Align: Bridging Local Reconstruction and Global Consistency for Long-Sequence 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15260)
*Wei Zhang,Yihang Wu,Songhua Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SGIA框架，通过提取场景几何不变量来约束块间尺度一致性，将7自由度Sim(3)对齐退化为6自由度刚体变换，切断尺度漂移的链式传播，并结合测试时适应策略，无需离线重训即可显著提升长序列3D重建的轨迹稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列3D重建中，分块推理流程的尺度自由度未被约束，导致估计误差乘法性累积，造成全局轨迹和点云几何的严重畸变。这是尺度漂移这一核心问题的根源。

Method: 1) SGIA：从每个块的预测点云中通过粗到精的鲁棒估计提取主导几何不变量，利用块间一致性建立独立于点云配准的尺度约束，将7-DoF Sim(3)对齐显式退化为6-DoF刚体变换；2) 轻量级测试时适应策略：仅微调归一化层参数，通过多目标自监督逐步改善块内预测。

Result: 在多个长序列基准上达到最先进性能，绝对轨迹误差最高降低32%，轨迹稳定性和重建质量均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 利用结构化场景中几何量的时序不变性来锚定尺度一致性，是一种有效的即插即用方案，无需离线重训即可从根源上消除尺度误差的链式传播。

Abstract: Maintaining global geometric consistency is a central challenge in long-sequence 3D reconstruction, with scale drift being the most critical failure mode. In chunk-based inference pipelines, the scale degree of freedom in sequential Sim(3) alignment is left unconstrained, causing estimation errors to compound multiplicatively and distort global trajectories and point cloud geometry. We present a scale-consistency enhancement framework built on a key insight: in structured environments such as driving scenes, geometric quantities arising from environmental regularity remain inherently invariant across temporal segments, and discrepancies in their per-chunk measurements directly expose inter-chunk scale drift. We propose Scene Geometric Invariant Anchoring (SGIA), which extracts dominant geometric invariants from each chunk's predicted point cloud via coarse-to-fine robust estimation and exploits their cross-chunk consistency to establish scale constraints independent of point cloud registration, explicitly degenerating 7-DoF Sim(3) alignment into 6-DoF rigid-body transformation and severing chain-wise scale error propagation at its source. We further introduce a lightweight test-time adaptation strategy that fine-tunes only normalization-layer parameters via multi-objective self-supervision, progressively improving intra-chunk predictions along the sequence. Both modules are plug-and-play and require no offline retraining. Experiments on multiple long-sequence benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing absolute trajectory error by up to 32% with significant gains in trajectory stability and reconstruction quality. Code: https://github.com/WZ-CS/VGGT-Align

</details>


### [71] [Boundary-Aligned Contribution Routing for Robust Optical--SAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15261)
*Haifa Zhang,Yijing Wang,Haoyu Wang,Zheng Li,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于任务效用视角的模态贡献路由方法，用于光学-SAR 遥感目标检测中的跨模态融合。通过两种路由器（Feature Router 和 Dual-Statistic Semantic Router）在无需额外监督的情况下，仅依靠检测监督信号来调节各模态的贡献，从而缓解负向跨模态迁移问题。在 M4-SAR 和 SpaceNet6-OTD 数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学图像提供丰富外观信息，而 SAR 对光照和天气不敏感，二者融合对遥感目标检测具有吸引力。然而，多模态并不保证有益融合——不完善的空间、时间和语义对应可能使原本完好的模态流产生条件性危害，导致负向跨模态迁移。现有方法缺乏从任务效用角度动态调节模态贡献的机制。

Method: 提出两种基于任务条件贡献路由的机制：(1) Feature Router——针对浅层频繁交互的架构，在输入附近执行跨条件、分组可寻址的调制；(2) Dual-Statistic Semantic Router——针对双骨干架构，利用模态特定均值和最大值统计信息预测流级贡献权重，在晚期语义融合前进行调节。两者均在第一次学习到的跨模态特征值混合操作之前调节各模态贡献，且仅依赖检测监督，无需效用监督、质量标签、重建或蒸馏。

Result: 在 M4-SAR 和 SpaceNet6-OTD 数据集上，路由方法在完整输入下提升 mAP50 0.5-5.9 点；相对于模态丢弃基线，缺失模态情况下提升 mAP50 7.6-41.6 点；负向迁移率最高降低 12.7 个百分点。学习到的路由权重与逐模态留一效用之间的 Spearman 相关系数为 0.45-0.66。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持路由系数具有任务效用解释，所提出的融合边界对齐路由方法能有效缓解负向跨模态迁移，提升光学-SAR 融合目标检测的整体性能，且在缺失模态和模态损坏等恶劣条件下表现稳健。

Abstract: Optical imagery provides rich appearance cues, whereas synthetic aperture radar (SAR) offers observations that are less sensitive to illumination and weather, making optical--SAR fusion attractive for remote-sensing object detection. However, the presence of multiple modalities does not guarantee beneficial fusion: imperfect spatial, temporal, and semantic correspondence can make an otherwise intact stream conditionally harmful and induce negative cross-modal transfer. We handle this issue through a model-specific task-utility perspective and learn task-conditioned contribution routing using detection supervision alone. The proposed fusion-boundary-aligned routing regulates each modality's contribution before the first learned cross-modal feature-value mixing operation. For architectures with frequent shallow interaction, a Feature Router performs cross-conditioned, group-addressable modulation near the input; for dual-backbone architectures, a Dual-Statistic Semantic Router predicts stream-level contribution weights from modality-specific average and maximum statistics before late semantic fusion. The routers require no explicit utility supervision, quality labels, reconstruction, or distillation. Experiments on M4-SAR and SpaceNet6-OTD cover nominal full inputs, controlled correspondence shifts, missing modalities, and four nonzero modality-corruption scenarios. Across the reported clean-training controls, routing improves full-input $\text{mAP}_{50}$ by 0.5--5.9 points. Relative to the corresponding modality-dropout baselines, it raises missing-modality $\text{mAP}_{50}$ by 7.6--41.6 points and reduces the negative-transfer rate by up to 12.7 percentage points. Spearman correlations between the learned routing weights and model-specific leave-one-modality-out utility range from 0.45 to 0.66, supporting the task-utility interpretation of the routing coefficients.

</details>


### [72] [On the Adversarial Robustness of Remote Sensing Semantic Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15267)
*Weikang Yu,Yonghao Xu,Pedram Ghamisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对语义变化检测（SCD）任务的任务专用对抗鲁棒性评估框架。与传统的单图像分割或二值变化检测不同，SCD涉及双时相输入、时间戳级语义预测、变化定位和语义变化解码之间的对抗性依赖关系。该框架将输出侧攻击目标与输入侧时间扰动访问分离，系统分析了组件脆弱性和跨时间传播。在四个数据集和六种代表性模型上评估了多种攻击方法，发现最终语义变化预测可被严重破坏即使二值变化定位保持相对稳定，且单时间戳扰动可传播影响另一时间戳的预测。研究证明SCD的对抗鲁棒性取决于完整的双时相预测通路而非单一分支或骨干架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗鲁棒性评估协议主要针对单图像分割或二元变化检测设计，无法捕捉语义变化检测中双时相输入与时间戳语义预测、变化定位、语义变化解码之间的对抗性耦合依赖关系，缺乏任务专用的系统化鲁棒性评估框架。

Method: 提出一个任务特有的评估框架，将输出侧攻击目标（组件级与时间戳级）与输入侧时间扰动访问（单/双时间戳扰动）进行分离，在四个数据集和六种涵盖CNN、Transformer和状态空间模型上，评估组件级和时间戳目标、多种攻击方法以及跨架构迁移性。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 即使二值变化定位保持相对稳定，最终语义变化预测仍可被严重破坏；(2) 与某一时间戳相关的扰动或攻击目标可传播影响另一时间戳的预测；(3) 这些行为在不同架构家族中普遍存在；(4) 直接的跨模型迁移攻击明显弱于白盒攻击。

Conclusion: SCD的对抗鲁棒性依赖于完整的双时相预测通路而非单个分支或骨干家族，本文提供了一个用于评估耦合双时相图像分析鲁棒性的结构化协议。

Abstract: Semantic change detection (SCD) is a bitemporal dense-prediction task that jointly identifies changed regions and their semantic states before and after change. Unlike single-image segmentation or binary change detection, SCD couples two temporal inputs with timestamp-wise semantic prediction, change localization, and final semantic-change decoding, creating adversarial dependencies that are not captured by conventional robustness protocols. We present a task-specific evaluation framework that separates output-side attack objectives from input-side temporal perturbation access, enabling systematic analysis of component vulnerability and cross-temporal propagation. Experiments on four datasets and six representative CNN-, Transformer-, and state-space-based models evaluate component-level and temporal objectives, single- and dual-timestamp perturbations, multiple attack methods, and cross-architecture transferability. The results show that final semantic-change predictions can be severely corrupted even when binary change localization remains comparatively stable, and that perturbations or attack objectives associated with one timestamp can propagate to the prediction of the other. These behaviors occur across different architecture families, while direct cross-model transfer remains considerably weaker than white-box attacks. The study demonstrates that adversarial robustness in SCD depends on the complete bitemporal prediction pathway rather than on an individual branch or backbone family, and provides a structured protocol for evaluating robustness in coupled bitemporal image analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/EricYu97/AdvSCD.

</details>


### [73] [Memory-Bounded Continuation of Greedy Sampling for Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15277)
*Yoon Gyo Jung,Jaewoo Park,Kuan-Chuan Peng,Seongdeok Bang,Octavia Camps*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContCore方法，通过"贪心延续核集"（greedy-continued coreset）实现在固定内存条件下对持续异常检测任务进行可靠的表示性数据采样，并提供了理论保证和广泛的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续异常检测中任务顺序到达，需要维护一个固定大小的数据核集以衡量与正常的距离。然而已有方法要么需要无界内存（朴素核集累积），要么容易灾难性遗忘（神经网络方法）。作者观察到反复在贪心采样集上继续贪心采样能在严格内存限制下保持表示性，但缺乏理论和系统性验证。

Method: ContCore包含两个关键步骤：1) 贪心扩展（greedy expansion）——对每个新任务的正常数据特征进行贪心采样以纳入核集；2) 贪心合并（greedy consolidation）——在核集可能超出内存预算时，对当前累积的核集再次进行贪心选择以压缩回固定大小。论文从理论上证明了该贪心延续核集能在一个有界差距内近似最优核集（oracle coreset）。

Result: 在MVTecAD和VisA数据集的11个任务调度上取得最先进性能（SOTA），并在在线持续异常检测设置中显著优于先前方法（后者性能大幅下降）。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 贪心延续策略是一种优雅且实用的解决方案：在固定内存约束下，通过反复贪心采样而非无界累积，核集质量以渐进方式而非灾难性方式退化，从而在持续异常检测中兼顾理论保证和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Greedy sampling produces a compact yet representative summary of normal data, which is essential for reliable anomaly detection that relies on measuring distance from normality. For continual anomaly detection where tasks arrive sequentially, extending greedy sampling is straightforward with unbounded memory through coreset accumulation. However, practical deployment requires fixed memory where the coreset size remains constant regardless of task count. We observe that continued greedy sampling, which iteratively applies greedy selection over previously greedy-sampled sets, effectively preserves representativeness under strict memory limits. Despite discarding data at each step to satisfy the memory constraint, coreset quality degrades gracefully rather than catastrophically, enabling reliable anomaly detection across the tasks. We provide theoretical justification by showing that resulting greedy-continued coreset approximates the oracle coreset within a bounded gap. We instantiate this principle in ContCore, which constructs a greedy-continued coreset through greedy expansion on new task features followed by greedy consolidation to enforce the memory budget. Unlike neural methods susceptible to catastrophic forgetting or naive coreset accumulation requiring unbounded memory, ContCore maintains fixed memory with theoretical guarantees. Empirically, ContCore achieves state-of-the-art performance across 11 task schedules on MVTecAD and VisA, and extends effectively to online continual AD settings where prior methods degrade significantly. Code: https://github.com/jungyg/ContCore

</details>


### [74] [Geometry-Aware Spatio-Temporal Context Modeling for 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15279)
*Sitao Chen,Zhuangwei Zhuang,Hui Luo,Qingyao Wu,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAST方法，一种几何感知的时空上下文建模方法，用于4D占据预测。通过渐进式显式-隐式生成和双路径时空建模，解决了现有方法在静态结构几何失真和时序一致性问题上的不足，在Occ3D-nuScenes上以2.84倍加速超越SOTA 7.67% mIoU和6.44% IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D占据预测方法依赖离散token化和自回归预测，存在两个关键问题：静态结构几何失真（geometric distortion）和预测范围内时序一致性不足（inconsistent temporal coherence），限制了自动驾驶极端场景模拟的可靠性。

Method: GAST包含两大核心模块：(1) 生成模块——通过pose-driven warping（位姿驱动扭曲）、motion-aware feature modulation（运动感知特征调制）和attention-based feature refinement（注意力特征精化）实现渐进式显式-隐式生成，保证每帧占据的高几何保真度和语义合理性；(2) 时空模块——通过全局上下文聚合增强空间一致性，通过时序动态提取捕捉场景演变，采用双路径建模。整体采用端到端联合优化历史重建和未来预测。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes数据集上，GAST在mIoU上超越SOTA 7.67%，在IoU上超越6.44%，同时实现2.84倍推理加速，并在长期预测场景中保持强性能。

Conclusion: GAST通过几何感知的显式-隐式渐进生成与双路径时空建模的统一设计，在精度、速度和长期预测鲁棒性上全面超越现有方法，为自动驾驶4D占据预测提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting models the spatio-temporal evolution of 3D scenes and is crucial for autonomous driving, especially for corner-case simulation. Existing methods often rely on discrete tokenization followed by autoregressive prediction, yet struggle with geometric distortion in static structures and inconsistent temporal coherence over the forecasting horizon. In this work, we propose a Geometry-Aware Spatio-Temporal context modeling method (GAST) for 4D occupancy forecasting, built upon progressive explicit-implicit generation and dual-path spatio-temporal modeling. Specifically, the generation module produces per-frame occupancy with high geometric fidelity and semantic plausibility through pose-driven warping, motion-aware feature modulation, and attention-based feature refinement. Subsequently, the spatio-temporal module enhances spatial consistency through global context aggregation while capturing scene evolution through temporal dynamics extraction. This unified design enables joint optimization of historical reconstruction and future forecasting in an end-to-end manner. Extensive experiments on Occ3D-nuScenes demonstrate the superiority of our method, outperforming the state-of-the-art by 7.67% in mIoU and 6.44% in IoU with a 2.84x speedup, while maintaining strong performance in long-term forecasting.

</details>


### [75] [SOS! : A Streamlined Object-Conditional Transformer for Model-free Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15295)
*Jiaqi Hu,Junwen Huang,Hongli Xu,Peter KT Yu,Nassir Navab,Benjamin Busam,Slobodan Ilic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SOS（Streamlined Object-conditional Transformer），一种无需3D模型的对象条件分割框架。仅需单张参考图像即可完成未见目标物体的分割，通过对象条件Transformer学习身份锚定查询，将掩码生成与目标识别统一到单次前向传播中，在多个基准上达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础分割模型虽能生成高质量的类别无关掩码，但难以将这些候选掩码与特定目标对象关联起来，这一语义鸿沟严重阻碍了其在机器人操作等下游任务中的应用。已有方法依赖详尽的3D对象模型先验，引入了高昂的计算开销和复杂的多阶段流程。

Method: 提出SOS框架，完全消除对3D模型的依赖，每类目标仅需一张参考图像。核心创新是对象条件Transformer，学习身份锚定查询（identity-anchored queries），将掩码生成和目标识别统一到单个前向传播中完成。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，SOS在无模型未见对象分割任务上达到了新的最先进水平，同时提供准确且高效的性能。

Conclusion: SOS通过无需3D模型的简洁设计，高效解决了未见目标对象的分割与识别问题，为机器人在非结构环境中的操作提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation segmentation models excel at generating high-quality, class-agnostic masks, but they struggle to associate these proposals with specific target objects. This semantic gap severely hinders their deployment in downstream applications like robotic manipulation, which demand precise unseen objects segmentation. Existing approaches attempt to resolve this by relying on exhaustive 3D object model priors, inherently introducing prohibitive computational overhead and complex, multi-stage pipelines. To address these limitations, we propose SOS (Streamlined Object-conditional Transformer for model-free Segmentation). SOS completely eliminates the reliance on 3D models, requiring only a single reference image per target object. Central to our framework is a novel Object-Conditional Transformer that learns identity-anchored queries, unifying mask generation and target identification into a single feed-forward pass. This streamlined design drastically improves both structural and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SOS establishes a new state-of-the-art for model-free unseen objects segmentation, delivering accurate and high-efficiency performance. The project page and code are available at https://sos-seg.github.io/.

</details>


### [76] [FMReward: Aligning and Evaluating Audio-Driven 3D Facial Animation with Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15296)
*Sijing Wu,Yunhao Li,Zhilin Gao,Huiyu Duan,Yucheng Zhu,Guangtao Zhai,Patrick Le Callet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于音频驱动3D面部动画的感知质量评估与优化框架，包含首个大规模人类偏好数据集FMPair、基于偏好的奖励模型FMReward，以及利用奖励模型直接微调扩散模型的算法FMFL，从而将生成结果从依赖ground-truth误差转向与人类偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动3D面部动画方法的训练和评估都依赖于ground-truth误差指标，这些指标与人类主观偏好不一致，缺乏能反映真实感知质量的方法。

Method: 1) 构建FMPair数据集：通过系统标注流程收集65,574个3D面部运动对（来自8,834个野外音频片段）用于成对偏好比较；2) 提出FMReward奖励模型：以音频和3D面部运动为输入，预测与人类偏好一致的感知质量分数；3) 提出FMFL微调算法：利用预训练的奖励模型对基于扩散的音频驱动3D面部动画模型进行直接微调，以更好地对齐人类偏好。

Result: 大量实验表明，FMReward在与人偏好的对齐程度上优于其他评估指标，FMFL能有效提升音频驱动3D面部动画的感知质量。

Conclusion: 通过引入人类偏好数据和奖励模型，该工作实现了音频驱动3D面部动画在评估与优化上与人类感知一致的目标，为该领域提供了新的范式和基准资源。

Abstract: Audio-driven 3D facial animation is essential for advancing immersion and interactivity in virtual experiences. Although recent advances have shown promising capabilities, the training and evaluation of existing methods typically rely on ground-truth-based errors, which fall short of aligning with human preferences. To address this, we present a comprehensive framework that learns an automatic perceptual model from human preference data and leverages it to improve and evaluate the perceptual quality of audio-driven 3D facial animation. To begin with, we construct FMPair (Facial Motion Pairwise preference), the first human preference dataset for audio-driven 3D facial animation, which is built through a systematic annotation pipeline and comprises 65,574 annotated 3D facial motion pairs from 8,834 distinct in-the-wild audio clips. Based on the pairwise comparison dataset, we propose a Facial Motion Reward model, termed FMReward, which takes audio and 3D facial motion as inputs and predicts a perceptual quality score aligned with human preferences. Building upon FMReward, we further introduce Facial Motion reward Feedback Learning (FMFL), a direct fine-tuning algorithm that leverages a pretrained reward model to optimize diffusion-based audio-driven 3D facial animation models for better alignment with human preferences. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of FMReward over other metrics in aligning with human preferences and the effectiveness of FMFL in improving the perceptual quality of audio-driven 3D facial animation.

</details>


### [77] [TinyDETR-Pose: Towards End-to-End Real-Time Single-Stage 6DoF Object Pose Estimation with Lightweight Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15297)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Duc Anh Nguyen,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Michael Weinmann,Arjan Kuijper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TinyDETR-Pose是一个轻量级端到端单阶段6D物体姿态估计框架，基于LW-DETR架构，通过DETR查询头直接回归旋转、单目深度和投影中心，消除PnP/NMS/迭代优化需求，在YCB-V数据集上达到85.9 ADD-S AUC，在Jetson Nano上仅需约4.5ms每帧推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的硬件上实现实时6D姿态估计仍具挑战性：传统的基于对应关系和精化流程依赖非可微的PnP/RANSAC阶段或昂贵的迭代精化，而基于基础模型的方法推理成本过高，无法用于边缘部署。

Method: 基于LW-DETR架构构建轻量级单阶段框架；将检测与姿态估计建模为集合预测问题；为每个解码器查询附加专用MLP头，分别回归旋转、单目深度和投影物体中心；使用ADD-S损失统一处理对称性；采用基于类别和2D空间线索的对称安全匈牙利匹配器进行预测与真值分配。

Result: 在YCB-V数据集上达到85.9的ADD-S AUC，与DETR类单阶段姿态估计方法相比参数减少多达72.7%；在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上使用TensorRT实现约4.5ms每帧的实时推理延迟。

Conclusion: 证明了准确且端到端的基于Transformer的6D姿态估计可以在边缘设备上实现实用化部署，无需依赖PnP、NMS或迭代精化等传统组件。

Abstract: Real-time 6DoF object pose estimation on resource-constrained hardware remains challenging, as accurate correspondence-based and refinement pipelines typically rely on non-differentiable PnP/RANSAC stages or costly iterative refinement, while recent foundation-model-based approaches incur inference costs that are prohibitive for edge deployment. We present TinyDETR-Pose, a lightweight, end-to-end, single-stage framework that jointly detects objects and regresses their full 6D pose in a single forward pass. Built on the efficient LW-DETR architecture, TinyDETR-Pose formulates detection and pose estimation as a set-prediction problem and attaches dedicated MLP heads for rotation, monocular depth, and projected object center regression to each decoder query, eliminating the need for PnP, NMS (non-maximum suppression), or iterative pose refinement. Object symmetries are handled through a ADD-S loss applied uniformly to all objects, without the need for object-specific loss schedules or separate geodesic/ADD supervision. In addition, predictions are assigned to ground truth using a symmetry-safe Hungarian matcher based on class and 2D spatial cues, yielding stable assignment under symmetry and depth ambiguity. On YCB-V, TinyDETR-Pose achieves a comparable ADD-S AUC of 85.9, while requiring up to 72.7% fewer parameters than other DETR-based single-stage pose-estimation approaches. Due to its compact design, TinyDETR-Pose runs in real time and achieves an inference latency of only ~4.5 ms per frame on an NVIDIA Jetson Nano using TensorRT, demonstrating that accurate end-to-end transformer-based 6D pose estimation can be made practical for edge deployment.

</details>


### [78] [Image Denoising via the Adaptive Rank-Cluster Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15298)
*Dmitry Pozdnyakov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Otsu聚类和模糊融合的空间局部图像去噪滤波器，在混合噪声（椒盐脉冲噪声与高斯噪声）条件下相比七种基线算法表现出最优的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪滤波器（如中值、自适应中值、高斯、双边、维纳、各向异性扩散和非局部均值）在处理混合噪声（椒盐脉冲噪声与高斯加性噪声的组合）时鲁棒性不足，需要一种能同时应对脉冲噪声和高斯噪声的滤波器。

Method: 在3x3窗口内，将按强度排序并修剪至七个元素的像素集合通过Otsu最优阈值分为两个聚类，将中心像素强度与其中一个聚类的统计多数强度对齐，然后将计算结果与窗口内像素中值进行模糊融合。

Result: 所提出的滤波器在不同图像噪声水平下展现出最高的鲁棒性，尤其在处理不同比例混合的椒盐脉冲噪声与高斯加性噪声时表现最佳，性能优于中值、自适应中值、高斯、双边、维纳、各向异性扩散和非局部均值等基线算法。

Conclusion: 该基于Otsu聚类与模糊融合的方法在处理混合噪声图像时具有显著优势，是一种鲁棒性强的空间局部去噪滤波器。

Abstract: A spatial-local image-denoising filter is proposed, and its performance metrics are evaluated in comparison with baseline filtering algorithms, including the median, adaptive median, Gaussian, bilateral, Wiener, anisotropic diffusion, and non-local means. The developed filter is based on aligning the intensity value of the central pixel in a 3x3 window with the statistical majority intensity of one of the two clusters formed by optimal Otsu's partitioning of a pixel set sorted by intensity and trimmed to seven elements. This is followed by a fuzzy fusion of the calculated value with the median intensity of the pixels within the window. The proposed filter demonstrates the highest robustness to variations in image noise levels, particularly when processing mixed noise consisting of salt-and-pepper impulse noise and additive Gaussian noise in various proportions

</details>


### [79] [LightLoc++: Sensor-Robust Representation Learning for Efficient Outdoor LiDAR Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15317)
*Wen Li,Shangshu Yu,Dunqiang Liu,Qiming Xia,Sheng Ao,Siqi Shen,Chenglu Wen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LightLoc++，一种传感器鲁棒且高效的户外LiDAR定位框架。通过引入同步城市多LiDAR数据集SULID（32/64/128线），采用跨传感器一致性学习预训练传感器鲁棒骨干网络，并融合样本分类引导和冗余样本下采样，在多个基准上实现最优定位精度与最低的新场景训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景坐标回归（SCR）在户外LiDAR定位中性能优异，但传统方法需要数天的场景专属训练，部署成本高。现有的解耦方法（场景无关骨干+场景专属轻量头）在LiDAR配置与预训练相似时表现良好，但面对不同LiDAR传感器时精度显著下降。这表明高效的LiDAR定位需要跨传感器配置下保持稳定场景几何的表征，现有方法缺乏对此的支撑。

Method: 1) 构建SULID数据集：包含32/64/128线旋转LiDAR的同步城市多传感器数据，具有丰富的跨传感器重叠和多样城市场景；2) 利用SULID通过跨传感器一致性学习预训练传感器鲁棒的骨干网络；3) 引入样本分类引导（降低回归模糊性）和冗余样本下采样（减少大规模户外场景的计算冗余）；4) 保持解耦框架的高效性：冻结预训练骨干，仅优化轻量预测头。

Result: 在多个户外LiDAR定位基准上，LightLoc++相比现有方法取得最先进的定位精度，同时拥有最低的新场景训练成本。

Conclusion: LightLoc++证明了通过跨传感器一致性学习预训练传感器鲁棒表征，可以有效解决解耦SCR范式对预训练骨干过度依赖的问题，同时保持高效的新场景适应能力，为户外LiDAR定位的实际部署提供了可行的方案。代码和数据集将开源。

Abstract: Scene coordinate regression (SCR) achieves strong performance in outdoor LiDAR localization, but it usually requires scene-specific training that can take days, limiting practical deployment. Recent works improve training efficiency by decoupling SCR into a scene-agnostic backbone and scene-specific prediction heads, where the backbone is pretrained on source datasets and frozen for new scenes, and only lightweight heads are optimized. However, we find that this paradigm heavily depends on the pretrained backbone. Existing decoupled methods can match conventional SCR methods fully optimized for each new scene when LiDAR configurations are similar to those used during backbone pretraining, but their accuracy drops noticeably on datasets collected with different LiDAR sensors. This suggests that efficient LiDAR localization requires representations that capture stable scene geometry across LiDAR configurations. Motivated by this observation, we propose LightLoc++, a sensor-robust and efficient outdoor LiDAR localization framework. To support sensor-robust representation learning, we introduce SULID, a synchronized urban multi-LiDAR dataset with representative 32-, 64-, and 128-beam rotating LiDARs, extensive cross-sensor overlap, and diverse urban scenes. Using SULID, we pretrain a sensor-robust backbone through cross-sensor consistency learning. LightLoc++ further preserves efficient new-scene learning by incorporating sample classification guidance and redundant sample downsampling, which reduce regression ambiguity and computational redundancy in large-scale outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on multiple outdoor LiDAR localization benchmarks demonstrate that LightLoc++ achieves state-of-the-art localization performance with the lowest new-scene training cost among compared methods. Code and dataset will be made available at https://github.com/liw95/LightLoc-PlusPlus.

</details>


### [80] [SAGE-OR: Semi-supervised Adaptive Scene Graph Generation for Operating Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15336)
*Brandon Leblanc,Charalambos Poullis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE-OR提出了一种面向手术场景场景图生成的特征中心框架，用解耦的表示-推理范式替代传统的检测-推理范式，利用冻结的基础模型进行定位（无需定位监督），配合轻量图Transformer进行关系推理。在4D-OR基准上达到76% F1（匹配全监督基线），通过无监督手部增强提升至86%（接近SOTA），整体模型仅1500万参数、训练1.4小时、推理约1ms/帧，适合手术室边缘硬件部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术场景图生成方法依赖稠密多模态监督和专用硬件（同步RGB-D传感器、校准装置），导致数据集构建昂贵，且所有现有基准都局限于模拟环境。这严重阻碍了该领域向真实手术场景的实际应用迁移。作者希望消除定位标注需求，降低对专用硬件的要求，使方法能够便捷地适应新的手术环境。

Method: 核心思想是将定位与推理解耦：1）定位能力来自冻结的基础模型，编码在预计算的特征中，无需定位监督；2）使用轻量级图Transformer在缓存特征上进行关系推理；3）采用半监督配方，用通用分割提示消除定位监督，同时通过额外的提示驱动实体（如手）实现无监督上下文增强；4）通用提示用于诱导近完美召回，精度交由下游基于注意力的推理处理，通过提示级修改即可适应新实体。特征提取作为离线缓存阶段运行（4.27秒/帧）。

Result: 在4D-OR基准上，核心模型达到76% F1，与需要完全监督的4D-OR基线持平，同时消除了所有定位标注；无监督手部增强将F1提升至86%，与需要稠密多模态监督的SOTA方法仅差4个点。模型仅有1500万参数，训练耗时1.4小时，推理约1ms/帧，峰值内存低于2GB，适合手术室边缘硬件。

Conclusion: SAGE-OR展示了无需定位标注即可实现与全监督方法相当性能的可行性，为手术场景图生成提供了一条无需额外标注（仅需关系和类别标签）即可适应新手术环境的实用路径，显著降低了数据构建成本和硬件门槛。

Abstract: Current surgical scene graph generation methods depend on dense multi-modal supervision and specialized hardware (synchronized RGB-D sensors, calibration rigs), making dataset construction expensive and restricting all existing benchmarks to simulated environments. We propose SAGE-OR, a feature-centric framework that replaces the traditional detect-then-reason paradigm with a decoupled representation-reasoning paradigm in which localization is derived from frozen foundation models, encoded implicitly in pre-computed features, and used without any localization supervision, while a lightweight graph transformer performs relational reasoning over cached features. We employ a semi-supervised formulation with general-purpose segmentation prompts to eliminate localization supervision while enabling unsupervised context augmentation through additional prompt-driven entities, such as hands, which are absent from annotations. General-purpose prompts are used to induce near-perfect recall, while precision is delegated to downstream attention-based reasoning, enabling simple adaptation to new entities via prompt-level modification. This design enables a lightweight 15M-parameter graph transformer that trains in 1.4 hours and runs relational inference at $\sim$1ms per frame with peak memory under 2GB, suitable for edge hardware used in the operating room; feature extraction runs offline as a separate caching stage (4.27s per frame). On the 4D-OR benchmark, the core model achieves 76% F1, matching the fully supervised 4D-OR baseline while eliminating all localization annotations, and unsupervised hand augmentation raises this to 86%, within 4 points of state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods requiring dense multi-modal supervision, providing a practical pathway for adaptation to new surgical settings without annotation other than relationship and class labels.

</details>


### [81] [TEA: Text Encoder Alignment for Robust Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15341)
*Alireza Dehghanpour Farashah,Zhuan Shi,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TEA是一个轻量级文本编码器对齐框架，通过将概念擦除建模为文本表示空间中的域对齐问题，仅微调文本编码器并冻结生成骨干网络，实现了零推理开销、少量微调步骤下的最先进概念擦除鲁棒性，同时保持良性提示的生成质量，并可扩展到Stable Diffusion v3.5等新架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型可能被对抗性或改写后的提示词绕过安全机制从而生成有害内容。现有概念擦除方法存在三类局限：对对抗提示的鲁棒性有限、退化良性生成质量、或依赖推理时的干预带来持续计算开销。本文旨在同时解决这些问题。

Method: 将概念擦除形式化为文本表示空间中的域对齐问题。提出TEA框架，仅微调文本编码器同时完全冻结生成骨干网络。给定概念-锚点提示对，训练一个判别器区分概念提示与安全锚点提示的token级表示，同时更新文本编码器使这些表示不可区分（对抗式训练）。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4上，TEA在黑盒和白盒对抗攻击下取得了最先进的擦除鲁棒性，同时保持良性提示的生成质量。此外，TEA是模型无关的，在Stable Diffusion v3.5上实现了最低的攻击成功率，将该方法扩展到基于Rectified Flow Transformer架构和T5条件化模型，这是先前方法尚未探索的领域。

Conclusion: TEA通过仅微调文本编码器的域对齐方法，实现了零推理开销、高效部署、最先进的对抗鲁棒性和模型无关性，为概念擦除提供了一种实用的、可扩展的解决方案，即使在面对对抗性攻击时也能有效防止有害内容生成。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can be misused to generate harmful content through adversarial or paraphrased prompts that bypass built-in safety mechanisms. Existing concept erasure methods often suffer from limited robustness against adversarial prompts, degradation of benign generation quality, or reliance on inference-time interventions that introduce persistent computational overhead. To address these limitations, we formulate concept erasure as a domain alignment problem in the text representation space. We propose a lightweight Text Encoder Alignment framework (TEA) that fine-tunes only the text encoder while keeping the generative backbone fully frozen. Given concept--anchor prompt pairs, our method trains a discriminator to distinguish token-level representations of concept-containing prompts from those of safe anchor prompts, while updating the text encoder to make these representations indistinguishable. TEA introduces zero inference-time overhead and requires only a small number of fine-tuning steps, making it highly efficient to deploy at scale. Despite this efficiency, TEA achieves state-of-the-art erasure robustness against black-box and white-box adversarial attacks on Stable Diffusion v1.4, while preserving generation quality on benign prompts. Furthermore, TEA is model-agnostic and achieves the lowest attack success rate on Stable Diffusion v3.5, extending concept erasure to a Rectified Flow Transformer architecture with T5 conditioning where prior methods remain largely unexplored. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/alirezafarashah/TEA.git}{https://github.com/alirezafarashah/TEA.git}

</details>


### [82] [Feed-Forward Hierarchical Gaussian Diffusion for Extreme CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15343)
*Yuezhe Yang,Li Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HiGDiff，一种前馈分层高斯扩散框架，用于从严重受限的投影中重建三维CT图像。该框架在空间上和从结构到细节两个维度对重建任务进行分解：先通过结构扩散阶段恢复全局衰减几何，再用其表示条件化细节扩散阶段恢复残差边界和组织过渡，最终通过梯度隔离残差模块进一步精化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏角度采样、受限角度覆盖和低光子计数等退化可能单独或联合出现，导致CT重建高度病态。现有的学习方法大多针对单一主导退化设计，而扩散和高斯方法通常在共享表征中同时恢复全局结构和局部细节，难以在多种退化组合下获得最优性能。

Method: HiGDiff包含几个关键组件：1) 物理条件化的解剖锚点和前景容量场，用于将可学习高斯原语分配到信息丰富区域；2) 结构扩散阶段，先恢复全局衰减几何；3) 细节扩散阶段，以结构阶段的表征为条件，恢复残差边界和组织过渡；4) 梯度隔离残差模块，将高斯库渲染为衰减场并进一步精化。

Result: 在三个不同的CT基准数据集上实现了最先进的重建性能，涵盖孤立、成对和联合退化设置。在LDCT-PD数据集上，宏平均PSNR提升5.81 dB，SSIM提升0.113。代码已开源。

Conclusion: HiGDiff通过空间分解和从结构到细节的分层扩散设计，有效解决了多种退化单独或联合出现时的CT重建问题，验证了分层表征在应对复杂退化场景中的优势。

Abstract: Reconstructing three-dimensional computed tomography (CT) from severely constrained projections is highly ill-posed. Sparse angular sampling, restricted angular coverage, and low photon counts can occur individually or jointly, obscuring global anatomy and local tissue detail. Many learned CT reconstruction methods are tailored to a single dominant degradation. Existing diffusion and Gaussian approaches commonly recover global structure and local detail within a shared representation. We propose HiGDiff, a feed-forward hierarchical Gaussian diffusion framework that decomposes reconstruction both spatially and from structure to detail. Physics-conditioned anatomical anchors and a foreground capacity field allocate learnable Gaussian primitives to informative regions. A structure diffusion stage first recovers global attenuation geometry, and its learned representation conditions a detail diffusion stage for residual boundaries and tissue transitions. The resulting Gaussian banks are rendered as attenuation fields and further refined by a gradient-isolated residual module. Experiments on three distinct CT benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art reconstruction performance across isolated, paired, and joint degradation settings, including improvements of 5.81 dB in macro-average peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and 0.113 in structural similarity index measure (SSIM) on the Low Dose CT Image and Projection Data (LDCT-PD) collection. Code and experimental configurations are openly available at https://github.com/Bean-Young/HiGDiff.

</details>


### [83] [ENAF: A Multi-Exit Network with an Adaptive Patch Fusion for Large Image Super Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15349)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Tuan Nghia Nguyen,Xuan Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ENAF是一个用于单图像超分辨率的动态网络，通过多个早期退出和自适应补丁融合机制，利用小型网络估计PSNR来关联图像纹理与计算成本，从而动态地为图像补丁分配执行路径，在大型图像（2K-8K）上实现了质量与计算复杂度的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态SISR网络在决定补丁执行路径时主要依赖手工设计的评估分数（如边缘检测），这些分数与SISR模型的计算复杂度关联较弱，无法准确反映补丁纹理与计算需求之间的关系，导致质量-复杂度权衡不佳。

Method: ENAF基于骨干网络构建，融入多个早期退出机制来处理过参数化的SISR模型。关键是嵌入了一个微型网络用于估计PSNR，将数据纹理与早期退出处的计算成本相关联。根据估计的分数，ENAF有效地将图像补丁分配到适当的退出位置，实现自适应补丁融合。

Result: 在多个常见数据集上使用流行的SISR骨干网络进行广泛实验，验证了ENAF在多种设置下的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: ENAF通过引入可学习的PSNR估计器替代手工设计分数，有效提升了动态SISR网络的质量-复杂度权衡，为大规模图像超分辨率提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: To accelerate single image super-resolution (SISR) networks on large images (2K-8K), many recent approaches decompose an image into small patches and dynamically determine an execution path according to its difficulty (referred to as a dynamic network). To quantify the hardness of a patch, they mainly rely on a handcrafted assessment score, e.g., edge, which weakly associates a patch's texture with the computational complexity of a SISR model. To address the problem, we introduce ENAF - a dynamic network for SISR with an adaptive patch fusion. Built on top of a backbone, ENAF incorporates multiple early exits (EEs) to tackle the over-parameterized SISR model. More importantly, ENAF plugs a tiny network that estimates PSNR to associate data texture with a computation cost at an EE. Based on the scores, ENAF effectively assigns image patches to an exit, enhancing the quality-complexity trade-off. Extensive experiments on common datasets with popular SISR backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of ENAF in various settings. The source code is provided in https://github.com/nmduonggg/ENAF

</details>


### [84] [Decomposing Whole Slide Image Report Generation with Graph-Constrained Multiple Instance Learning Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15353)
*Antony Gitau,Martyna Borak,Bjørn-Jostein Singstad,Martin Paulson,Karl Thomas Hjelmervik,Ola Marius Lysaker,Veralia Gabriela Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分解式的全切片图像（WSI）报告生成框架：使用冻结的Virchow2 tile嵌入，通过多实例学习（MIL）分类头回答器官特异性诊断问题，再由器官条件图约束答案组装成结构化推理链，最后由语言模型生成病理报告。在REG2026数据集上，链式Jaccard得分达0.702，且各组件（图约束、器官特异性、链构建方式）均对性能有显著贡献；在外部TCGA数据上展现了良好的泛化性，并揭示了器官路由是域迁移下的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接视觉-文本映射的WSI报告生成模型虽能生成流畅报告，但掩盖了视觉识别、结构化推理和语言生成各自的作用与失败模式。作者希望通过分解框架实现各阶段的错误定位与可控推理。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 冻结的Virchow2 tile嵌入经MIL分类头回答器官特异性诊断问题；(2) 器官条件图约束答案组装成结构化推理链；(3) 语言模型将推理链实现为病理报告。每个组件均可独立评估和替换，支持消融分析。

Result: REG2026测试集（2,028张切片）上链式Jaccard得分0.702；移除图约束后降至0.420，用器官无关图替代降至0.398，语言模型自由构建链降至0.371。报告得分从0.330提升到0.495。外部700+TCGA数据上，预期器官图选择率64.0%（top-3达86.6%），正确器官图将诊断一致性从61.8%提升至92.6%。

Conclusion: 器官条件、图约束的链式组装方法能显著提升结构化推理和报告生成质量，同时支持分阶段错误定位。器官路由被识别为域迁移下的主要瓶颈，为未来系统优化指明了方向。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) report generation requires recognizing spatially distributed pathological features and organizing them into a coherent diagnostic narrative. Although direct vision-to-text models can yield fluent reports, they obscure the contributions and failure modes of visual recognition, structured reasoning, and language generation. We propose a decomposed framework in which frozen Virchow2 tile embeddings are aggregated by multiple-instance learning (MIL) classification heads that answer organ-specific diagnostic questions. An organ-conditioned graph constrains the assembly of these answers into a structured reasoning chain, which a language model realizes as a pathology report. On the REG2026 held-out set of 2,028 slides, the proposed workflow achieved a chain-Jaccard score of 0.702. Performance fell to 0.420 without graph-based chain construction, 0.398 when the organ-specific graphs were replaced by a single organ-agnostic graph, and 0.371 when the language model constructed the chain freely from MIL predictions. Using the same report generator, graph-structured chains improved the report score from 0.330 to 0.495. On 350 external TCGA WSIs spanning the seven REG organs without fine-tuning, the expected organ graph was selected in 64.0% of cases and ranked among the top three in 86.6%. Providing the correct organ graph increased agreement with coarse TCGA primary-diagnosis labels from 61.8% to 92.6%, identifying organ routing as a main bottleneck under domain shift. Overall, organ-conditioned, graph-constrained chain assembly improves structured reasoning and report generation while enabling stage-specific error localization.

</details>


### [85] [A Multi-Annotator Study of Segmentation Noise and Uncertainty in Turbid Underwater Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15363)
*Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Vasiliki Ismiroglou,Malte Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了真实水下场景中分割任务的标注不确定性，涉及100多名参与者，并在不同受控浊度水平下进行。研究发现水下数据集面临与其他视觉任务相似的标注挑战，且浊度会引入额外的系统性误差，并进一步探讨了改善浑浊水下环境中标注质量的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注不确定性和标注者分歧是计算机视觉中的常见挑战，但现有研究主要集中在医学领域或通用图像识别数据集。水下数据集因需要领域专业知识、能见度降低以及难以在不可达环境中建立可靠真实标注而特别容易受到这些问题的影响，但水下图像中的标注不确定性问题在很大程度上仍未被探索。

Method: 进行了首次针对真实水下场景分割的多标注者系统研究，招募超过100名参与者，在受控的多种浊度水平下进行标注实验。研究分析了水下数据集面临的标注挑战，考察浊度带来的系统性误差，并探索了特权信息、个体努力和标注者集成等改进标注质量的方法。

Result: 研究表明水下数据集面临与其他视觉任务相同的许多标注挑战，同时浊度会引入额外的系统性标注误差。研究还确定了驱动标注噪声的主要因素，并展示了改善浑浊水下环境中标注质量的可行方法。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了水下图像标注不确定性研究的空白，提供了首个系统的多标注者水下分割标注数据集，所有（元）数据将公开在项目页面上，为后续水下视觉研究和标注方法改进奠定基础。

Abstract: Label uncertainty and annotator disagreement are common challenges in the field of computer vision, yet their study has largely been confined to the medical domain or to generic image-recognition datasets. Underwater datasets are particularly susceptible to these issues due to the need for domain expertise, degraded visibility conditions, and the inherent difficulty of establishing reliable ground truth in inaccessible environments. Despite these challenges, annotation uncertainty in underwater imagery remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first systematic multi-annotator study of segmentation in real underwater scenes, with over 100 participants, and across varying, controlled levels of turbidity. We show that underwater datasets face many of the same annotation challenges as other vision tasks, while turbidity introduces additional systematic errors. We further investigate the main factors driving label noise and explore ways to improve annotation quality in turbid underwater environments, including privileged information, individual effort and annotator ensembles. All (meta-) data collected in this study will be available on the project page: https://vap.aau.dk/tubcertainty

</details>


### [86] [JoLT: Joint Latent Trajectories for Context-Guided High-Resolution Tiled Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15395)
*Mathis Koroglu,Guillaume Jeanneret,Hugo Caselles-Dupré,Matthieu Cord,Arnaud Dapogny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JoLT（Joint Latent Trajectories）方法，通过双流联合去噪机制同时生成低分辨率和高分辨率潜变量图像，以解决文本生成图像模型难以生成高细节高分辨率图像的问题。低分辨率潜变量控制整体布局，高分辨率潜变量控制细节，两分支相互连接以整合信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型虽然效果出色，但难以生成细节丰富的高分辨率图像。当前文献采用从低分辨率到高分辨率的渐进式方法，先生成低分辨率图像，再加入超分辨率过程生成高分辨率版本，但这些方法存在信息割裂和细节丢失的问题。

Method: JoLT采用双流架构，在每一步采样过程中联合去噪低分辨率和高分辨率的潜变量图像。低分辨率流控制整体布局结构，高分辨率流控制细节纹理。两个分支之间通过信息互连实现联合信息整合，使布局与细节在同一采样过程中协同演化。

Result: 通过大量实验验证，JoLT在生成质量上优于现有基线方法。生成的图像不仅细节丰富，而且在视觉上令人愉悦，表现优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: JoLT开辟了高分辨率文本到图像生成的新方向，为艺术创作等应用提供了新的可能性。其联合去噪框架证明了同时优化布局与细节的有效性。

Abstract: Although text-to-image generative models produce impressive results, they struggle to generate densely detailed, high-resolution (HR) images. Current literature addresses this issue with a low-to-high-resolution approach. First, a low-resolution (LR) image is generated. Then, an upsampled version is generated using the LR image as an additional cue. In this paper, we present Joint Latent Trajectories (JoLT). To generate an image, JoLT uses two streams that jointly denoise LR and HR latent images at each sampling step. The LR latent controls the overall layout, while the HR latent controls the details. We interconnect both branches to jointly integrate their information. We extensively validate our method, demonstrating its advantages over competing baselines. The resulting images are not only richly detailed but also visually pleasing, opening new avenues for artistic creation.

</details>


### [87] [CBX-Bench: A Human-Aligned MLLM Council for Benchmarking Concept Bottleneck Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15404)
*Yusuf Meric Karadag,Gulay Oklan,Seref Baris Cagliyan,Umut Ozdemir,Emre Akbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CBX-Bench，一个基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）委员会来量化评估概念瓶颈模型（CBM）解释质量的基准和排行榜。通过人类研究建立解释质量的地面真值参考，验证了五个开源MLLM组成的委员会能够恢复超过70%的人类偏好排名（在人类一致同意的项目上达83%），从而为CBM提供超越准确率和孤立定性示例的、人类对齐且可扩展的解释评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的核心动机是可解释性，但其评估仍主要依赖下游分类准确率，辅以孤立的定性示例。现有的量化评估方法面临两大挑战：大规模地面真值概念标注不可行，以及概念列表缺乏共识导致开放性。因此迫切需要一种可扩展、人类对齐的量化解释质量评估方法。

Method: 1) 设计一个由五个开源权重MLLM组成的委员会，给定图像及其CBM解释，生成解释质量评分。2) 进行人工研究建立地面真值参考：在CUB-200、ImageNet-100和Places365三个数据集上，对900个图像比较项目收集2700个判断，让标注者比较LF-CBM、VLG-CBM和CBM-Suite中两个解释的优劣。3) 验证委员会的评分与人类判断的一致性。4) 基于验证通过的委员会推出CBX-Bench公共基准和排行榜平台。

Result: 五模型MLLM委员会能恢复超过70%的严格人类偏好排名，在人类标注者一致同意的项目上准确率达83%。在三个数据集（CUB-200、ImageNet-100、Places365）上完成了900个图像比较项目的2700个人类判断，作为地面真值参考。

Conclusion: CBX-Bench提供了首个人类对齐、可扩展的CBM解释质量量化评估框架，填补了CBM评估仅依赖准确率和定性示例的空白。该基准和排行榜允许新CBM的作者提交解释并获得基于MLLM委员会的质量评分，从而推动CBM在可解释性方面的真正进展。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) are designed to make visual classification interpretable by expressing predictions through human-understandable concepts. Although interpretability is the central motivation for CBMs, they are still largely evaluated as predictive models by downstream classification accuracy, supplemented by isolated qualitative examples. This highlights a pressing need for quantitative measures, a challenge complicated by the infeasibility of ground-truth concept annotation at scale and the open nature of concept lists due to a lack of consensus. To fill this gap, we develop a multimodal large language model (MLLM) council that, given an image and its CBM explanation, produces an explanation quality score. To ground and validate the council, we first conduct a human study to establish a ground-truth reference for CBM explanation quality: for an image, annotators compare explanations from two of LF-CBM, VLG-CBM, and CBM-Suite and choose the more useful one, or mark them as equally good or equally bad, yielding 2700 judgments over 900 image-comparison items on CUB-200, ImageNet-100, and Places365. Against this human reference, our five-model council, consisting of open-weight MLLMs, recovers over 70% of strict human preference rankings, rising to 83% on items where human annotators unanimously agree. Building on this validated council, we introduce CBX-Bench, a public benchmark and leaderboard: authors of new CBMs can submit their model's explanations, and CBX-Bench scores them with the council and maintains dataset-level rankings of explanation quality. CBX-Bench thus provides a human-aligned, scalable evaluation of CBM explanations beyond accuracy and isolated qualitative examples. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/meric-karadag/cbx-bench.

</details>


### [88] [ArtLang: Structured Language-to-Kinematics Grounding for Articulated 3D Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15419)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtLang框架，将重建的铰接物体资产表示为语义-运动学铰接图，通过开放词汇语言指令实现对物体的连续运动控制。系统通过图到图的全局匹配解决语义、空间、关系和运动学兼容性问题，支持不确定部分的空赋值和歧义弃权，最后将指令转换为关节目标并通过正运动学执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的铰接物体重建虽然能恢复显式几何和运动学，但部件通常是语义匿名的，只能通过部件索引和数值关节参数来控制，缺乏自然语言控制能力。

Method: ArtLang包含三个核心组件：(1) 语义-运动学铰接图表示，将重建资产表示为图结构并增强表面语言特征和运动学约束；(2) 类型化解析器，将自然语言命令转换为包含指代表达、动作、幅度、参考系和关系的指令图；(3) 全局图到图接地求解器，联合推理语义、空间、关系和运动学兼容性，支持空赋值和歧义条件下的弃权处理，最终将指令转换为连续关节目标并通过正运动学执行。

Result: 在合成重建、基于网格的资产和真实捕捉数据上的实验表明，ArtLang在重复部件、空间指代、关系命令和歧义指令场景下均实现了可靠的语言接地和连续铰接控制。

Conclusion: ArtLang成功弥合了自然语言与重建铰接物体控制之间的鸿沟，通过语义-运动学图的联合推理实现了开放词汇下的鲁棒语言控制，并能妥善处理歧义和不确定性。

Abstract: Articulated-object reconstructions recover explicit geometry and kinematics, but their parts often remain semantically anonymous and must be controlled through part indices and numerical joint parameters. We present ArtLang, a framework for open-vocabulary language control of persistent reconstructed articulated assets. ArtLang represents an asset as a semantic-kinematic articulation graph and augments its surface with language features and graph-constrained motion. Open-vocabulary proposals are bound to reconstructed parts while allowing uncertain parts to remain unnamed. A typed parser converts a command into a directive graph containing referring expressions, actions, magnitudes, reference frames, and relations. We then solve a global graph-to-graph grounding problem that jointly reasons about semantic, spatial, relational, and kinematic compatibility, with support for null assignments and abstention under ambiguity. Accepted directives are converted into continuous joint targets within the observed motion range and executed through forward kinematics. Experiments on synthetic reconstructions, mesh-based assets, and real captures demonstrate reliable language grounding and continuous articulated control across repeated parts, spatial references, relational commands, and ambiguous instructions.

</details>


### [89] [HistReNeRF: Historic Image Relocalisation within Contemporary Neural Radiance Field Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15420)
*Benjamin T. Hughes,Stuart James*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HistReNeRF框架，通过将适应后的DINOv2补丁特征与从现代NeRF重建中采样的候选光线进行匹配，估计历史照片的6自由度位姿，实现跨时代的场景重定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史照片与现代场景模型之间的重定位极具挑战性，因为历史与现代视图在摄影外观、可见物体和空间布局上存在显著差异。

Method: 1) 利用NeRF的连续表示提供可查询的场景接口，从中采样候选光线；2) 将适应后的DINOv2补丁特征与候选光线进行匹配；3) 在用于定位的特征表示中直接进行历史摄影与现代图像之间的域适应。

Result: 在包含10,545张现代街景图像和230张历史照片的三个欧洲地标跨时间数据集中，嵌入空间域适应相比像素空间方法平均降低了11%的平移误差和16%的旋转误差。

Conclusion: 神经场景重定位为特征空间适应提供了自然接口，无需修改查询图像即可减少跨时间外观偏移，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Relocalising archival photographs within a contemporary scene model is challenging because historic and modern views can differ in photographic appearance, visible objects, and spatial layout. Therefore, we present HistReNeRF, a framework that estimates the 6-DoF pose of a historic photograph by matching adapted DINOv2 patch features to candidate rays sampled from a contemporary Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) reconstruction. The continuous representation of a NeRF provides a queryable scene interface from which candidate rays can be sampled and matched, enabling domain adaptation between historic photography and contemporary images directly in the feature representation used for localisation. We evaluate embedding-space-based domain adaptation against pixel-space methods on a new cross-temporal dataset comprising 10,545 contemporary street-level images and 230 archival photographs from three European landmarks. Embedding-space adaptation reduces translation and rotation errors by an average of 11% and 16%, respectively, across the three scenes. These results show that neural scene relocalisation provides a natural interface for feature-space adaptation, reducing cross-temporal appearance shift without modifying the query image. Code and dataset at https://github.com/ARTUROLab/HistReNeRF.

</details>


### [90] [NumerosityVLM: A Cognitively Inspired Benchmark for Interpreting Numerosity Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15425)
*Yiming Fu,Fangjun Li,Xiujin Liu,Ruidong Ma,Hang Yu,Zhichen Lu,Kanwei He,Alessandro Di Nuovo,Angelo Cangelosi,Zhegong Shangguan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出认知启发的诊断基准 NumerosityVLM，包含10,800张合成图像、六种受控条件，正交操纵物体大小、空间排列与数量，并逐步消融纹理、形状和颜色。对七个VLM的零样本评估显示，模型架构是性能差异的最大来源，且数量感知信号在视觉编码器早期阶段就已线性可分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计数基准将数量感知与相关的视觉因素纠缠在一起，导致我们无法准确理解VLM中数量感知（numerosity perception）这一人类婴儿在语言习得前就具备的认知能力。

Method: 构建包含10,800张合成图像、六种受控条件的诊断基准NumerosityVLM，正交操纵物体大小、空间排列和数量，同时逐步消融纹理、形状和颜色；对七个VLM进行零样本评估，采用多因素分析和逐层探测（layer-wise probing）方法。

Result: 多因素分析显示模型架构解释了最大的性能方差比例（partial ω²=0.325），远超视觉条件；逐层探测显示数量信号在视觉编码器早期阶段即线性可分，而模型间的性能差异主要与语言模型组件相关。

Conclusion: 当前VLM的数量感知能力受架构影响远大于视觉条件设计；数量信号虽在视觉编码器早期就已出现，但最终性能差异主要由语言模型部分决定，这为改进VLM的数量感知能力提供了针对性方向。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on high-level multimodal tasks, yet numerosity perception, a cognitive ability that emerges in human infants before language acquisition, remains poorly understood in current models, as existing counting benchmarks entangle numerosity with correlated visual factors. We introduce a cognitively inspired diagnostic benchmark, NumerosityVLM, comprising 10,800 synthetic images across six controlled conditions. The benchmark orthogonally manipulates object size, spatial arrangement, and numerosity, while progressively ablating texture, shape, and color. Evaluating seven VLMs in a zero-shot setting, multi-factor analysis reveals that model architecture explains the largest proportion of performance variance (partial $ω^{2}=0.325$), far exceeding visual conditions. Layer-wise probing further shows that linearly separable numerosity signals consistently emerge at early stages of the vision encoder, while performance differences across evaluated models are primarily associated with the language model component. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/fuy3/NumerosityVLM-Benchmark, and https://huggingface.co/datasets/fuy3/NumerosityVLM.

</details>


### [91] [Spatially-Grounded Flow Matching: Structured Source Distributions for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15452)
*Arman Zarei,Mahdi M. Kalayeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StructFlow提出将空间局部性直接编码到流匹配模型的源噪声分布中，通过让局部像素共享噪声分量，使传输路径与图像区域几何对齐，从而实现精细局部编辑、结构保持和语义插值，并可通过轻量级后训练应用到预训练大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型使用i.i.d.高斯噪声作为源分布，但该分布缺乏空间结构信息，与图像天然的局部相关性（邻近像素强相关）不匹配。这种设计差异迫使模型在学习噪声邻居作为上下文时绕过了对图像真实局部结构的学习，削弱了模型的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出StructFlow架构，将源分布从i.i.d.高斯噪声改为结构化噪声——小区域内像素共享同一噪声分量，从而编码空间局部性。这使得传输路径与图像区域几何对齐，且可通过轻量级后训练阶段整合进预训练大模型。

Result: 在无条件、类别条件和文本条件等多种设置下，使用不同diffusion transformer架构在多个数据集上验证，StructFlow不仅保持竞争力的图像生成质量，还显著改善了局部可控的再合成能力。

Conclusion: 通过在源分布中编码空间局部性，StructFlow弥合了源分布与图像域归纳偏置之间的差距，为流匹配模型赋予了细粒度局部编辑、结构保持和语义插值等原本难以实现的能力，且对预训练模型具有兼容性。

Abstract: Current flow matching models learn to transport the source i.i.d. Gaussian noise into the target distribution of natural images, yet this source distribution carries no notion of spatial structure. Images however are fundamentally local since nearby pixels are strongly correlated. By sampling the noise independently, we hypothesize that models are implicitly encouraged to exploit less noisy neighbors as context during training, partially bypassing the need to properly learn the true local structure of images. The source distribution, in other words, works against the inductive bias of the image domain. To ameliorate this design discrepancy, we propose StructFlow which encodes spatial locality directly into the source by having the pixels within a small region share a common noise component. This structured source produces transport paths that are geometrically aligned with image regions - enabling properties that generic flow matching struggles to provide: fine-grained local editing that naturally respects boundaries, robust structure preservation, and smooth semantic interpolation between images. We show that these benefits also extend to large pre-trained models, demonstrating that StructFlow can even be incorporated through a lightweight post-training phase. Comprehensive experiments on multiple datasets, in unconditional, class and text-conditioned regimes, using different diffusion transformer architectures confirm that StructFlow not only offers competitive image generation quality, but also significantly improves localized controllable re-synthesis.

</details>


### [92] [AlignJEPA: Predictive Vision-Language Alignment for Remote Sensing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15456)
*Md Aminur Hossain,Omkumar Vaghasiya,Rajeev Ranjan Dwivedi,Vinod Kurmi,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignJEPA是一个基于JEPA思想的遥感基础模型视觉-语言对齐框架，仅训练轻量级预测对齐网络，通过预测掩码视觉token对应的文本嵌入来实现遥感图像与自然语言的对齐，参数高效且无需重新训练庞大的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基础模型虽然在传感器、分辨率和地理范围上具有很强的可迁移性，但大多数模型与自然语言的弱对齐限制了其在自然语言档案检索、图像-文本检索和基于问题分析等下游任务中的应用。

Method: AlignJEPA使用预训练的AnySat视觉编码器和RemoteCLIP文本编码器，仅训练轻量级预测对齐网络。其核心是掩码感知的多尺度预测对齐器，从细粒度、区域和全局三个尺度聚合可见token，通过跨尺度Transformer联合建模，并利用学习查询池化将表示投影到文本空间。训练结合语义预测和双向对比检索。

Result: 在BigEarthNet.txt上训练和评估自然语言Sentinel检索，在RSICD上评估跨数据集适应能力，并使用RSVQA作为闭集表示探针进行评估。

Conclusion: AlignJEPA提供了一种参数高效的方式，将地球观测基础模型与语言进行对齐，为遥感领域的视觉-语言理解提供了可行的路线。

Abstract: Remote sensing (RS) foundation models provide transferable Earth observation representations across sensors, resolutions, and geographies, yet most remain weakly aligned with natural language, limiting natural-language archive search, image-text retrieval, and question-conditioned analysis. We propose AlignJEPA, a JEPA-inspired predictive vision-language alignment framework for remote sensing foundation models. AlignJEPA uses a pretrained AnySat visual encoder and a RemoteCLIP text encoder while training only a lightweight predictive alignment network. Instead of relying on global image--text contrastive alignment alone, the framework predicts remote-sensing text embeddings from masked visual foundation-model tokens. Its mask-aware multi-scale predictive aligner aggregates visible tokens at fine, regional, and global scales, jointly models them with a cross-scale Transformer, and projects the resulting representation into the text space using learned query pooling. Training combines semantic prediction with bidirectional contrastive retrieval. We train and evaluate AlignJEPA on BigEarthNet.txt for natural-language Sentinel retrieval, evaluate cross-dataset adaptation on RSICD, and use RSVQA only as a closed-set representation probe. AlignJEPA provides a parameter-efficient route for aligning Earth observation foundation models with language.

</details>


### [93] [GLaQ: Grounding Latent Queries in Visual Evidence for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15517)
*Zesheng Yang,Lingling Zhang,Xinyu Zhang,Cheng Zhang,Pengyu Li,Heng Wang,Lin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLaQ提出了一种基于grounded latent-query的框架，用固定数量的上下文条件查询替代自回归的连续潜在推理，这些查询直接锚定在原始视觉token上并重新注入用于答案生成，从而在不依赖外部工具或自回归潜在展开的情况下恢复细粒度视觉证据。在五个基准上，GLaQ-7B相比基础模型提升5.99-9.66%，并优于所有对比的视觉潜在方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通过链式思维推理提升了问题解决能力，但细粒度视觉证据在基于文本的推理步骤中难以保留和复用。现有方法要么依赖外部工具（需预定义工具和额外推理开销），要么使用连续视觉潜在推理（但自回归构造导致后序潜在状态重复已有信息而非补充互补的视觉细节）。

Method: GLaQ用固定数量的、基于上下文条件化且锚定在原始视觉token上的查询（grounded queries）替代顺序的潜在推理展开。这些查询在生成答案时被重新注入，提供对源视觉证据的直接和协调访问。训练采用局部视图监督（localized-view supervision）之后接任务级奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在五个细粒度视觉理解与感知基准上，GLaQ-7B相比其基础模型获得5.99-9.66%的提升，并且领先所有对比的视觉潜在推理方法。

Conclusion: 直接查询到图像的锚定（query-to-image grounding）能够在不依赖外部视觉操作或自回归潜在展开的情况下，从完整图像中恢复局部证据，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving capabilities of multimodal large language models. Fine-grained visual evidence, however, remains difficult to preserve and reuse across text-based reasoning steps. To address this limitation, tool-augmented thinking-with-images methods maintain visual access externally by revisiting or manipulating the image, but require predefined tools and additional inference-time processing. As an internal alternative, continuous visual latent reasoning retains intermediate computation in hidden states. However, its prevailing autoregressive construction makes each latent state depend on its predecessors, so later states may repeat information already present in the latent sequence rather than capture complementary visual details. We introduce GLaQ, a grounded latent-query framework that replaces sequential latent rollout with a fixed set of context-conditioned queries grounded in the original visual tokens. The grounded queries are reinjected for answer generation, providing direct and coordinated access to source visual evidence. We train GLaQ with localized-view supervision followed by reinforcement learning under task-level rewards. Across five benchmarks for fine-grained visual understanding and perception, GLaQ-7B gains 5.99--9.66\% over its base model and leads all compared visual latent methods, suggesting that direct query-to-image grounding can recover localized evidence from the full image without external visual operations or autoregressive latent rollouts.

</details>


### [94] [Efficient Audio-Visual Generation via Synchrony-Aware Cross-Modal Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15522)
*Shengchuan Gao,Teng Hu,Bohao Feng,Luchen Li,Wenqiang Wang,Hongqian Deng,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种同步感知的加速框架，针对音频-视频联合生成模型的高推理成本问题。关键发现是双向音频-视频交叉注意力呈现结构化交互模式，高响应集中在少数与声音相关的视觉和时序区域。基于此，提出保护性稀疏注意力策略，保留同步关键令牌的高保真计算，同时稀疏化冗余注意力交互，从而在提升推理效率的同时维持视频质量、音频质量及音视频同步性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-视频生成模型虽然能统一生成同步的音视频，但推理成本高，因为长视频令牌序列需在多个去噪步骤中反复进行注意力计算。已有加速技术（如低比特量化、注意力稀疏化、特征缓存）主要针对纯视频生成设计，直接应用到音视频模型会忽略音频与视频分支间的交互，可能破坏音视频同步。

Method: 提出同步感知加速框架：(1) 观察到双向音视频交叉注意力呈现结构化交互，高响应集中在少数与声音相关的视觉和时序区域；(2) 基于此交互模式，设计保护性稀疏注意力策略——对同步关键令牌保留高保真计算，同时对冗余注意力交互进行稀疏化处理；(3) 在加速过程中显式考虑跨模态依赖。

Result: 该方法在提升推理效率的同时，保持了视频质量、音频质量和音视频同步性。

Conclusion: 通过显式将跨模态依赖性纳入加速过程，本工作表明在加速音视频联合生成时，利用交叉注意力中的结构化交互模式可以兼顾效率与同步质量，为多模态生成模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent audio-visual generation models can synthesize synchronized video and sound in a unified diffusion process, but their inference cost remains high because long video token sequences require repeated attention computation across denoising steps.A variety of acceleration techniques have been developed for video generation models, including low-bit quantization, attention sparsification, and feature caching.However, since these methods are originally designed for video generation, directly applying them to audio-visual models overlooks the interactions between the audio and video branches and may therefore disrupt audio-video synchronization.We present a synchronization-aware acceleration framework for efficient audio-visual generation.Our key observation is that bidirectional audio-video cross-attention reveals structured interactions between the two branches, with high responses often concentrated on a few sound-related visual and temporal regions.Guided by this interaction pattern, we introduce a protected sparse attention strategy that preserves high-fidelity computation for synchronization-critical tokens while sparsifying redundant attention interactions.By explicitly accounting for cross-modal dependence during acceleration, our method improves inference efficiency while keeping video quality, audio quality, and audio-video synchronization.

</details>


### [95] [EA-LiteUNet: An Edge-Adaptive and Resource-Efficient U-Net for Boundary-Sensitive Dermoscopic Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15537)
*Wang Jiangtao,Nur Intan Raihana Ruhaiyem,Fu Panpan,Yang Yu,Huang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EA-LiteUNet，一种边界感知且计算高效的U-Net变体，用于皮肤镜图像分割。通过边界感知表示学习、注意力引导特征调制和资源自适应推理策略三个核心机制，在ISIC 2018等三个公开数据集上实现了优于现有方法的边界精度（HD95降至12.89像素，Dice达92.08%），且参数量仅0.29M，计算量1.17 GFLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜图像分割中边界轮廓描绘一直面临挑战，因为病变边界属于高频分量，易受混叠、噪声放大和信息丢失影响。传统卷积架构中的重复下采样和特征变换会严重退化边界表示，因此需要专门设计边界敏感的轻量级分割模型。

Method: EA-LiteUNet整合三个核心机制：(1)边界感知表示学习——抑制混叠并保留高频结构细节；(2)注意力引导特征调制——在多尺度特征中选择性增强边界相关响应；(3)资源自适应推理策略——动态平衡分割精度与计算效率。

Result: 在ISIC 2018数据集上，HD95显著降至12.89像素，Dice得分达92.08%；在三个公开皮肤镜数据集上均取得一致的优秀边界精度。模型仅0.29M参数和1.17 GFLOPs计算量，属于超轻量级配置。

Conclusion: 消融实验验证了三个组件的互补效应，确认它们对增强边界保真度和稳定优化的贡献，证明了EA-LiteUNet在边界敏感医学图像分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate boundary delineation remains a persistent challenge in dermoscopic image segmentation because of blurred lesion margins, heterogeneous textures, and complex background artifacts. From a signal-processing perspective, lesion boundaries represent high-frequency components that are highly susceptible to aliasing, noise amplification, and information loss. Consequently, repeated downsampling and feature transformations in conventional convolutional architectures often lead to severely degraded boundary representations. To address these limitations, we propose EA-LiteUNet, an edge-adaptive and computationally efficient U-Net variant specifically designed for boundary-sensitive medical image segmentation. The architecture integrates three core mechanisms: (1) boundary-aware representation learning to suppress aliasing and preserve high-frequency structural details; (2) attention-guided feature modulation to selectively enhance boundary-relevant responses across multi-scale features; and (3) a resource-adaptive inference strategy to dynamically balance segmentation accuracy and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations across three public dermoscopic datasets demonstrate that EA-LiteUNet consistently achieves superior boundary precision. Specifically, on the ISIC 2018 dataset, the method significantly reduces the 95% Hausdorff Distance (HD95) to 12.89 pixels while maintaining a robust Dice score of 92.08%. Notably, this strong performance is achieved with an ultralightweight configuration of merely 0.29M parameters and 1.17 GFLOPs. Ablation studies further validate the complementary effects of these components, confirming their contribution to enhanced boundary fidelity and stable optimization.

</details>


### [96] [CrossView: Can Vision-Language Models Reason Across Cameras?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15539)
*Sahil Shah,S P Sharan,Harsh Goel,Manvik Pasula,Adithya Hebbalae,Minkyu Choi,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出传统视频理解基准仅关注单摄像头设置，而现实世界中的自动驾驶、安防、机器人等系统都依赖多摄像头网络。作者提出多摄像头推理与单摄像头问题本质不同，涉及上下文规模随视角数增长、遮挡消解、视角选择与跨视角证据整合等独特挑战。为此引入CrossView基准，覆盖自动驾驶、安防监控、自我/他人中心视频及机器人四个领域，评测GPT-5.2、Qwen3-VL等模型，结果显示准确率普遍偏低，开源模型大幅落后，且性能与联合多视角处理能力强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准均基于单摄像头设定，而真实世界中的多摄像头系统（自动驾驶、安防、机器人）具有重要意义。多摄像头推理面临上下文规模扩展、跨视角遮挡消解、视角重要性判断和证据整合等独特挑战，但缺乏针对性的系统基准，导致当前模型在这些挑战上表现不足却无法被衡量。

Method: 构建CrossView多摄像头视频问答基准，覆盖自动驾驶、安防监控、自我/他人中心视频和机器人四个应用领域；评测包括GPT-5.2等专有模型和Qwen3-VL等开源模型，分析模型在多视角联合处理下的表现。

Result: 所有评测模型在CrossView上准确率普遍偏低；开源模型与专有模型存在明显差距；模型性能与其联合处理多视角的能力高度相关，验证了CrossView作为多摄像头视频严格基准的有效性。

Conclusion: 多摄像头视频理解是独立于单摄像头问题的重要研究方向，现有模型在此任务上能力不足。CrossView提供了系统化评测基准，为多摄像头视频理解研究指明了方向，并公开代码与数据集以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Video understanding benchmarks have long centered on single-camera settings, where modern multi-modal language models achieve strong performance across image and video tasks. Yet, the real world runs on multi-camera networks: autonomous vehicles, security systems, and robots all gather data across many simultaneous views. We argue that this is not simply "more" of the single-camera problem; it is fundamentally different. Multi-camera reasoning requires handling context that scales with the number of views, resolving occlusions visible from only a subset of cameras, judging which views matter, and integrating evidence across perspectives that may overlap or diverge. Current models struggle with exactly these challenges, yet no benchmark systematically targets them. We introduce CrossView, a multi-camera video question-answering benchmark spanning autonomous driving, security surveillance, egocentric/exocentric video, and robotics. Evaluation of proprietary models, such as GPT-5.2, and open-source models, like Qwen3-VL, reveals consistently low accuracy, with open-source models trailing by a wide margin. Performance scales strongly with a model's ability to jointly process multiple viewpoints, positioning CrossView as a rigorous benchmark for multi-camera video. We open-source our code and dataset at https://utaustin-swarmlab.github.io/CrossView.

</details>


### [97] [RigidBench: Evaluating Rigid-Body Physics in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15555)
*Swarnim Jain,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者提出RigidBench，一个基于模拟器的基准测试，用于分离评估视频预测模型在运动、几何、身份、背景稳定性和视觉相似性等方面的表现。通过五个刚体任务和十种指标评估八个模型，发现排名高度依赖所测指标，并且SSIM与3D轨迹误差呈正相关（r=0.89）。此外，利用5000个带精确模拟状态的训练视频微调Wan 2.2 TI2V-5B，将3D轨迹误差降低约20%而不影响SSIM，并通过干预实验揭示物体位置信息在扩散Transformer中的表示方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频预测模型的评估指标（如SSIM）无法区分运动中不同方面的错误，例如运动、几何、身份、背景稳定性和视觉相似性可能独立失败，但整体帧分数将这些误差混合在一起，导致无法准确判断模型是否正确地预测了物体运动。需要一个能够分离这些方面、以模拟器为基准的评估方法。

Method: 1）构建RigidBench：基于模拟器的基准测试，包含五个刚体任务，变化物体、材质、视角及室内外场景，提供逐帧掩码、深度、6自由度轨迹和接触信息用于评分；2）对八个模型在100个样本上使用十种测量指标进行独立评估；3）生成5000个带精确模拟状态的训练视频，用于微调Wan 2.2 TI2V-5B；4）通过teacher-forced探针和靶向干预分析模型的内部表示。

Result: 1）没有模型在所有十项指标上领先，排名强烈依赖于所测量的方面；2）模型均值上，较高的SSIM伴随着较大的3D轨迹误差（r=0.89），表明纯视觉相似性指标可能误导对运动准确性的判断；3）全量微调Wan 2.2 TI2V-5B可将3D轨迹误差降低约20%，同时SSIM几乎不变；4）干预实验表明物体位置信息在整个扩散Transformer中均有表示并被去噪计算使用。

Conclusion: 视频预测模型评估需要多维度的、能够分离不同错误来源的基准，RigidBench提供了一个模拟器支撑的解决方案。当前模型在视觉相似性与运动准确性之间存在矛盾关系，而针对性的微调和干预分析表明可以通过训练改进模型的运动预测能力而不损害视觉质量。

Abstract: Video models are increasingly used to predict what happens next in a scene, yet the metrics commonly used to compare their outputs say little about whether the predicted objects move correctly. Motion, geometry, identity, background stability, and visual similarity can fail independently, but whole-frame scores often mix these errors together. We introduce RigidBench, a simulator-grounded benchmark that compares a generated continuation with a reference rollout from the same initial frame and motion description. Its five rigid-body tasks vary objects, materials, viewpoints, and indoor and outdoor scenes, with per-frame masks, depth, 6-DoF trajectories, and contacts available for scoring. We evaluate eight models on the same 100 examples with ten measurements that keep these aspects separate. The resulting rankings depend strongly on what is measured: no model leads on all ten, and across model means, higher SSIM accompanies larger 3D trajectory error (r = 0.89). RigidBench also includes 5,000 training videos with exact simulator state, which we use to fine-tune and analyze Wan 2.2 TI2V-5B. Full fine-tuning reduces 3D trajectory error by about 20% with almost no change in SSIM, while teacher-forced probes and targeted interventions show that object position is represented throughout Wan's diffusion transformer and used by its denoising computation.

</details>


### [98] [Catching Hallucinated Citations in Video-LLM Question Answering: A Self-Verification Pipeline and Verifier Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15574)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视频问答系统中视觉语言模型产生"欺骗性幻觉"（时间戳引用暗示接地但事实并不支持）的问题，提出了一条闭环验证管线：由检索增强语言模型草拟带逐声明时间戳引用的答案，每个被引用的帧在展示给用户前被独立重新审查。实验对比了朴素基线和三种验证设计，发现直接询问视觉模型会因谄媚行为完全失败（0%检出率），盲重新描述+通用LLM裁判不稳定（0%-100%波动），而使用小型自然语言推理模型作为裁判则稳定且可解释，在对抗性错误前提问题上能捕获79%的捏造声明且不影响真实声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频问答系统基于视觉语言模型生成带时间戳的声明时，往往高置信度地产出不被引用帧支持的答案。时间戳的存在暗示了接地性，却没有保证正确性，从而增加用户信任但并未提高准确率，这种欺骗性幻觉亟需解决。

Method: 提出闭环验证管线：(1) 检索增强语言模型生成带逐声明时间戳引用的答案草稿；(2) 每个被引用的帧在展示给用户前被独立重新审查。对比朴素基线和三种验证设计方案，包括直接询问视觉模型、盲重新描述+通用LLM裁判、盲重新描述+小型NLI模型裁判，并在Apple Silicon (MLX) 和 Google Colab (CUDA) 两个平台上评估。

Result: 直接询问视觉模型完全失败（40条声明0%检出率，归因于谄媚）；盲重新描述加通用LLM裁判结果不稳定（0%-100%波动取决于提示措辞）；用小型自然语言推理模型替代裁判后获得稳定可解释的验证器，在对抗性错误前提问题上捕获79%的捏造声明，同时不误伤真实声明。

Conclusion: 时间戳引用不足以保证接地正确性，必须对每个引用帧进行独立验证。小型NLI模型作为验证器是稳定、可解释且有效的方案，优于直接询问和大模型裁判。作者开源了完整管线、评估框架及双平台实现。

Abstract: Video question answering systems built on vision-language models often produce timestamped claims with high confidence even when unsupported by the cited frame. This deceptive hallucination arises because timestamps imply grounding without ensuring correctness, increasing user trust but not accuracy. We introduce a pipeline that closes this loop. A retrieval-augmented language model drafts answers with per-claim timestamp citations, and each cited frame is independently re-examined before being shown to the user. We compare against a plain baseline and ablate three verification designs, evaluated on both Apple Silicon (MLX) and Google Colab (HF Transformers, CUDA). Directly asking the vision model whether a frame supports a claim fails completely (0% catch rate on 40 claims) due to sycophancy. Blind re-captioning plus a general LLM judge improves results but is unstable, oscillating between 0% and 100% flagged depending on prompt phrasing. Replacing that judge with a small natural language inference model yields a stable, interpretable verifier that catches 79% of fabricated claims on adversarial false-premise questions while leaving true claims untouched. We release the full pipeline, evaluation harness, and implementations for both Apple Silicon and Colab. Code is available at https://github.com/yogesh-iitj/grounded-video-qa.

</details>


### [99] [PoseAdapter: Dual-Stream 2.5D Controllable Image Generation for Complex Multi-Object Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15583)
*Yufeng Chi,Huimin Ma,Fan Gao,Zhice Niu,Keqin Li,Jianmin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PoseAdapter，一种轻量级框架，通过对象描述、2D边界框和3D角度等条件布局实现高保真2.5D可控图像生成，并引入上下文感知双流表示解决属性泄露和场景一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I扩散模型在多对象场景的精确空间和方向控制上仍存在挑战：密集3D地图方法计算成本高，而现有方法存在严重的属性泄露和"剪切粘贴"伪影问题。

Method: 1) 提出高效的2.5D条件布局：使用对象描述、2D边界框和3D角度作为空间角度锚点，替代密集空间图；2) 引入上下文感知双流表示（Context-Aware Dual-Stream Representation）：通过并行掩蔽和非掩蔽路径，将局部对象token和关系增强的场景token注入MM-DiT架构的视觉流；3) 构建OrientLayout高质量数据集，包含标准化2.5D标注和实例级解耦语义。

Result: 实验表明PoseAdapter在空间准确性、方向精度和多对象视觉保真度方面均优于最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: PoseAdapter通过轻量的2.5D条件布局和双流表示机制，有效解决了属性泄露与场景连贯性之间的生成权衡问题，为多对象可控图像生成提供了高效方案。

Abstract: While Text-to-Image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved remarkable success, precise spatial and orientational control in multi-object scenes remains a persistent challenge. Existing methods either rely on computationally expensive dense 3D maps or suffer from severe attribute leakage and "cut-and-paste" artifacts. To address these limitations, we propose PoseAdapter, a lightweight framework for high-fidelity 2.5D controllable image generation. Instead of dense spatial maps, it establishes precise spatial-angular anchors using an efficient condition layout: individual object captions, 2D bounding boxes, and 3D angles. To resolve the generative trade-off between strict instance isolation and global coherence, we introduce a Context-Aware Dual-Stream Representation. By injecting local object tokens and relation-enriched scene tokens into the visual stream of modern MM-DiT architectures via parallel masked and unmasked pathways, PoseAdapter eliminates attribute leakage while preserving natural inter-object relationships and scene-level coherence. To support this paradigm, we construct OrientLayout, a high-quality dataset featuring standardized 2.5D annotations and instance-level decoupled semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PoseAdapter outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in spatial accuracy, orientational precision, and multi-object visual fidelity. Code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/cyf23/PoseAdapter.

</details>


### [100] [AlloEgo-VLM: Disambiguating Allocentric and Egocentric Reference Frames in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15605)
*Kuan-Lin Chen,Tzu-Ti Wei,Chao-Chi Liao,Yu-Chee Tseng,Jen-Jee Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AlloEgo-View数据集和AlloEgo-VLM框架，用以解决视觉语言模型（VLM）在空间语义理解中因缺乏明确参考框架而产生的歧义问题，并通过NVIDIA Isaac Sim中的具身机器人实验验证了其实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间认知中的方向性受认知心理学、空间科学和文化背景影响，但自然语言描述空间关系时常省略明确的参考框架，导致语义歧义，可能对具身AI机器人造成严重错误。现有VLM因缺乏对参考框架和物体朝向的训练，常产生不一致的响应。

Method: 1) 构建AlloEgo-View数据集，包含（图像、查询、视角特定答案）三元组，从自我中心（egocentric）和他人中心（allocentric）两个视角捕捉关键物体关系；2) 采用结构化空间表示，标注详细场景描述、参考物体与目标物体、朝向、参考框架及视角类型；3) 基于该数据集开发AlloEgo-VLM框架，通过监督微调实现歧义消解并易集成到现有VLM中；4) 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中的具身机器人平台上部署验证。

Result: 实验揭示了当前VLM在处理视角特定查询方面的局限性，同时证明了AlloEgo-VLM在歧义消解方面的强大能力，并在开放目标搜索任务的真实场景中验证了其可行性。

Conclusion: AlloEgo-VLM能有效解决VLM中自我中心与他人中心参考框架的歧义问题，为具身AI在空间语义理解方面提供了可靠方案，且具有良好的可集成性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: This study investigates the challenge of ambiguity faced by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in understanding spatial semantics. Spatial cognition, shaped by cognitive psychology, spatial science, and cultural context, often assigns directionality to objects. However, natural language descriptions of spatial relations frequently omit explicit reference frames, leading to semantic ambiguity and potentially serious errors for embodied AI robots. Existing VLMs, due to insufficient training on reference frames and object orientations, often produce inconsistent responses. To address this issue, we construct a new dataset, AlloEgo-View, comprising (image, query, view-specific answer) triplets that capture key object relations from both allocentric and egocentric perspectives. The view-specific descriptions follow a structured spatial representation that annotate detailed scene descriptions, reference and target objects, their orientations, reference frames, and view types. Building on AlloEgo-View, we develop AlloEgo-VLM, a framework to disambiguate allocentric and egocentric reference frames, even under ambiguous queries, and to be easily integrated into existing VLMs via supervised fine-tuning. Furthermore, we deploy our framework onto an embodied robotic platform within NVIDIA Isaac Sim to validate its real-world feasibility in open-ended object searching tasks. Experiments highlight the limitations of current VLMs in handling view-specific queries and demonstrate the strong disambiguation ability of AlloEgo-VLM.

</details>


### [101] [EgoGazeLite: On-Device Egocentric Gaze Prediction for Token-Efficient Multimodal LLM Video Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15614)
*Matteo Stoiber,Niels Buus Lassen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGazeLite是一个轻量级双过程视线预测器，用软件替代专用眼动追踪硬件，实现对可穿戴设备上多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的令牌高效凝视裁剪。该模型仅15.7M参数、6.71 GFLOPs，在消费级加速硬件上以21.6ms/帧实时运行，在十种测试情况下其预测凝视裁剪与真实凝视裁剪在下游描述质量上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
