Existing occlusion-aware refinements usually provide obstacle masks as auxiliary inputs, so the state-update path is only indirectly protected. We propose SIGMA-Lane, which treats this failure mode as state contamination in State Space Model (SSM)-based temporal modeling. SIGMA-Lane places occlusion-aware gates on the SSM write and residual-fusion paths, controlling how current observations enter temporal memory and are fused back after temporal propagation. After coordinate-consistent affine alignment, the model combines two complementary paths: SSM-consistent dual-gating for temporal filtering and Structural Spatial Retrieval (SSR) for recovering missing lane structure from aligned historical priors. Experiments on VIL-100 and OpenLane-V show improved temporal stability under heavy occlusion, with competitive F1 and mIoU scores.

</details>


### [168] [Depth-Dominant Skeleton Detection for Natural Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16367)
*Chengkun Rao,Yixuan Deng,Min Li,Yangjun Ou,Ye Li,Ziwei Luo,Zhaojing Wang,Junwei Tang,Bangchao Wang,Xiaoyun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出以深度图为主导模态、RGB图为辅助模态的骨架检测新范式DDSkel，通过非对称编码器设计将RGB信息融入深度特征，仅用36%的可训练参数便在最具挑战性的SymPASCAL数据集上超越所有现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然场景骨架检测均以RGB图像为唯一输入，在复杂内容图像上性能显著下降。深度图像对颜色和纹理不敏感，能提供清晰的区域轮廓和区域间空间关系，天然缓解复杂场景下的骨架检测难题。

Method: 提出以深度图像为主导模态、RGB图像为辅助模态的新范式。采用非对称编码器设计，将RGB信息融合进深度特征中，其中RGB分支参数仅为深度分支的12%。模型结构简洁，无复杂设计。

Result: DDSkel仅使用当前最优方法36%的可训练参数，在最具挑战性、包含大量复杂图像的SymPASCAL数据集上超越了所有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了深度主导、RGB辅助这一新范式的有效性，表明深度图像在复杂场景骨架检测中具有显著优势，且高效轻量。

Abstract: To date, all natural scene skeleton detection follows the paradigm of taking RGB images as the sole input; despite notable progress, methods under this paradigm suffer significant performance degradation on complex-content images. We observe that depth images are inherently insensitive to color and texture, and can provide clear regional contours and inter-region spatial relationships, which naturally alleviates the difficulty of skeleton detection in complex scenarios. Motivated by this observation, this paper proposes for the first time a novel skeleton detection paradigm where depth images serve as the dominant modality and RGB images act as the auxiliary, and accordingly presents a model DDSkel (short for Depth-Dominant Skeleton Detection) under this paradigm. DDSkel employs an asymmetric encoder design to fuse RGB information into depth features, with the RGB modality branch having only 12% the parameters of the depth modality branch. DDSkel has a simple structure without intricate designs. Nevertheless, with only 36% of the trainable parameters of the current best method, DDSkel outperforms all state-of-the-art approaches on SymPASCAL, the most challenging dataset with a large volume of complex images.

</details>


### [169] [Adaptive Post-Processing Drives Instance-Level Detection in Stroke Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16377)
*Qinghui Liu,Jon André Ottesen,Atle Bjørnerud,Kyrre Eeg Emblem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对医学图像分割中实例级病灶检测与体素重叠指标不匹配的问题，提出了体积条件自适应后处理（VCAP）方案。在ISLES'26竞赛中，作者发现后处理比架构设计对Lesion-F1的提升更重要：VCAP将Lesion-F1提升0.032，约为任何架构改进的6倍。同时提出分辨率感知注意力架构Viola2Plus，虽未改善小病灶Dice但将检出率提升3.7%。最终两架构集成在1453例训练集上获得Dice 0.651和Lesion-F1 0.614。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准医学图像分割管线通常只针对体素级重叠（如Dice）训练和后处理，但临床更关注实例级病灶检测（如Lesion-F1）。这种不匹配对小病灶尤为严重：接近匹配阈值的预测与完全漏检得分相同。作者旨在解决这一指标错配问题。

Method: 提出Volume-Conditioned Adaptive Post-Processing (VCAP)方案，根据每个病例的预测病灶负担自适应调整组件尺寸阈值，以优化实例级检测指标。此外设计了分辨率感知注意力架构Viola2Plus，专门针对小病灶分割。

Result: VCAP使Lesion-F1提升0.032（约6倍于任何架构改进的效果）；Viola2Plus将小病灶检出率提升3.7%但Dice不变。集成后处理系统在1453例训练集上5折交叉验证达到Dice 0.651和Lesion-F1 0.614，优于未处理单模型基线的0.644和0.573。

Conclusion: 在实例级病灶检测任务中，后处理策略（如VCAP）对性能的贡献远大于架构设计。这提示研究者应更重视指标错配问题，将训练与后处理目标对齐到实例级检测，而非仅关注体素重叠。标准Dice指标可能掩盖对小病灶检测的真实改进。

Abstract: Instance-level lesion detection has been an increasingly larger focal point in medical image segmentation besides the more standard voxel-level overlap. Still, most pipelines are trained and post-processed for voxel overlap alone. In particular, the mismatch is most pronounced for small lesions, where a near-miss prediction---substantial overlap that falls just short of the instance-matching threshold---scores the same as a complete miss. In our ISLES'26 submission, we found that closing this gap mattered far more in post-processing than in architecture design. Our Volume-Conditioned Adaptive Post-Processing (VCAP) scheme adjusts component-size thresholds to each case's predicted lesion burden, improving Lesion-F1 by 0.032 (unbiased cross-fold estimate)---approximately 6 times larger than any architectural change we tested. A resolution-aware attention architecture (Viola2Plus), designed for small-lesion segmentation, shows why the distinction matters: it left small-lesion Dice unchanged but raised small-lesion detection rate by 3.7\%, a real effect voxel-overlap metrics alone would have missed. Under 5-fold cross-validation on the 1,453-case training set, our post-processed two-architecture ensemble achieves Dice 0.651 and Lesion-F1 0.614, versus 0.644 and 0.573 for the unprocessed single-model baseline.

</details>


### [170] [Synthetic Data Augmentation for Satellite-Based Analysis of Battle-Damaged Agricultural Fields in Ukraine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16380)
*Marta Sumyk,Oleksandr Kosovan,Iryna Voitsitska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究利用合成数据增强（GAN和DDPM）改善卫星图像中农田受损分类的性能，在数据稀缺且类别不平衡的条件下，通过平衡DDPM增强将准确率从84%提升至88%，平衡准确率从67%提升至81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测乌克兰战争对农业用地的损害对粮食安全、环境稳定和战后恢复至关重要，但基于卫星图像的计算机视觉系统开发受限于标注数据稀缺，尤其是受损农田的标注图像非常有限。

Method: 训练类条件GAN和去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）生成合成卫星图像（含轰炸和未轰炸农田样本），仅用于训练数据增强；使用Vision Transformer分类器在多种真实+合成数据配置下进行评估，测试集全部为真实数据。

Result: 基于平衡DDPM增强的最佳配置将准确率从84%提升至88%，平衡准确率从67%提升至81%，宏F1从65%提升至78%，未轰炸类（代表性不足类别）的召回率从41%提升至69%。

Conclusion: 结果表明合成卫星图像在数据稀缺的战区地理空间应用中具有显著潜力，能有效提升分类模型在数据有限且不平衡条件下的性能。

Abstract: Monitoring war-induced damage to agricultural land in Ukraine is important for understanding threats to food security, environmental stability, and post-war recovery. However, the development of computer-vision systems for satellite-based damage analysis is limited by the scarcity of labeled imagery, especially for damaged agricultural fields. This work investigates synthetic data augmentation as a method for improving classification under limited and imbalanced training data. We train class-conditional Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) and Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) architectures on real satellite images and use them to generate additional bombed and not-bombed agricultural-field samples. The generated images are used only for training augmentation, while all downstream evaluation is performed on an exclusively real test set. A Vision Transformer classifier is trained under multiple real and synthetic data configurations to measure the practical utility of each generative approach. The best configuration, based on balanced DDPM augmentation, improves accuracy from 84\% to 88\%, balanced accuracy from 67\% to 81\%, macro F1 from 65\% to 78\%, and recall for the underrepresented not-bombed class from 41\% to 69\%. These results demonstrate the potential of synthetic satellite imagery for data-scarce geospatial applications in war-affected regions.

</details>


### [171] [Self-Routed Tensor Adapters for Parameter-Efficient Universal Visual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16384)
*Suraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Routed Tensor Adapters (SRTA)，一种紧凑的多域视觉自适应框架。SRTA将每个输入投影到低秩空间，利用可学习的域矩阵计算路由权重，并用这些权重混合共享Tucker核心的切片，生成样本特定的自适应矩阵，无需外部门控网络。同时引入渐进式深度加权路由目标来强化路径学习。在五个多域视觉分类基准上，SRTA以显著更少的可训练参数达到与MoE风格PEFT方法相当或略优的平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用的视觉表示需要能够在异构域之间自适应而不将知识碎片化为域特定模块。标准低秩适配器对所有输入使用固定子空间，在域风格、背景和语义上下文不同时受限；而MoE基适配器虽有多个专家路径，但常依赖外部路由器和大型专家库，增加参数并分离路由与自适应。

Method: SRTA将每个输入投影到低秩空间，从该表示中通过可学习域矩阵计算路由权重，用这些权重混合共享Tucker核心的切片，生成样本特定的自适应矩阵，不依赖外部门控网络。此外引入渐进式深度加权路由目标，在多个适配器层间监督路由决策。

Result: 在五个异构多域视觉分类基准上，SRTA达到与MoE风格PEFT基线相当或略优的平均精度，同时使用更少的可训练参数。在4域设置中rank 64时SRTA仅用2.77M参数（MoLoRA为9.52M），6域设置中用3.00M参数（MoLoRA为14.31M）。

Conclusion: SRTA在精度与参数效率之间提供了有效的折衷方案，支持将视觉基础模型适配为通用的多域表示，同时保持共享视觉因子的复用与域感知专业化能力。

Abstract: Universal visual representations require adaptation mechanisms that adapt across heterogeneous domains without fragmenting knowledge into domain-specific modules. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts frozen visual foundation models efficiently, but standard low-rank adapters use a fixed subspace for all inputs, which can be restrictive when domains differ in style, background, and semantic context. MoE-based adapters improve specialization through multiple expert pathways, but often rely on external routers and large expert banks, adding parameters and separating routing from adaptation. We propose \textbf{Self-Routed Tensor Adapters}, a compact framework for multi-domain visual adaptation. SRTA projects each input into a low-rank space, computes routing weights from this representation using a learnable domain matrix, and uses these weights to blend slices of a shared Tucker core. This produces a sample-specific adaptation matrix without an external gating network, allowing shared visual factors to be reused while supporting domain-aware specialization. To strengthen pathway learning, we introduce a progressive depth-weighted routing objective that supervises routing decisions across adapter layers. Across five heterogeneous multi-domain visual classification benchmarks, SRTA achieves competitive or slightly stronger average accuracy than MoE-style PEFT baselines while using substantially fewer trainable parameters. At rank 64, SRTA uses 2.77M parameters in the 4-domain setting compared with 9.52M for MoLoRA, and 3.00M in the 6-domain setting compared with 14.31M. Overall, SRTA offers an effective accuracy-parameter trade-off for adapting visual foundation models toward universal multi-domain representations. \href{https://github.com/surajyadav-research/SRTA}{GitHub}

</details>


### [172] [Joint Flow Matching Enables Continuous Dose-Conditioned Cell Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16424)
*Lea Bogensperger,Manuela Merlo,Martin Baumgartner,Michael Krauthammer,Bernard Ciraulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合流匹配方法，通过双时间步公式同时建模细胞潜变量和药物浓度，实现剂量条件下的单细胞形态变换，并支持从细胞形态估计浓度及外推至未见剂量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将扰动建模为分布到分布的映射而缺乏显式浓度处理，要么将浓度视为离散类别标签，无法实现连续的剂量控制，这限制了细胞扰动预测的精度和泛化能力。

Method: 提出联合流匹配（joint flow matching）方法，采用双时间步（dual-timestep）公式同时建模细胞潜在表示和药物浓度，利用流匹配的可逆性实现剂量条件下的单细胞变形，并诱导潜在空间中单调的剂量-反应几何结构。

Result: 在两种化合物上与代表性基线相比，达到有竞争力或更优的每浓度指标；同时实现了离散类方法结构上无法提供的能力，如连续剂量控制和浓度估计，并验证了对训练中未见剂量的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 联合流匹配方法为连续剂量条件下的细胞扰动预测提供了新范式，相比离散类别方法具备结构性优势，可在保持性能的同时支持更丰富的剂量相关任务。

Abstract: Generative modeling has shown increasing promise for predicting cellular perturbation effects under chemical compound treatments. Existing approaches either model perturbation as a distribution-to-distribution mapping without explicit concentration handling, or treat concentration as a discrete class label, precluding continuous dose control. We introduce a joint flow matching approach that simultaneously models cell latents and drug concentration via a dual-timestep formulation, enabling dose-conditioned single-cell morphing through the invertibility of flow matching. The joint formulation induces a monotonic dose-response geometry in latent space and additionally supports concentration estimation from cell morphology. As proof of concept, we further demonstrate generalization to an unseen dose held out during training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive or improved per-concentration metrics on two compounds compared with representative baselines, while enabling capabilities structurally unavailable to discrete-class methods.

</details>


### [173] [Contrastive Energy Fields for Inference-Time Procedure Planning in Instructional Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16457)
*Mohamed Afham,Christoph Reich,Oliver Hahn,Daniel Cremers,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CEFITO提出了一种将过程规划（Procedure Planning）重新定义为任务约束优化问题的新方法。通过学习一个动作条件化的表示空间，CEFITO在推理时显式地省略不相关动作，从而在动作空间中强制执行任务特定的逻辑约束，在两个基准数据集上达到了最先进的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程规划方法（如前馈神经网络或扩散模型）将所有动作视为同等可能，无法强制执行任务特定的逻辑约束，导致预测出不可靠或不相关的动作序列。作者希望引入一种能够显式推理动作空间并排除不合理动作的方法。

Method: CEFITO的核心思想包括：(1) 学习一个预测器来构建动作条件化的表示空间；(2) 将过程规划形式化为任务约束下的优化问题；(3) 在推理时显式地在动作空间上进行推理，通过省略不相关动作来强化任务约束。

Result: CEFITO在两个已建立的过程规划基准数据集上取得了最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过将过程规划重新表述为任务约束优化问题，并在推理时显式排除不相关动作，CEFITO有效提升了过程规划的准确性，验证了利用任务逻辑约束进行动作空间推理的有效性。

Abstract: Procedure planning seeks to estimate a sequence of actions to transition from an observed initial state to a given goal state. Current procedure planning approaches directly predict action sequences from latent representations using feed-forward neural networks or diffusion-based inference. These paradigms treat every action as plausible, lacking the ability to enforce task-specific logical constraints that render certain actions irrelevant or not plausible. We propose CEFITO, a procedure planning approach that learns a predictor to express an action-conditioned representation space. Based on this representation space, we formulate procedure planning as a task-constrained optimization problem. Unlike prior methods, CEFITO explicitly reasons over the action space by omitting irrelevant actions during inference-time planning. This reformulation enables effective procedure planning and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on two established procedure planning benchmarks.

</details>


### [174] [Shared-Structure 4D Spectral Gaussian Representation for Sparse-View Spectral CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16463)
*Jiancheng Fang,Shaoyu Wang,Wenjun Xia,Yang Chen,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-SG（Shared-Structure 4D Spectral Gaussian Representation），一种用于稀疏视角能谱CT重建的共享结构4D高斯表示方法。通过共享高斯几何结构并利用GSC-Net预测光谱密度变换，实现连续4D表示，在六个数据集上超越最强高斯基线（PSNR提升1.05dB，SSIM提升0.005，LPIPS降低0.014）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角能谱CT重建需要同时处理角度欠采样和光谱耦合两大挑战。现有方法通常对每个光谱通道独立优化几何结构，导致计算开销大且无法充分利用光谱间的结构共享信息，需要一种能有效分离共享空间结构与光谱衰减变化的重建方法。

Method: 提出4D-SG表示法，包含两个关键组件：(1) 从全光谱结构投影中学习共享高斯几何结构；(2) 使用GSC-Net（高斯级光谱密度曲线网络）预测每个高斯的原始密度变换。这种因式分解将共享空间结构与光谱衰减变化分离，建立了从离散光谱测量到任意未知光谱通道查询的连续4D表示。

Result: 在六个包含合成、模拟投影和真实投影的数据集（50视角）上取得最佳平均性能。与最强高斯基线相比，PSNR从35.56dB提升至36.61dB，SSIM从0.909提升至0.914，LPIPS从0.208降至0.194。

Conclusion: 4D-SG通过共享几何结构+光谱密度回归的因式分解策略，有效解决了稀疏视角能谱CT重建中角度欠采样与光谱耦合的联合挑战，验证了该方法在合成与真实数据上的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Sparse-view spectral computed tomography (CT) reconstructs energy-resolved attenuation volumes from limited projection views, requiring simultaneous handling of angular undersampling and spectral coupling. We propose a SharedStructure 4D Spectral Gaussian Representation (4D-SG) that learns shared Gaussian geometry from full spectrum structural projections and uses a Gaussian-wise Spectral Density Curve Network (GSC-Net) to predict Gaussian raw density transformations. This factorization separates shared spatial structure from spectral attenuation variation, avoids independent channel geometry optimization, and establishes a continuous 4D-SG representation from discrete spectral measurements for unobserved spectral channel queries. Experiments on six synthesized, simulated projection, and real projection datasets with 50 views demonstrate the best average performance. Compared with the strongest Gaussian baseline, 4D-SG improves PSNR from 35.56 dB to 36.61 dB, increases SSIM from 0.909 to 0.914, and reduces LPIPS from 0.208 to 0.194, demonstrating its effectiveness for sparse-view spectral CT reconstruction.

</details>


### [175] [Sterilizable Scene Graph Generation for Operating Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16469)
*Nick Lemke,Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Antoine P. Sanner,John Kalkhof,Henry John Krumb,Ghazal Ghazaei,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-NCA，一种基于神经细胞自动机（NCA）的轻量级手术视频场景图生成框架，专为无风扇手术室边缘设备设计。相比现有方法，SG-NCA参数减少55倍，性能与既有基线相当，并支持下游任务如手术视频字幕生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术场景图生成方法依赖大规模深度模型，因硬件占用、卫生约束、延迟和数据隐私等问题难以在手术室（OR）实际部署。需要一个轻量且高效的解决方案，能在无风扇设备上运行以满足OR卫生规范和部署需求。

Method: SG-NCA首次将神经细胞自动机（NCA）应用于场景图生成：利用NCA-based多类分割实现高效目标检测与特征提取，结合轻量级关系预测器生成场景图。这是首个基于NCA构建并能学习结构化表示的框架。

Result: 在白内障手术和胆囊切除术视频上进行评估，SG-NCA性能与既有基线相当，但参数数量减少55倍；成功在无风扇边缘设备上部署，并展示了手术视频字幕生成等下游应用。

Conclusion: SG-NCA证明了NCA在手术场景理解中的可行性，为经济实惠、隐私保护且适配手术室环境的术中场景理解提供了新路径，有望推动手术AI在真实临床场景中的落地部署。

Abstract: Scene graph generation from surgical video enables a holistic and structured understanding of surgical scenes by modeling objects and their semantic relationships. Despite recent advances, state-of-the-art approaches rely on large, parameter-heavy deep learning models that are impractical for deployment in the operating room (OR) due to hardware footprint, hygiene constraints, latency, and data privacy concerns. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first scene graph generation method built on NCAs and the first NCA framework capable of learning structured representations. We introduce SG-NCA, a lightweight scene graph generation framework based on Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), designed for inference in fanless devices critical for OR hygiene protocols. SG-NCA is the first scene graph generation combining NCA-based multiclass segmentation for efficient object detection and feature extraction with a lightweight relation predictor. We evaluate SG-NCA on videos of cataract surgery and cholecystectomy, demonstrating performance comparable to established baselines while requiring 55x fewer parameters. We showcase deployment on fanless edge devices better suited for the OR and demonstrate downstream applications such as surgical video captioning, highlighting SG-NCA's potential for affordable, privacy-preserving, and OR-ready intraoperative scene understanding.

</details>


### [176] [RISE: Roadside Infrastructure Sequence Understanding across 3D Tracking and Structured Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16480)
*Yanbo Jiang,Haotian Zheng,Jiahao Wang,Hanxiao Ren,Yitao Xu,Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Hao Cheng,Yiqian Tu,Jinhao Li,Zhiyuan Xuan,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RISE是一个涵盖指标级3D跟踪和结构化视觉语言推理的框架。在跟踪方面，它结合SAM3视频身份与标定引导的掩码一致性进行多视角身份关联，无需LiDAR或特定任务的3D训练即可恢复持久3D轨迹。在视觉语言推理方面，通过人工审核的MLLM流程构建了RISE-VQA数据集（33,910个QA对，557个片段，16个路口，61个路边视角），并提出了路口留出的RISE-Bench评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路边感知场景缺乏同时涵盖指标级3D跟踪与视觉语言推理的统一框架，现有方法要么依赖昂贵传感器（如LiDAR）或需针对特定布局重新训练，要么缺乏高质量、无信息泄露的预测性视觉问答数据集，难以评估多视角路口感知能力。

Method: 1) 指标3D跟踪：图像only方法，融合SAM3视频身份与标定引导的掩码一致性进行多视角身份关联，通过标定条件几何实现跨路口实例化而无需布局特定重训练；2) 结构化VQA：人工审核的MLLM流程挖掘高价值片段，用受限全上下文Oracle构建bbox锚定的预测性QA，避免向评估模型泄露未来证据；3) 构建RISE-VQA数据集与RISE-Bench基准，采用确定性任务特定指标评估语义选择、坐标、未来框和交互集。

Result: 在6个路口的20个人工审核片段上，生成轨迹在定义的多视角评估范围内达到66.9 MOTA。RISE-VQA数据集包含33,910个QA对。实验显示领域自适应持续带来收益，时间上下文总体有益，但空间定位、未来定位和交互推理仍是持久挑战。

Conclusion: RISE框架证明了无需LiDAR或任务特定3D训练即可实现跨路口泛化的指标级3D跟踪，并首次构建了大规模、防泄露的路边预测性视觉问答数据集与基准。同时揭示了当前方法在空间锚定、未来位置预测和交互推理方面的不足，为路边智能感知研究提供了新基准与方向。

Abstract: We present RISE (Roadside Infrastructure Sequence Understanding and Evaluation), a framework spanning metric 3D tracking and structured vision-language reasoning in roadside sequences. For metric tracking, our image-only method combines SAM3 video identities with calibration-guided mask agreement for multi-view identity association, recovering persistent 3D tracks without LiDAR or task-specific 3D training. Its calibration-conditioned geometry allows the procedure to be instantiated at different calibrated multi-camera intersections without layout-specific retraining. On 20 human-reviewed clips from six intersections, the generated tracks achieve 66.9 MOTA within the defined multi-view evaluation scope. For structured vision-language reasoning, a human-reviewed MLLM pipeline mines high-value clips and uses a constrained full-context Oracle to construct bbox-grounded predictive QA without exposing future evidence to evaluated models. The resulting RISE-VQA dataset contains 33,910 QA pairs from 557 clips across 16 intersections and 61 roadside views. Its intersection-held-out RISE-Bench evaluates semantic choices, coordinates, future boxes, and interaction sets with deterministic task-specific metrics. Experiments show consistent benefits from domain adaptation and generally from temporal context, while revealing persistent challenges in spatial grounding, future localization, and interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [177] [Remote-Sensing City Layout Extraction with MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16484)
*Zigan Zhou,Kai Li,Yupeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Code-as-City"框架，将单张俯视遥感图像中的城市布局提取任务转化为带约束的代码生成问题。系统通过多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的三轮有序推理，结合图像模型生成的五类语义先验，恢复道路、土地覆盖区域及建筑，最终将记录归一化为城市图和受限布局程序。执行该程序可同时生成可渲染的3D城市布局和正射语义投影，实现平面与三维的一致表达与协同再生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感系统以检测框、语义掩膜或矢量边界描述城市内容，虽能定位类别并支持图像平面评分，但无法构成可执行的城市布局——即不能保留对象身份、类型化关系、拓扑结构及再生规则，缺乏可编辑、可检查的"城市代码"表达能力。

Method: 1) 图像模型先产生对齐的五类语义布局先验；2) 三个有序的MLLM推理轮次利用图像与先验分别恢复道路、土地覆盖区域及其关系、建筑；3) 确定性归一化将累积记录转换为城市图和受限布局程序；4) 执行程序生成可渲染的3D城市布局以及共享几何上的正射语义投影。

Result: 在CityLayout-100数据集的100个场景上，完整框架获得41.1%的平均交并比（mIoU）和48.3%的全局交并比（gIoU）。

Conclusion: 实验结果提供了定量证据，表明视觉观测可以被转化为可检查、可编辑的城市代码，并同时生成耦合的平面与三维输出，验证了以代码生成方式实现城市布局提取的可行性。

Abstract: Remote-sensing systems usually describe urban content with detection boxes, semantic masks, or vector boundaries. Such outputs locate classes and support image-plane scoring, yet they do not by themselves constitute an executable layout that retains object identities, typed relations, topology, and regeneration rules. Code-as-City instead casts urban-layout extraction from a single top-down image as constrained code generation with a multimodal large language model (MLLM). An image model first produces an aligned five-class semantic layout prior. Three ordered MLLM passes use the image and this prior to recover roads, land-cover regions and relations, and buildings. Deterministic normalization converts the accumulated records into a city graph and a restricted layout program. Executing the program creates a renderable 3D city layout and an orthographic semantic projection over shared geometry. The projection admits pixel-level comparison with remote-sensing masks, while named objects, relations, and editing operations remain available for synchronized regeneration of both views. Evaluated on the 100 scenes of CityLayout-100, the complete framework obtains 41.1% mean intersection-over-union and 48.3% global intersection-over-union. This result provides quantitative evidence that visual observations can be translated into inspectable, editable city code with coupled planar and 3D outputs.

</details>


### [178] [HiFi-BRep: High-Fidelity Latent Representation for Robust B-Rep Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16485)
*Junhao Hou,Chenqi Luo,Pufan Wang,Jiaying Lu,Yusheng Liu,Feiwei Qin,Meie Fang,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiFi-BRep是一个新颖的边界表示（B-Rep）生成框架，通过拓扑感知编码器（消除padding噪声和特征污染）和单阶段解码器（并行预测几何与拓扑，嵌入可微分的流形约束）来解决现有深度生成方法中的表示脆弱性和生成脆弱性问题，在结构有效性和几何保真度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-Rep生成是计算机辅助设计中的基础任务，但直接合成高保真且结构有效的B-Rep仍具挑战。现有深度生成方法存在两类脆弱性：（1）表示脆弱性——由padding噪声和潜在空间特征污染引起；（2）生成脆弱性——源于顺序误差传播以及由于不可微分的有效性约束导致的训练-推理不匹配。

Method: HiFi-BRep通过两个协同贡献解决上述问题：首先，拓扑感知编码器通过可学习查询消除padding，并通过拓扑引导注意力防止特征污染，构建高保真潜在表示；其次，单阶段解码器并行预测几何与拓扑，将核心流形约束嵌入为可微分的学习目标，确保几何与拓扑相互引导，避免级联误差。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiFi-BRep在结构有效性和几何保真度两方面均显著优于现有最先进方法，为高质量B-Rep合成提供了鲁棒解决方案。代码和模型已公开。

Conclusion: HiFi-BRep通过同时解决表示层面的脆弱性和生成层面的脆弱性，实现了高质量的B-Rep合成，验证了拓扑感知编码与单阶段并行解码设计的有效性，为该领域提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-Rep) generation is a fundamental task in computer-aided design, yet the direct synthesis of high-fidelity and structurally valid B-Reps remains a major challenge. Existing deep generative methods suffer from two forms of brittleness: representation brittleness, caused by padding noise and feature contamination in the latent space, and generation brittleness, stemming from sequential error propagation and a train-inference mismatch due to non-differentiable validity enforcement. We propose HiFi-BRep, a novel framework that addresses these limitations through two synergistic contributions. First, a topology-aware encoder constructs a high-fidelity latent representation by eliminating padding via learnable queries and preventing feature contamination with topology-guided attention. Second, a single-stage decoder jointly predicts geometry and topology in parallel, embedding core manifold constraints as a differentiable learning objective. This design ensures mutual guidance between geometry and topology while avoiding cascaded errors. Extensive experiments show that HiFi-BRep significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both structural validity and geometric fidelity, providing a robust solution for high-quality B-Rep synthesis. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/1nnoh/HiFi-BRep.

</details>


### [179] [Towards Real-Time and Adaptable LiDAR Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16490)
*Azhar Hussian,Martin Vossiek,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RapidLiDAR是一种快速LiDAR场景补全方法，通过自适应初始化模块学习预测每个点的空间位移来生成粗略场景初始化，再使用多尺度重建模块通过体素和BEV特征图细化场景。该方法在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-360上达到与最先进方法相当的性能，同时仅需0.1秒完成全场景补全，比最快先前方法快2.3倍，满足了10Hz的典型车载LiDAR采集速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiDAR场景补全方法遵循"初始化-细化"范式，存在两个主要问题：(1) 生成式方法迭代细化随机高斯噪声导致速度慢；(2) 非生成式方法使用固定噪声尺度扰动部分场景，无法覆盖大间隙和遮挡区域，且需要针对每种传感器配置手动重新校准。因此需要一个无需手动调参、速度足够快的场景补全方法。

Method: RapidLiDAR包含两个核心模块：1) 自适应初始化模块：预测每个部分输入点的空间变化位移，将稀疏观测扩展为适应局部几何的粗略场景初始化，无需手动噪声调优；2) 多尺度重建模块：通过查询多尺度3D体素和2D BEV特征图来细化点位置。架构用体素和BEV特征提取替代最远点采样和K近邻搜索等点邻域算子，从而提高速度并适应不同输入分辨率。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-360数据集上的实验表明，RapidLiDAR达到与最先进方法相当的补全性能，同时以0.1秒完成全场景补全，比最快的先前方法快2.3倍，匹配了典型车载LiDAR传感器10Hz的采集速率。

Conclusion: RapidLiDAR通过将初始化本身设计为可学习的数据驱动组件，并采用体素和BEV特征提取替代点邻域操作，实现了无需手动调参的快速LiDAR场景补全，向实时LiDAR场景补全迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: LiDAR scene completion is a key component of 3D perception in autonomous driving, where the scene must be completed in real time to be usable in downstream tasks. Existing approaches typically follow an initialize-and-refine paradigm, in which a coarse initialization of the scene is first constructed, then refined into complete 3D geometry. Generative models are slower because they iteratively refine random Gaussian noise into the scene, while non-generative methods perturb the partial scene with a fixed noise scale, which limits coverage of large gaps and occluded regions and requires manual recalibration for each new sensor configuration. We present RapidLiDAR, a LiDAR scene completion method that treats the initialization itself as a learned, data-driven component. We propose an adaptive initialization module that predicts a spatially varying displacement for each partial input point, expanding the partial observations into a coarse scene initialization adapted to the local geometry, without requiring manual noise tuning. To refine this coarse initialization into a complete and coherent scene, we additionally propose a multi-scale reconstruction module that further refines point positions by querying multi-scale 3D voxel and 2D BEV feature maps constructed from the input scan. By replacing point-neighborhood operators such as farthest point sampling and $k$-nearest neighbor search with voxel- and BEV-based feature extraction, our architecture is faster and can handle different input resolutions by design. Experiments on SemanticKITTI and KITTI-360 show that our method achieves completion performance on par with the state of the art while completing a full scene in 0.1 seconds, which is 2.3 times faster than the fastest prior method. This matches the 10 Hz acquisition rate of typical automotive LiDAR sensors, taking a step toward real-time LiDAR scene completion.

</details>


### [180] [MLLM-Guided Semantic Correction for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16513)
*Junhao Chen,Zheqi Lv,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang,Zhou Zhao,Feiyang Chen,Xinyu Duan,Baoxing Huai,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的、可解释的视频生成中途修正框架，通过将多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的反馈直接集成到扩散采样循环中，在视频生成过程中实时检测和纠正语义偏差（如缺失物体、属性错误、动作不匹配），从而提升语义对齐、视觉保真度和时间一致性，且无需修改模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频生成模型常出现语义错误（缺失物体、错误属性、动作不匹配）。已有的语义修正方法要么在采样前进行优化，要么在采样后进行细化，但如何在视频生成过程中检测和纠正语义偏差仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一个无需训练的可解释中途生成修正框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 语义评估监督器（Semantic Assessment Supervisor），生成中间预览帧进行语义评估和偏差诊断；(2) 语义修改助手（Semantic Modification Assistant），通过可控潜在轨迹干预在推理过程中纠正语义漂移。整体通过将MLLM反馈集成到扩散采样循环中，实现连续的自我反思和轨迹修正。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性，显著提升了语义对齐、视觉保真度和时间一致性，且无需修改模型参数。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态大语言模型的语义评估信号直接注入扩散采样过程，可以实现生成过程中的实时语义纠错，为视频生成提供了一种训练免费的、可解释的修正范式，为未来生成过程中的质量控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models and Transformer architectures have led to significant progress in text-to-video generation. However, these models often suffer from semantic errors such as missing objects, incorrect attributes, or mismatched actions. Although some semantic correction methods perform optimization before sampling or refinement after sampling, how to detect and correct semantic deviations during the video generation process remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a training-free, interpretable mid-generation correction framework that integrates multimodal large language model (MLLM) feedback directly into the diffusion sampling loop. Our framework achieves diffusion trajectory correction by injecting semantic evaluation signals during video synthesis, enabling the model to optimize the generated content through continuous self-reflection. We propose two key modules: a Semantic Assessment Supervisor that generates intermediate preview frames for semantic evaluations and deviation diagnostics, and a Semantic Modification Assistant that corrects semantic drift during inference via a controllable latent trajectory intervention. Our method improves semantic alignment, visual fidelity, and temporal consistency without modifying model parameters. We validate the effectiveness of our approach through extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [181] [Matched Outcomes, Divergent Gaze: How Foveated MLLMs Search Compared to Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16514)
*Mohamed Amine Kerkouri,Marouane Tliba,Aladine Chetouani,Ulas Bagci,Alessandro Bruno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种通用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）与人类在目标导向视觉搜索（COCO-Search18）中的眼动扫描路径。在人类匹配的中央凹视觉输入条件下，模型在目标存在性判断和目标获取效率上达到或超过人类水平，但注视过程本身与人类不同：模型的扫描路径呈现低熵、大幅值、高度自洽的特征，缺乏人类搜索的序列性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉搜索本质上是序列性的——中央凹必须依次落在候选目标上进行确认，形成扫描路径。研究MLLMs在相同中央凹输入下是否像人类一样搜索，关系到两件事：一是能否将MLLMs用作人类视觉的模型；二是注意力对齐分数（attention-alignment scores）的有效性。现有度量可能忽略了过程层面的本质差异。

Method: 在COCO-Search18数据集上，将三个通用MLLMs与人类眼动扫描路径进行比较。方法核心是逐次注视（fixation by fixation）驱动每个模型，使其接受与人类完全匹配的中央凹视觉输入，并从三个维度评估：目标存在性决策、到达目标的效率、以及注视过程本身。通过消融实验测试不同图像退化条件。

Result: 三个维度发生分离：在决策和目标获取上，模型达到或超越人类——目标存在检测接近天花板效应，且比人类更常在第一次扫视就到达目标。但注视过程不像人类：在人类匹配条件下，三个模型共享同一特征——低熵、大幅值、自洽的扫描路径，模型自身的一致性远高于两个人之间的一致性。任何退化条件都无法恢复既像人类又保持人类成功率的表现。

Conclusion: 模型的搜索符合单次通过、非序列的架构特征，而非视觉度限制所致。匹配的视网膜输入能复现人类注视的位置，但不能复现注视在时间上如何展开。这一差距存在于过程维度上，是答案对齐指标和显著性（saliency）指标无法测量的。因此这些指标不能证明人类级别的视觉能力，零样本模型适合回答结果性和空间性问题，但不适合时间性和过程性问题。

Abstract: Human visual search is serial: the fovea must land on a candidate to confirm it, and those landings form a scanpath. Whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs), given the same foveated input, search as humans do bears on their use as models of human vision and on attention-alignment scores. We compare three general-purpose MLLMs with human eye-movement scanpaths on goal-directed search (COCO-Search18), driving each model fixation by fixation through an identical, human-matched foveated view and assessing it along three axes: the decision of target presence, the efficiency of reaching the target, and the gaze process itself. The axes dissociate. On the decision and on target acquisition the models match or exceed humans, detecting present targets near ceiling and reaching them on the first saccade more often than people do. The gaze process is not human. Under the human-matched condition, all three share one signature: low-entropy, large-amplitude, self-consistent scanpaths that agree with themselves far more closely than two humans agree with each other. That is consistent with a single-pass, non-serial architecture rather than a limit of acuity. Matched retinal input reproduces where humans look but not how the looking unfolds in time, and no degradation regime recovers human-like search at human-like success. The gap sits on a process axis that answer-alignment and saliency metrics do not measure. Because they miss it, such metrics cannot certify human-like vision, and zero-shot models suit outcome and spatial questions but not temporal, process-level ones.

</details>


### [182] [FLEET: Token-Based Feature Extraction for Event Camera-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16523)
*Tristan Gottwald,Maximilian Schier,Melanie Schaller,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLEET是一种用于事件相机数据的特征提取器，直接处理异步事件流，利用随机傅里叶特征和交叉注意力将可变长度事件序列压缩为固定大小的潜在表示，解耦了推理成本与传感器分辨率，无需辅助损失即可端到端学习，在新基准上超越了SOTA方法并展现出对观测频率变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有异步、高频、空间稀疏等理想特性，但现有的强化学习方法未能充分利用这些特性：基于CNN的方法将事件聚合为稀疏网格，导致计算成本与传感器分辨率耦合且模糊了时间信息；而生成式基线依赖于轨迹数据进行预训练。

Method: 提出FLEET特征提取器，直接处理事件序列。利用随机傅里叶特征（RFF）和交叉注意力机制，将可变长度的事件流压缩成固定大小的潜在表示，从而将骨干网络的推理成本与传感器分辨率解耦，实现无辅助损失的端到端学习。

Result: 在新的高吞吐量基准上验证，基于序列的方法超越了SOTA性能，并展现出对观测频率变化的优越鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于序列的事件处理方法是事件相机强化学习的有效方向，FLEET证明了直接处理事件流而非聚合为网格可以更好地发挥事件相机的优势，并为未来研究提供了高吞吐量基准。

Abstract: Event cameras generate asynchronous, high-frequency data streams offering spatially sparse information at lower latency than traditional cameras.In principle, these properties should be ideal for the design of control policies.However, reinforcement learning research in this field remains limited as existing approaches fail to fully exploit the sensor's properties.CNN-based methods negate the sensors benefits by aggregating events into sparse grids. This couples compute cost to sensor resolution and blurs the temporal information. Meanwhile, existing generative baselines rely on the availability of trajectory data to pretrain the model. We propose FLEET (Feature Learning from Events via Efficient Tokenization), a feature extractor that processes event sequences directly. Leveraging random Fourier features and cross-attention, our architecture compresses variable streams into fixed-size latent representations. This decouples inference cost of the feature extractor's backbone from the sensor's resolution, enabling end-to-end learning without auxiliary losses. We validate FLEET on a new, high-throughput benchmark. The results demonstrate that our sequence-based approach surpasses SOTA performance and exhibits superior robustness to variations in observation frequencies.

</details>


### [183] [Automatic Cephalometric Landmark Localization on CBCT-Derived Digitally Reconstructed Radiographs for Skeletal Malocclusion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16535)
*Benjamin Hou,Konstantinia Almpani,Janice S. Lee,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CephViT，一种基于Vision Transformer的自动二维侧位头影测量标志点定位模型，在公开数据集上达到1.28±1.42 mm的平均径向误差和92.0%的成功检测率（3.0 mm阈值）。在私有3D CBCT队列中，通过生成DRR图像作为2D输入进行自动分析，其骨骼性错颌畸形分类准确率（70.0%）与人工标志点标注（68.3%）相当，验证了CBCT衍生DRR用于自动化头影测量分析的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统头影测量标志点人工标注耗时费力且难以规模化，需要开发自动化方法以支持颅颌面评估和骨骼性错颌畸形分类。

Method: 采用Vision Transformer架构构建CephViT模型，在公开侧位头影图数据集上训练和验证；对私有3D CBCT数据生成数字重建放射影像（DRR）作为2D输入，将标志点坐标归一化到统一坐标系，利用共享标志点进行骨骼性错颌畸形分类。

Result: CephViT在公开数据集上平均径向误差1.28±1.42 mm，3.0 mm阈值下成功检测率92.0%；DRR自动定位的标志点用于分类达到70.0%准确率，高于人工标注的68.3%。

Conclusion: 自动化头影测量分析在CBCT衍生DRR上具有可行性，可用于骨骼性错颌畸形评估，为临床大规模自动化头影测量提供了支持证据。

Abstract: Manual cephalometric landmark annotation is important for craniofacial assessment but is labor-intensive and difficult to scale. We introduce CephViT, a Vision Transformer-based model for automated 2D lateral cephalometric landmark localization, and evaluate its use in downstream skeletal malocclusion classification. CephViT was trained and benchmarked on a public lateral cephalogram dataset, achieving a mean radial error of 1.28 +/- 1.42 mm and a successful detection rate of 92.0% at 3.0 mm. Because the private evaluation cohort consisted of 3D CBCT scans, lateral cephalogram-like digitally reconstructed radiographs (DRRs) were generated from each volume and used as 2D inputs to the landmark localization model. Landmark coordinates were normalized into a common coordinate frame, and skeletal malocclusion classification was performed using landmarks shared between the reference and DRR-based pipelines. Classification performance using DRR-localized landmarks was comparable to that obtained using manually annotated reference landmarks, with accuracies of 70.0% and 68.3%, respectively. These results support the feasibility of automated cephalometric analysis on CBCT-derived DRRs for skeletal malocclusion assessment.

</details>


### [184] [Supervising the Path to Fine Scales: GalerkinFlow for Scientific-Field and Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16546)
*Zikang Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GalerkinFlow是一个方程无关的超分辨率框架，将每个粗-细图像对转化为整个重建路径上的监督信号。模型在中间状态预测粗到细的残差速度，并通过粗锚点定义伪端点，使每个中间状态都直接贡献于最终目标的监督，同时结合有限差分目标和尺度条件的Galerkin算子混合，在一系列基准上取得最优或持平的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超分辨率模型仅在最终高分辨率输出上施加监督，缺乏对预测从降采样观测到精细目标之间演化过程的控制。这种"只监督终点"的方式限制了模型学习重建路径的中间过程信息。

Method: 1) 在重建路径的随机中间状态处预测粗到细残差速度；2) 使用粗锚点定义伪端点，证明其重建损失与中间速度损失通过已知时间相关权重精确关联；3) 额外监督一步推理使用的粗端点；4) 有限差分目标约束局部空间变化；5) 结合卷积特征与尺度条件的Galerkin算子混合。

Result: 在Navier-Stokes和Darcy Flow上取得评估的方程无关基线中最低的原始空间误差，在DIV2K上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过在重建路径的每个中间状态上施加监督而非仅监督最终输出，GalerkinFlow证明了"全程监督"策略的有效性，且无需任何物理方程或元数据，实现了方程无关的通用超分辨率框架。

Abstract: Most super-resolution models learn from paired data by supervising only the final high-resolution output. This provides little control over how the prediction should evolve between the downsampled observation and its fine target. We introduce GalerkinFlow, an equation-agnostic framework that turns each coarse--fine pair into supervision along an entire reconstruction path. At a random sample of intermediate states on the reconstruction path, the model predicts the coarse-to-fine residual velocity and uses coarse-anchor point to define a pseudo-endpoint. We show that the reconstruction loss of this pseudo-endpoint is exactly related to the intermediate velocity loss through a known time-dependent weight. Consequently, every intermediate state contributes supervision toward the same fine target, rather than serving only as an internal step toward an endpoint loss. Because intermediate states already reveal part of the missing fine-scale structure, we additionally supervise the coarse endpoint used during one-step inference. A finite-difference objective further constrains local spatial variation. GalerkinFlow combines convolutional features with scale-conditioned Galerkin operator mixing and requires no governing equation or physical metadata. It achieves the lowest raw-space errors among the evaluated equation-agnostic baselines on Navier--Stokes and Darcy Flow, while remaining competitive on DIV2K.

</details>


### [185] [SQuad: Sub-Quadratic Attention Distillation for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16585)
*Animesh Karnewar,Denis Korzhenkov,Amirhossein Habibian,Mohsen Ghafoorian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SQuad提出了一种次二次注意力蒸馏框架，通过将预训练的二次复杂度Softmax注意力视频DiT蒸馏为O(n√n)复杂度的SQuad-Attention模型，在Wan 2.2 5B文本到视频模型上匹配了教师模型的VBench性能（83.20 vs 83.08），同时将注意力FLOPs降低约67倍、注意力延迟降低约11倍，端到端DiT延迟降低2倍，并仅需6次NFE即可生成视频（默认100次）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer（DiTs）的大部分计算集中在自注意力操作上，其计算成本随潜在token数量n呈二次增长O(n²)。对于视频生成任务，token数量庞大，导致该计算项主导运行时间和内存，限制了可生成视频的分辨率和时长。虽然线性O(n)和低秩O(nk)的自注意力替代方案通过更廉价的核函数替代完整的softmax QK^T，但很少能恢复原始的表达能力，留下难以弥合的质量差距。

Method: SQuad提出了Sub-Quadratic Attention Distillation框架，实现O(n√n)复杂度的蒸馏注意力，自然平衡效率与表达力。该方法不是从头训练视频DiT（成本过高），而是将预训练的完整softmax自注意力DiT适配到SQuad-Attention中，通过两阶段蒸馏：第一阶段为Flow-Matching监督微调（SFT），第二阶段为改进的分布匹配蒸馏（DMD2），后者同时使采样更高效。

Result: 在Wan 2.2 5B文本到视频模型上，SQuad匹配了二次复杂度的教师模型的VBench性能（83.20 vs 83.08），同时将每步每块的注意力FLOPs降低约67倍，注意力延迟降低约11倍，端到端DiT延迟降低2倍，并且仅需6次神经函数评估（NFE）即可生成视频（默认需要100次）。

Conclusion: SQuad通过中间复杂度O(n√n)的注意力蒸馏方案，在保持与二次复杂度教师模型相当的视频生成质量的同时，大幅降低了计算成本并加速了采样过程，为高效视频生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) spend most of their compute inside the Self-Attention operation, whose cost grows quadratically, $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$, with the number of latent tokens $n$. For the task of video generation, the token count is large, so this term dominates runtime and memory, and thereby caps the resolution and duration we can generate. Linear $\mathcal{O}(n)$ and low-rank $\mathcal{O}(nk)$ surrogates of Self-Attention trade the full softmax $QK^T$ for cheaper kernels, but rarely recover the original's expressivity, leaving a stubborn quality gap. Motivated by this, we propose SQuad, a Sub-Quadratic Attention Distillation framework that achieves a complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n\sqrt{n})$ in the resulting distilled Attention, naturally balancing the efficiency v/s expressivity trade-off. Instead of training our own Video DiT from scratch, which is prohibitively expensive, we fit a pretrained full softmax Self-Attention DiT into our proposed SQuad-Attention one by distilling the former in two stages: Flow-Matching Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), followed by improved Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD2) which additionally makes the sampling more efficient. On the Wan~2.2 5B text-to-video model, SQuAD matches the quadratic teacher on VBench ($83.20$ v/s $83.08$) while cutting the per-step per-block attention FLOPs by $\sim$$67\times$ and attention latency by $\sim$$11\times$, and end-to-end DiT latency by 2$\times$, all while also generating a video in only $6$ Neural Functional Evaluations (NFEs) instead of the default $100$.

</details>


### [186] [Ultra: Unsupervised Cross-Task Optimization for Reliable Restoration Segmentation Collaboration under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16589)
*Shiqin Wang,Zhiqian Li,Haoyuan Du,Junming Chen,Jiayuan Li,Tianrun Xu,Haoyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ultra框架，一种用于恶劣天气下无监督域自适应语义分割的新型无监督复原-分割协同学习框架。该框架将跨任务交互重新定义为不确定性下的方向选择和因果效应估计，通过候选方向生成（CTDN）和基于干预的过滤（CMIL）实现可靠协同，抑制幻觉驱动的错误传播，在三个基准上达到最先进的语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UDA-ASS方法隐式假设复原和分割任务能提供互利的引导，但在严重退化且缺乏目标域监督的情况下，跨任务优化方向的有效性从根本上不可识别，导致幻觉驱动的错误传播。这促使作者重新思考跨任务交互的可靠性问题。

Method: 提出Ultra协同学习框架：1) CTDN（跨任务方向网络）利用互补的视觉结构和语义信息生成候选优化方向，并在复原与分割之间进行协作方向选择；2) CMIL（因果干预）将跨任务信息传递从基于相关性的传播重构为因果效应评估，抑制幻觉传播。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的UDA-ASS基准上实现最先进的语义分割性能；在无监督复原方面也优于现有UDA-ASS复原方法；并泛化到无监督复原和目标检测协同任务。

Conclusion: 通过将跨任务协同从"隐式假设互利"转向"不确定性下的方向选择+因果效应估计"，Ultra证明了可靠的跨任务协同学习是可行的，且具有超越分割任务的泛化能力，代码将公开。

Abstract: Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Adverse Weather Semantic Segmentation (UDA-ASS) aims to transfer semantic knowledge from labeled normal-weather images to unlabeled adverse environments. Existing approaches implicitly assume that restoration and segmentation provide mutually beneficial guidance. However, under severe degradation and without target-domain supervision, the validity of cross-task optimization directions becomes fundamentally unidentifiable, leading to hallucination-driven error propagation. In this work, we propose a novel Unsupervised Restoration-Segmentation Collaborative Learning Framework (Ultra), which reframes cross-task interaction as direction selection under uncertainty and causal effect estimation, enabling reliable collaboration through candidate direction generation and intervention-based filtering. In detail, we propose CTDN and CMIL. The former exploits complementary visual structures and semantic information to generate candidate optimization directions and performs cooperative direction selection between restoration and segmentation. The latter reformulates cross-task information transfer from correlation-based propagation into causal effect assessment, suppressing hallucination propagation. Extensive experiments on three widely used UDA-ASS benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Beyond segmentation, our framework achieves better unsupervised restoration results than existing UDA-ASS restoration methods and generalizes to unsupervised restoration and object detection collaboration tasks. Code and models will be available at https://github.com/Wang-Shiqin/Ultra.

</details>


### [187] [Towards Zero-Shot Domain Generalization for ID Cards Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16591)
*Mario Nieto-Hidalgo,Juan M. Espin,Juan E. Tapia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原型网络（Prototypical Network）的演示攻击检测（PAD）方法，用于国家身份证件识别。该方法仅需每类4个真实样本即可构建可靠原型，并通过剧集式训练（episodic training）固定PAD类别、变化卡片域，使网络学习通用攻击特征。在跨国数据集和DLC-2021基准上达到约9%的平均等错误率（EER），优于softmax和CLIP零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家身份证件的演示攻击检测面临缺乏公开真实样本的困境，导致系统难以跨国家泛化。现有方法在数据受限条件下性能不佳，需要一种既能保护隐私又能减少数据采集、同时具备跨域泛化能力的PAD方案。

Method: (1) 原型网络头部（Prototypical Network head）采用EfficientNet-V2-b0作为骨干网络，每类仅需4个真实样本即可创建可靠原型；(2) 剧集式训练策略（episodic training regime）保持PAD类别固定而变换卡片域，使网络学习通用的攻击线索而非特定于某国的特征。

Result: 在大型多国数据集和公共DLC-2021基准上评估，平均等错误率（EER）约为9%，优于传统softmax和CLIP零样本基线，即使仅使用单一来源国数据也能取得良好性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持隐私性的同时实现了准确且数据高效的PAD检测，大幅减少了数据收集需求，有助于实现可扩展的跨辖区远程身份注册。

Abstract: Presentation-Attack Detection (PAD) for national ID cards is limited by the lack of publicly available genuine samples, making it difficult for systems to generalize across countries. This paper introduces two main innovations: (1) a Prototypical Network head using an EfficientNet-V2-b0 backbone that requires only four genuine samples per class to create reliable prototypes; and (2) an episodic training regime that keeps PAD classes fixed while varying the card domain, allowing the network to learn universal attack cues.
  Evaluated on a large multi-country dataset and the public DLC-2021 benchmark, this method achieves an average Equal Error Rate of around 9\%, outperforming conventional softmax and CLIP zero-shot baselines even with data from a single source country. This approach provides accurate, privacy-preserving PAD while minimizing data collection, facilitating scalable cross-jurisdictional remote onboarding.

</details>


### [188] [GeoPose: Patient-agnostic CTA-to-DSA registration through projection-space calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16600)
*Rudolf L. M. van Herten,Robert Graf,Paula Feldman,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoPose是一种群体训练的框架，用于将术中双平面DSA与术前CTA进行快速准确的3D-to-2D配准，无需患者特定适配或显式容积间预配准，通过学习规范坐标帧中的C臂姿态，再通过投影空间校准和变换组合将其转移到新CTA的原生帧中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D-to-2D配准方法存在明显局限：基于优化的方法对初始化敏感且需要大量迭代；基于学习的方法通常依赖患者特定的训练数据。这些限制阻碍了术中DSA与术前CTA快速准确的配准，因此需要一种无需患者特定适配、能快速运行的群体级训练方案。

Method: GeoPose包含两个核心步骤：1) 在学习的规范坐标帧中估计C臂姿态；2) 通过投影空间校准和变换组合将姿态转移到未见CTA的原生帧。框架由群体训练的残差网络进行姿态细化，之后可选地进行低预算的图像驱动优化。

Result: 在80个DSA观测（来自20个留出患者）上，无优化的GeoPose达到颈动脉平均投影中心线距离（mPCD）5.8mm和clDice 0.45，而最佳基线为14.5mm和0.28，推理仅需0.15秒。经25次优化迭代后，GeoPose达到mPCD 4.6mm和clDice 0.58，约需2秒；在相同预算下，原生初始化优化仅达到14.6mm和0.15。

Conclusion: GeoPose以固定的群体级权重实现了快速的原生帧配准，性能显著优于现有基线，并为下游双平面3D血管重建提供了所需的几何对应关系。

Abstract: Aligning intraoperative biplanar digital subtraction angiography (DSA) to pre-procedural computed tomography angiography (CTA) requires rapid and accurate 3D-to-2D registration. Optimization-based methods are sensitive to initialization and may require hundreds of iterations, whereas learning-based approaches commonly rely on patient-specific training. We propose GeoPose, a population-trained framework that estimates the C-arm pose in a learned canonical frame and transfers it to the native frame of an unseen CTA through projection-space calibration and transform composition. A population-trained residual network refines the pose, followed optionally by low-budget image-driven optimization. GeoPose requires neither patient-specific adaptation nor explicit inter-volume preregistration. On 80 DSA observations from 20 held-out patients, optimization-free GeoPose achieved a carotid mean projected centerline distance (mPCD) of 5.8 mm and a clDice of 0.45, compared with 14.5 mm and 0.28 for the best-performing baseline, while requiring only 0.15 s. After 25 optimization iterations, GeoPose reached an mPCD of 4.6 mm and a clDice of 0.58 in approximately two seconds. Under the same budget, native-initialized optimization achieved 14.6 mm and 0.15, respectively. GeoPose thus provides rapid native-frame registration with fixed population-level weights and the geometric correspondence required for downstream biplanar 3D vascular reconstruction.

</details>


### [189] [Interactive Whole Slide Images for RL-based Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16607)
*Mohamad Mohamad,Francesco Ponzio,Maxime Gassier,Nicolas Pote,Xavier Descombes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的强化学习框架，将全切片图像（WSI）建模为分层多分辨率环境，智能体通过移动、缩放和肿瘤选择动作直接进行肿瘤分割，在肺腺癌WSI上实现了与基于补丁方法相当的粗分割质量，同时将推理时间缩短至每张切片数秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI分析面临两大核心挑战：切片空间分辨率极大导致计算开销高，以及肿瘤区域分布稀疏。现有基于预定义候选补丁的方法效率低下且缺乏灵活性，需要一种能够直接在完整WSI上进行顺序分割的新方法。

Method: 将WSI视为分层多分辨率环境，智能体通过移动、缩放和肿瘤选择三类动作进行导航与分割；采用演员-评论家（actor-critic）架构，同时处理局部观测与全局缩略图表示，使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练。

Result: 在肺腺癌WSI上的实验表明，该框架能够实现全切片上的直接顺序肿瘤分割，在相似放大倍率下获得与基于补丁方法相当的粗分割质量，并将推理时间降至每张切片数秒。

Conclusion: 将WSI建模为交互式环境为基于强化学习的计算病理学提供了有前景的方向，环境设计与动作空间粒度的分析为未来研究提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) analysis remains computationally challenging due to the extremely large spatial resolution of slides and the sparse distribution of tumour regions. We propose an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework for sequential tumour segmentation directly on WSIs. Instead of treating the slide as a predefined collection of candidate patches, we formulate the WSI itself as a hierarchical multi-resolution environment through which an agent navigates using movement, zooming, and tumour selection actions. The agent jointly processes local observations and a global thumbnail representation within an actor-critic architecture trained using proximal policy optimization (PPO). Experiments on pulmonary adenocarcinoma WSIs demonstrate the feasibility of direct sequential tumour segmentation on full slides, achieving comparable coarse segmentation quality relative to patch-based approaches operating at similar magnification levels, while reducing inference time to a few seconds per slide. We further analyse the impact of environment design and action-space granularity. Our results suggest that modelling WSIs as interactive environments provides a promising direction for RL-based computational pathology

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Accuracy: Assessing Calibration of Geospatial Foundation Models and Their Sensitivity to Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16614)
*Nils Lehmann,Jakob Gawlikowski,Burak Ekim,Isaac Corley,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究批判性地指出，当前地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）仅以标准基准准确率平均排名来评估的方式过于狭隘。通过16个冻结编码器、4个分类和5个分割数据集，以及两种正交压力轴的系统实验，发现所有编码器在腐败（corruption）加剧时性能退化且排名变化。关键发现是EO预训练和ImageNet预训练在干净数据的准确率和校准上无差异，但在分布偏移下EO预训练模型更易过度自信。CKA分析揭示EO嵌入表示刚性（移动少但丢失任务信息），而温度缩放和深度集成无法缓解退化，高斯过程探针虽能将云严重条件下的ECE减半但使干净数据ECE翻三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeoFMs在关键地球观测（EO）任务中的实际部署需要更全面的评估视角，而不仅仅是基准准确率的平均排名。现有的评估协议忽略了模型校准（置信度与正确率的一致性）这一关键维度，无法反映模型在真实世界分布偏移下的可靠性，亟需更全面的评估框架。

Method: 在16个冻结编码器上系统评估，覆盖4个分类和5个分割数据集，沿两种正交压力轴（corruption）施加分布偏移。评估维度包括：干净准确率、干净校准、偏移下的准确率与校准；使用CKA分析检查表示变化；并对比三种不确定性量化方法（温度缩放、深度集成、高斯过程探针）以及选择性预测（置信度弃权）在腐败条件下的表现。

Result: 1) 所有编码器随腐败加剧而退化且排名改变；2) EO预训练与ImageNet预训练在干净准确率和校准上无差异，但EO预训练在偏移下更过度自信；3) CKA显示EO嵌入表示刚性——移动少却丢失同样多任务信息并保持过度自信；4) 温度缩放和深度集成无法对抗退化，高斯过程探针在严重云条件下ECE减半但干净数据ECE翻三倍；5) 置信度弃权无法规避自信但错误的预测。

Conclusion: 基准排名和评估应在多种条件和度量下进行，以更全面地评估模型开发进展并弥合与现实部署场景的差距。当前以准确率排名为主的评估协议无法反映模型在关键EO任务中的真实可靠性，需要将校准、鲁棒性和不确定性纳入评估标准。

Abstract: Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) are most commonly ranked and selected by accuracy on standard benchmark conditions via averaged ranks. We show that this protocol is too narrow: the promised deployment in critical EO tasks requires further angles of analysis, mainly calibration, the agreement between a model's confidence and its correctness. Across 16 frozen encoders, four classification and five segmentation datasets, and two orthogonal stress axes, every encoder degrades as corruption intensifies, and the ranking changes as well. Across the four classification benchmarks, EO-pretrained and ImageNet-pretrained encoders are indistinguishable on clean accuracy and clean calibration, and EO pretraining provides no more stability under shift than ImageNet pretraining. Under shift the GeoFMs drift further into overconfidence than the ImageNet-pretrained encoders, at every grade and in every corruption family. A centered kernel alignment (CKA) analysis ties this to representational rigidity: EO-pretrained embeddings move less under corruption while losing just as much task information and remaining overconfident. We apply three commonly explored uncertainty quantification methods and find that temperature scaling and deep ensembles cannot counteract the degradation, while a Gaussian-process probe roughly halves ECE under severe cloud only by tripling it on clean data. In selective prediction experiments, we find that confidence-based abstention cannot defer around confidently wrong predictions, and advocate that benchmark rankings and evaluations should therefore operate across a multitude of conditions and metrics to more holistically evaluate model development progress and close the gap to real world deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [191] [HarmTrace: Anchor-Calibrated Decoupled Optimization for Fine-Grained Target Identification in Harmful Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16622)
*Yujia Li,Yiqun Zhang,Zihan Cheng,Yijie Huang,Tenglong Ye,Zihan Wang,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文扩展了多模态有害模因检测任务，从仅判断有害性扩展到细粒度目标识别（目标类型、实体、文本提及和视觉区域），并提出新数据集 Meme3W、严格指标 JRA 以及 HarmTrace 优化框架，将 JRA 从 17.58% 提升至 52.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害模因检测仅关注图像-文本的有害性分类，模型可能正确预测有害性却错误识别被攻击的目标及其证据。这种粗粒度评估无法全面反映模型对有害内容的理解能力，需要更细粒度的目标识别任务。

Method: 提出 (1) Meme3W 数据集：统一多个公开有害模因数据集并提供人工验证的细粒度标注；(2) JRA 指标：要求有害性标签与所有目标识别字段同时正确的严格记录级指标；(3) HarmTrace 框架：通过实体感知的有监督微调增强目标实体监督，并使用条件目标识别策略优化（CTPO）解耦有害性与目标识别优势，通过虚拟正锚点（VPA）进行优势归一化。

Result: 实验表明代表性多模态大语言模型在有害性准确率和 JRA 之间存在显著差距。HarmTrace 在多个基座上同时改善了 JRA 和有害性准确率，其中 Qwen3-VL-8B 基座的 JRA 从 17.58% 提升到 52.51%。

Conclusion: 细粒度目标识别对于全面的有害模因检测至关重要，HarmTrace 框架有效弥合了有害性分类与目标识别之间的差距，代码已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal harmful meme detection is typically formulated as image--text harmfulness classification. A model may correctly predict harmfulness while misidentifying the attacked target or its supporting evidence. We therefore extend harmful meme detection with fine-grained target identification, asking what type of target is attacked, who is targeted, and where the target appears in the meme. The model predicts harmfulness for every meme and, for harmful memes, outputs the target category, target entity, textual mention, and visual region. To support this task, we introduce Meme3W, which unifies multiple public harmful meme datasets and provides human-verified annotations for harmful instances. We further introduce Joint Record Accuracy (JRA), a strict record-level metric requiring the harmfulness label and all target-identification fields to be jointly correct. Experiments with representative multimodal large language models reveal a substantial gap between harmfulness accuracy and JRA. To narrow this gap, we propose HarmTrace, an anchor-calibrated decoupled optimization framework. HarmTrace strengthens target-entity supervision through entity-aware supervised fine-tuning. It then applies Conditional Target-identification Policy Optimization (CTPO) to decouple harmfulness and target-identification advantages, restricting target-identification optimization to label-correct responses for harmful examples. CTPO uses a Virtual Positive Anchor (VPA) as a fully correct reference for target-identification advantage normalization. HarmTrace improves both JRA and harmfulness accuracy across the evaluated backbones, with JRA on the Qwen3-VL-8B backbone increasing from 17.58\% to 52.51\%. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/llly1234/HarmTrace-for-Harmful-Memes.

</details>


### [192] [DRAFE: Domain-Robust Asymmetric Fusion of Heterogeneous Detection Transformers for Cross-City Fine-Grained Traffic Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16632)
*Divine Yao Agbobli,Geoffery Eyram Agorku,Israel Afriyie,Kwadwo Amankwah-Nkyi,Marvin Osei-Kuffour,Richmond Owusu Duah,Bright Seglah,Kelvin Asamoah Terkper,Kwabena Amoako Adjei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DRAFE方法，融合LW-DETR和RF-DETR两个独立训练的检测器，用于跨城市细粒度交通目标检测。采用两阶段训练策略，先在多样公开交通数据集上进行预训练（包含伪标签扩展和人工审核标注），再在Hafnia Track 6数据集上进行挑战赛契合的微调。推理时采用锚点条件类别一致匹配、可靠性加权坐标融合、一致性感知置信度重校准和互补假设恢复策略。在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6上取得0.4022 mAP，排名第六，较基线集成提升0.0553 mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统需要细粒度车辆识别和跨城市域泛化能力，但现有检测器难以同时兼顾这两方面。作者旨在解决细粒度车辆识别与跨城市域泛化之间的挑战。

Method: DRAFE采用两阶段训练策略：先在多个公共交通数据集上预训练LW-DETR和RF-DETR两个互补检测器，利用伪标签扩展和人类在环标注精炼生成6,049张图像、203,619个标注的数据集；然后在Project Hafnia Track 6数据集上进行挑战赛契合的微调。推理阶段采用锚点条件类别一致匹配、可靠性加权坐标融合、一致性感知置信度重校准和互补假设恢复四种融合策略。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6上，DRAFE达到0.4022 mAP，在25个参赛队伍中排名第六，相比初步集成提升了0.0553 mAP。

Conclusion: DRAFE通过异构检测器集成与精细化训练/推理策略，有效提升了跨城市细粒度交通目标检测性能，验证了独立训练互补检测器融合的可行性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detectors are fundamental to intelligent transportation systems, enabling traffic monitoring, vehicle analytics, and infrastructure management. However, achieving both fine-grained vehicle recognition and robust cross-city domain generalization remains challenging. We present the Domain-Robust Asymmetric Fusion Ensemble (DRAFE), which combines independently trained LW-DETR and RF-DETR detectors for cross-city fine-grained traffic object detection. DRAFE employs a two-stage training strategy that first pretrains complementary detectors on diverse public traffic datasets using pseudo-label expansion and human-in-the-loop annotation refinement, producing a curated corpus of 6,049 images and 203,619 annotations, before challenge-compliant fine-tuning on the Project Hafnia Track 6 dataset. At inference, DRAFE applies anchor-conditioned class-consistent matching, reliability-weighted coordinate fusion, agreement-aware confidence recalibration, and complementary hypothesis recovery. On AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, DRAFE achieves 0.4022 mAP, ranks sixth among 25 participating teams, and improves by 0.0553 mAP over a preliminary ensemble evaluated under identical benchmark conditions.

</details>


### [193] [Training-Free Reconstruction-Based AI-Generated Image Detectors Are Inherently Vulnerable to Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16646)
*Roman Demchenko,Jonas Ricker,Asja Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对基于自编码器重建误差的图像伪造检测器的对抗攻击方法，通过构造不可感知的对抗样本增加原始图像与重建图像之间的距离，使伪造图像被错误分类为真实图像。实验表明该方法在多个生成器和检测器上显著降低检测性能，且对抗样本可跨检测器迁移，揭示了重建型检测器固有的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像的视觉质量和普及度日益提升，需要可靠且稳健的检测方法。重建型检测器因其透明性和无需训练的特性成为有前景的方向，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出两种针对利用自编码器重建误差的检测器的攻击方法，通过构造不可感知的对抗样本来人为增大原始图像与重建图像之间的距离，使伪造图像被误判为真实。

Result: 在三个最先进生成器和三个检测器的评估中，检测性能显著下降，即使攻击图像经过真实世界退化处理仍然有效。对抗样本可自然地在检测器之间迁移，因为它们共享相同的工作原理。

Conclusion: 重建型检测器存在固有的脆弱性，其共享的检测原理使其对对抗攻击具有通用可迁移的弱点，这对其在实际应用中的安全性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: The impressive visual quality and ubiquity of AI-generated images call for reliable and robust detection methods. Reconstruction-based detectors have emerged as a promising direction for transparent and training-free identification of synthetic images. However, due to their fundamentally different mode of operation (compared to standard, classifier-based methods), little is known about their adversarial robustness. In this work, we propose two novel attack methods targeted at detectors that leverage autoencoder reconstruction error. We find that by constructing imperceptible adversarial examples, the distance between original and reconstruction can be artificially increased, causing fake images to be wrongly classified as real. Our evaluation including images from three state-of-the-art generators and three detectors demonstrates that detection performance is significantly decreased, even if attacked images additionally undergo real-world degradations. Critically, our adversarial examples naturally transfer across detectors, as they all share the same principle, pointing towards an inherent vulnerability of reconstruction-based detectors.

</details>


### [194] [X$^2$Localizer: Cross-grained Alignment for Progressive Cross-view Video Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16658)
*Zichao Zeng,Weijia Fan,Yufan Chen,June Moh Goo,Junwei Zheng,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jiaming Zhang,Rainer Stiefelhagen,Jan Boehm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式跨视角视频地理定位（PCVG），作为CVG的部署导向扩展与评测协议，支持变时间预算、前缀推理、随机起点和长距离中断定位。作者提出X²Localizer跨粒度对齐框架（全局前缀-航拍检索+令牌级帧-航拍瓦片匹配，配合预算依赖的非对称目标函数）以及滑动窗口重定位（SWRL）策略，在保持全视频性能的同时显著提升早期定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVG方法依赖固定长度输入和事后精炼，无法在部分或动态观测下进行在线定位。实际部署场景中定位需要在时间预算可变、前缀推理、随机起点和长距离中断等条件下工作，这促使作者定义PCVG协议并开发相应方法。

Method: 1) PCVG评测协议：定义变时间预算、前缀推理、随机起点评估和长距离中断定位。2) X²Localizer：跨粒度对齐框架，包括全局前缀到航拍图像检索（粗粒度）和令牌级帧到航拍瓦片匹配（细粒度），并使用预算依赖的非对称目标函数联合监督。3) SWRL：滑动窗口重定位策略，动态刷新候选区域，无需全序列重处理即可实现失败恢复和长距离部署。

Result: X²Localizer保持传统全视频性能（Recall@1 +0.1，Recall@10 +0.3），同时显著提升早期定位能力。在单帧挑战性设定下，粗检索Recall@1提升+4.7，Recall@10提升+11.5。结合SWRL，在随机起点和长距离场景下实现鲁棒的渐进式定位。

Conclusion: 本文通过PCVG协议和X²Localizer+SWRL方法，弥合了基准评测与现实部署之间的差距，证明渐进式、在线友好的跨视角视频定位是可行且有效的方向。

Abstract: Cross-view Video Geo-localization (CVG) aims to localize ground-view videos by retrieving their corresponding geo-tagged aerial images. However, CVG approaches rely on fixed-length inputs and post-hoc refinement, hindering online-oriented localization under partial or dynamic observations. In this work, we formulate Progressive Cross-view Video Geo-localization (PCVG) as a deployment-oriented extension and evaluation protocol of CVG, enabling localization under varying temporal budgets, prefix-based inference, random-start evaluation, and long-range localization with interruptions. To explore PCVG, we introduce X$^2$Localizer, a cross-grained alignment framework that jointly supervises global prefix-to-aerial retrieval and token-aggregated frame--aerial-tile matching with a budget-dependent asymmetric objective. Furthermore, we introduce a Sliding-Window Re-Localization (SWRL) strategy that dynamically refreshes candidate regions for failure recovery and long-range deployment without full-sequence reprocessing. Extensive experiments show that X$^2$Localizer preserves conventional full-video performance, with marginal gains of +0.1 Recall@1 and +0.3 Recall@10, while substantially improving early localization. In the challenging single-frame setting, X$^2$Localizer improves coarse retrieval by +4.7 Recall@1 and +11.5 Recall@10 over the previous state-of-the-art method. With SWRL, our approach further enables robust progressive localization under random-start and long-distance scenarios, narrowing the gap between benchmark evaluation and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [195] [Turning spectra into images improves plant trait retrieval with 2D-CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16661)
*Javier Lopatin,Teja Kattenborn,Eya Cherif,Sebastián Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将高光谱一维光谱转换为二维图像表示，并使用EfficientNet-B0 CNN进行多性状预测。研究发现简单的直接重塑（Reshape）变换效果最佳（R²=0.684），比最先进的1D基线提高0.097。基于13.9万未标记光谱图像预训练的2D掩码自编码器在线性探测下也超越了所有1D自监督方法。可解释性分析显示模型对蛋白质和叶片水分等具有尖锐吸收特征的性状能读取已建立的叶片化学信息，但对类胡萝卜素和叶面积指数的预测与PROSAIL模型不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习处理高光谱数据时将其视为一维序列，限制了模型对长距离波段间依赖关系的捕捉。研究者质疑将一维光谱转换为二维图像表示是否能改善基于CNN的多性状预测性能。

Method: 在GreenHyperSpectra数据集（7,897个标记光谱、8个性状、400-2450nm）上比较了九种一维到二维的变换方法，使用EfficientNet-B0从零训练。随后在139,000个未标记光谱图像上预训练2D掩码自编码器（MAE-2D），并进行线性探测评估。通过Integrated Gradients和Grad-CAM将波段重要性映射回光谱轴，与PROSAIL辐射传输模型的敏感性模拟进行对比验证。

Result: 最简单的直接Reshape变换表现最佳（R²=0.684±0.001），比1D基线（R²=0.587）高0.097。MAE-2D线性探测达R²=0.646，超过所有1D自监督方法（包括微调后的MAE-1D R²=0.641）。交叉数据集评估中所有模型准确率大幅下降，无模型显著超越1D基线。解释性分析中蛋白质（r=0.45）和叶片水分（r=0.33）与PROSAIL一致，而类胡萝卜素（r=0.06）和叶面积指数（r=-0.11）不一致。

Conclusion: 二维光谱图像的表征优势（而非架构复杂度或ImageNet预训练）是超越一维方法的关键驱动因素。但跨数据集泛化仍是挑战，且模型对某些性状的可解释性不足。

Abstract: Hyperspectral reflectance spectroscopy enables non-destructive estimation of plant functional traits, yet current deep learning approaches process spectra as one-dimensional sequences, which limits how they capture long-range inter-band dependencies. We asked whether transforming 1D spectra into 2D image representations improves multi-trait prediction with convolutional neural networks (CNN). We compared nine transformations using EfficientNet-B0 on the GreenHyperSpectra dataset (7,897 labeled spectra, eight traits, 400-2450 nm), benchmarked against published 1D CNN results on the same split. Trained from scratch, the simplest transformation, a direct Reshape of the spectrum into a 2D grid, performed best ($R^2 = 0.684 \pm 0.001$) and improved on the state-of-the-art 1D baseline ($R^2 = 0.587$, $+0.097$). We then pretrained a 2D masked autoencoder (MAE-2D) on 139,000 unlabeled spectral images. Linear probing, which freezes the encoder and trains only a multilayer perceptron head, reached $R^2 = 0.646$ and exceeded every 1D self-supervised counterpart, including the fine-tuned MAE-1D ($R^2 = 0.641$). Under cross-dataset evaluation all models lost most of their accuracy and none beat the 1D baseline significantly. To identify which wavelengths drive each prediction, we applied Integrated Gradients and Grad-CAM and unfolded band importance back to the spectral axis. Protein ($r = 0.45$) and leaf water ($r = 0.33$) agreed with sensitivities simulated by the PROSAIL radiative-transfer model, while carotenoids ($r = 0.06$) and leaf area index ($r = -0.11$) did not, showing that the model reads established leaf chemistry for traits with sharp absorption features. The representational advantage of 2D spectral images, rather than architectural complexity or ImageNet pretraining, drives the gain over 1D approaches.

</details>


### [196] [Concept-based explanation of gene expression prediction from H&E images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16669)
*Amos Muench,Jonathan Thielmann,Reduan Achtibat,Maximilian Dreyer,Philip Bischoff,Caroline Forsythe,Hamidreza Parand,Thomas Walter,David Horst,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Teresa Gabriela Krieger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的病理基础模型框架，结合ViT感知的逐层相关性传播（LRP）与松弛原型TopK稀疏自编码器（concept discovery），用于从H&E图像预测空间转录组（ST），并提供局部热图与全局概念图谱两种维度的解释，在结直肠癌HEST-1k及TCGA COAD数据上验证了其预测性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ViT的病理模型的解释方法仅限于局部热图，无法揭示形态学概念如何贡献于空间转录组预测，缺乏连接转录程序与组织形态学的桥梁。

Method: 构建ViT基础的虚拟ST预测框架，结合两大技术：(1) ViT感知的逐层相关性传播用于局部解释；(2) 松弛原型TopK稀疏自编码器用于全局概念发现，从而提供局部与全局双层次解释。

Result: 准确预测临床相关ST签名及分子表型；空间分辨和聚合的iCMS分类加权F1分数分别为0.872和0.819（TCGA COAD为0.770），均能对患者预后进行分层；相关性来源的概念比激活来源的概念更能直接连接组织形态与下游预测。

Conclusion: 建立了基于概念的病理空间预测解释通用策略，框架可广泛适用于各类ViT病理模型，并提供了相关性概念图谱以连接分子表型与组织学表现。

Abstract: Recent advances in pathology foundation models have enabled accurate prediction of spatial transcriptomics (ST) from routine H&E images. However, existing explainability methods for vision transformer (ViT)-based models are largely limited to local heatmaps and do not reveal how morphological concepts contribute to ST predictions. Here, we introduce an explainable framework that combines relevance propagation and concept discovery to link transcriptional programs to tissue morphology. We developed a ViT-based framework for virtual ST from H&E images that combines ViT-aware layer-wise relevance propagation with relaxed archetypal TopK sparse autoencoder-based concept discovery. This approach provides both local explanations and global insights into the morphological patterns associated with transcriptional programs. We applied the framework to colorectal cancer ST data from the HEST-1k cohort and evaluated its generalizability in TCGA COAD. Our architecture accurately predicts clinically relevant ST signatures and accompanying molecular phenotypes. Measured and predicted gene expression profiles reveal substantial spatial heterogeneity of the colorectal cancer subtypes iCMS2 and iCMS3 across a large number of samples. Spatially resolved and aggregated iCMS classification achieve weighted F1 scores of 0.872 and 0.819 (0.770 in TCGA COAD), respectively, and both stratify patient outcome. Beyond prediction, our framework establishes a relevance-based concept atlas linking molecular phenotypes to histopathological representations. Comparison of activation- with relevance-derived concepts demonstrates that relevances provide a more direct link between tissue morphology and downstream predictions. We establish a general strategy for concept-based explanation of spatial prediction, and our framework is readily applicable to a broad range of ViT-based pathology models.

</details>


### [197] [How Sampling Strategy Affects Imbalance Mitigation in LiDAR Segmentation: A Study of Structured vs. Random Point-Based Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16673)
*Antonis Savva,Christos Kyrkou,Theocharis Theocharides*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种重加权方案和五种不平衡感知损失在三个LiDAR点云数据集（DALES、S3DIS、STPLS3D）和两种架构（KPConv、RandLA-Net）上的语义分割性能。研究发现逆频率加权会严重损害性能（最高下降12%），均匀加权在结构化采样下表现接近复杂损失，但随机采样下差距可达4.6%。损失景观分析揭示采样策略、不平衡程度和数据采集特性之间存在复杂交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云中的类别不平衡问题对自动驾驶和城市测绘中的语义分割构成挑战，虽然2D视觉领域已有众多缓解技术，但它们在3D点云中的有效性尚不明确，缺乏系统性基准研究。

Method: 对六种重加权方案和五种不平衡感知损失进行基准测试，使用两种代表性架构（KPConv结构化采样、RandLA-Net随机采样），在三个涵盖真实与合成数据的数据集上进行评估，并结合损失景观分析揭示不同因素间的交互作用。

Result: 逆频率加权相比均匀加权性能下降高达12%，少数类出现灾难性失败；均匀加权在KPConv上表现优异（差距<2%），但在RandLA-Net上差距达4.6%；损失景观表明结构化采样下不平衡比决定景观几何（真实数据），合成数据上则解耦，随机采样对数据集几何高度敏感。

Conclusion: 对于点云语义分割的不平衡缓解方法，其有效性取决于采样策略（结构化vs随机）、不平衡严重程度和数据采集特性的三者交互作用，不存在放之四海而皆准的通用方案。

Abstract: Class imbalance in LiDAR point clouds poses challenges for semantic segmentation in autonomous navigation and urban mapping. While 2D vision has numerous mitigation techniques, their effectiveness in 3D remains unclear. We benchmark six reweighting schemes and five imbalance-aware losses across three datasets (DALES, S3DIS, STPLS3D) using two architectures (KPConv, RandLA-Net). Inverse-frequency weighting degrades performance by up to 12% compared to uniform weighting, with catastrophic failures in minority classes. Uniform weighting performs within 2% of complex losses for structured sampling (KPConv) but benefits less for random sampling (RandLA-Net, up to 4.6% gap). Loss landscape analysis reveals a complex interplay: for structured sampling, imbalance ratio determines landscape geometry on real LiDAR data but decouples from it on synthetic data; for random sampling, landscapes show high sensitivity to dataset geometry regardless of imbalance ratio. For the two evaluated point-based architectures, these results suggest that the interaction between sampling strategy (structured vs. random), imbalance severity, and data acquisition characteristics shapes which mitigation approaches are effective.

</details>


### [198] [Bridging the Gap between Labeled and Unlabeled Data via Unified Flow with Feature Memory Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16681)
*Shanwen Wang,Xin Sun,Danfeng Hong,Junyu Dong,Patrick Le Callet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向遥感图像的半监督语义分割新方法UFFM，通过统一流动（UF）和特征记忆库（FMB）两大创新，解决标记与未标记数据独立训练导致的伪标签质量退化问题，在遥感数据集上取得了优于现有SOTA方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督语义分割方法对标记数据和未标记数据独立训练，导致标记数据占据主导地位，严重降低伪标签质量。这一问题在遥感图像领域尤为突出，亟需一种能有效弥合两类数据优化与特征表示差距的方法。

Method: UFFM包含两个关键创新：(1) 统一流动（UF）：一种新的训练流程，通过结合外部视觉基础模型（VFM）与遥感领域教师模型，生成偏差较小的伪标签，并在统一训练目标下联合优化标记数据和伪标记数据；(2) 特征记忆库（FMB）：一种新颖的半监督记忆模块，在训练过程中动态更新类特定特征，通过类特征对齐减少标记与未标记数据之间的特征差异。

Result: 在遥感数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明UFFM优于现有的SOTA半监督语义分割方法，验证了该方法在弥合标记与未标记数据之间优化和特征表示差距方面的有效性。

Conclusion: UFFM成功解决了半监督语义分割中标记数据主导和伪标签质量退化的问题，通过统一训练流程和特征对齐机制有效提升了遥感图像分割性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Although semi-supervised semantic segmentation ($\text{S}^4$) utilizes abundant unlabeled data to reduce manual labeling burdens, independent training of labeled and unlabeled data causes the former to dominate, which severely degrades pseudo-label quality. To address this challenges, we propose a novel remote sensing (RS) $\text{S}^4$ method via unified flow with feature memory bank (UFFM). Specifically, UFFM comprises two key innovations: unified flow (UF) and feature memory bank (FMB). The UF is a new training flow that generates less biased pseudo-labels by combining an external visual foundation model (VFM) with an RS domain teacher, and jointly optimizes labeled and pseudo-labeled data under a unified training objective. The FMB is a novel memory module for $\text{S}^4$ that dynamically updates class-specific features during training and reduces the feature discrepancy between labeled and unlabeled data through class-feature alignment. To verify the effectiveness of our model, we conduct extensive experiments on RS datasets. The experimental results show the superiority of our method over SOTA $\text{S}^4$ methods. Moreover, the results demonstrate the effectiveness of our contributions in bridging the optimization and feature representation gap between labeled and unlabeled data. Our code is released at \href{https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RS-UFFM}{https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RS-UFFM}.

</details>


### [199] [AnchorScore: A CLIP-Based Diagnostic of MLLM Annotation Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16690)
*Yan Ma,Lizhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorScore，一种基于冻结CLIP逐类零样本准确率的低成本先验诊断信号，用于预先预测多模态大语言模型(MLLM)在各类别上的标注难度。在课堂行为数据上，AnchorScore与MLLM逐类准确率显著相关(Spearman rho=0.769)，在Stanford40上复现效果接近(rho=0.817)，并可用于CLIP/MLLM混合路由、提示消歧和人工审核优先级预测等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在自动标注中广泛使用，但其逐类准确率差异巨大（如13个类别中12%-98%），且评估成本极高——评估一个27B MLLM在5,416张验证图像上需约14小时，而冻结CLIP仅需约3分钟。目前缺乏一种低成本信号来预先按预期MLLM标注难度对类别进行排序。

Method: 基于companion study引入的AnchorProxy构造（逐类零样本CLIP准确率），系统评估其全帧形式AnchorScore作为先验诊断指标。在SCB5数据集（13类、6个MLLM）上验证其与MLLM逐类准确率的相关性，并与DINOv2、ResNet-50、SigLIP及MLLM自我口述不确定性等替代预测器对比；在Stanford40 Actions上独立复现，同时检验跨模型一致性控制。

Result: AnchorScore与MLLM逐类准确率显著相关（rho=0.769, p=0.002, n=13），优于所有替代困难度预测器（均不显著）。跨模型一致性控制表明其捕获共享类别困难因素而非CLIP特有信号。Stanford40复现rho=0.817（p<0.001），在活动识别数据上关联最强，在医学和卫星图像上减弱。实际应用包括：预测类路由策略（较纯CLIP最高提升23个百分点，同时节省约44% MLLM成本）、硬类提示消歧和审核优先级预测。

Conclusion: AnchorScore不估计MLLM的绝对准确率，而是提供一种低成本的类别排名信号，将昂贵的MLLM评估引导到信息量最大的类别上，是一种实用且可扩展的先验诊断工具。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely used for automated annotation, yet their per-class accuracy varies widely (e.g., 12%-98% across the 13 classes of three classroom sub-datasets) and is expensive to measure: evaluating one 27B MLLM on 5,416 validation images takes roughly 14 hours, whereas a frozen-CLIP pass over the same images completes in about 3 minutes. A low-cost signal for ranking classes by expected MLLM annotation difficulty a priori remains underexplored. Building on the AnchorProxy construct (per-class zero-shot CLIP accuracy) introduced in the companion study, this paper systematically evaluates its full-frame formulation, termed AnchorScore here, as an a priori diagnostic that flags the classes MLLMs are least likely to annotate reliably.
  On classroom behavior data (SCB5, 13 classes, 6 MLLMs), AnchorScore correlates with per-class MLLM accuracy (Spearman rho = 0.769, p = 0.002, n = 13). None of the alternative difficulty predictors (DINOv2, ResNet-50, SigLIP, or MLLM self-verbalized uncertainty) showed a significant class-level correlation at n = 13. A cross-model consensus control suggests AnchorScore primarily captures a shared class-difficulty factor rather than a CLIP-specific signal. An independent replication on Stanford40 Actions yields a nearly identical effect (rho = 0.817, p < 0.001); the association is strongest on activity-recognition data and attenuates on medical and satellite imagery.
  Three practical applications follow: a deployable hybrid CLIP/MLLM routing strategy (predicted-class routing: up to +23 pp over CLIP-only at roughly 44% MLLM cost savings), prompt disambiguation on hard classes (exploratory), and review-priority prediction for human verification. AnchorScore does not estimate exact MLLM accuracy; it provides a low-cost ranking signal that directs expensive MLLM evaluation to the classes where it is most informative.

</details>


### [200] [MIRROR: Multimodal Intelligent Radiology Reasoning and Observation Reporter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16709)
*Vignesh Nagarajan,Sriram Venkatapathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRROR是一个研究原型系统，通过将多标签分类器、Grad-CAM定位器和报告生成器串联，确保生成的放射学报告中的发现可被审计（因为语言层看不到图像），从而将模型预测中的"识别错误"与"叙述错误"分离。在ChestMNIST上分类器达到macro AUROC 0.729，但在默认0.5阈值下对14个标签中的11个不做任何预测，揭示类别不平衡下聚合指标的误导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生面对模型输出时有两个问题：模型只返回数值而没有理由；任何将数值转换为可读文本的系统都可能悄悄添加模型从未做出的宣称。作者旨在构建一个能区分这两种失败的框架，并警告在类别不平衡条件下聚合指标会美化无效模型。

Method: MIRROR链式架构：①多标签分类器产生标签与概率；②Grad-CAM定位器将每个阳性发现映射到命名解剖区域；③报告生成器只接收标签、概率和区域，但不接收图像。由于语言层无法看到像素，它不能断言分类器未做出的发现。一个统一注册表管理胸部X光、脑MRI和头部CT的分类学、解剖学和措辞，添加模态仅是数据变更。作者还用忽略图像的预测器作为基线来评估聚合指标。

Result: 在ChestMNIST上分类器macro AUROC为0.729，在全部14个标签上优于随机排序，精度是随机排序器的1.6至6.8倍。但在默认0.5阈值下，对11个标签不产生任何阳性预测；Brier分数0.045看似优秀，却与忽略图像预测器的0.047几乎相同。区分能力是真实的，但决策能力不足。

Conclusion: 在放射学常见的类别不平衡条件下，聚合指标会美化"什么都不做"的模型，应在报告时与忽略图像的基线做对照。MIRROR通过架构约束使报告发现可审计，展示了将语言生成与分类决策解耦的价值。

Abstract: A radiologist reading a model's output faces two problems. The model returns a number and no reason, and any system that turns that number into readable prose can quietly add claims the model never made. MIRROR is a research prototype built to separate those failures. It chains a multi-label classifier, a Grad-CAM localizer that turns each positive finding into a named anatomical region, and a report writer that receives the labels, probabilities, and regions but never the image. Because the language layer cannot see pixels, it cannot assert a finding the classifier did not make. We are precise about what that buys: a MIRROR report's findings are auditable against the probability vector, while the sentences framing them are ordinary generated text, and we show one stating a cardiothoracic ratio the system never measured. One registry holds the taxonomy, anatomy, and phrasing for chest X-ray, brain MRI, and head CT, so adding a modality is a data change; all three are routed and tested, one is trained. On ChestMNIST that classifier reaches macro AUROC 0.729 and ranks better than chance on all 14 labels, at 1.6 to 6.8 times the precision a random ranker would get. Yet at the default 0.5 threshold it emits no positive prediction at all for 11 of them, and its excellent-looking Brier score of 0.045 sits beside the 0.047 earned by a predictor that ignores the image. The discrimination is real; the decisions are not. Under the class imbalance normal in radiology, aggregate metrics flatter models that do nothing, and should be reported against that floor.

</details>


### [201] [PersonaShot: Benchmarking Person-Centric Narrative Continuity in Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16717)
*Yuji Wang,Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Ran Yi,Yijia Hong,Han Feng,Weijian Cao,Chengjie Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV
